# Clear environment at the start of the session/script
rm(list = ls())
gc() # Explicitly call garbage collector

# ==== 1. Project Setup ====
# Purpose: Configure the R session environment.

# ===== 1.1 Load Core Packages =====
# Load packages essential for setup and definition stages.
# install.packages(c("dplyr", "readr", "ipumsr", "srvyr", "survey", "rlang", "tibble", "purrr", "here")) # Run once if needed

library(dplyr)
library(readr)
library(ipumsr)
library(srvyr)
library(survey)
library(rlang) # Needed for rlang::sym
library(tibble)
library(purrr) # For map functions if used later
library(here) # For robust relative file paths

print("Section 1.1: Core packages loaded.")

# ===== 1.2 Define Global Parameters & File Paths =====
# Define parameters and paths consistently used across the project scripts using here().
# Assumes script is run within an RStudio Project or a directory containing a .here file.

# ====== 1.2.1 IPUMS Sample Details ======
# Define the specific IPUMS sample IDs required for the analysis.
ACS_SAMPLE_ID      <- "us2023a"      # Primary source for income borders
CPS_ASEC_SAMPLE_ID <- "cps2023_03s"  # Using Mar 2023 for CY2022 income consistency
NHIS_SAMPLE_ID     <- "ih2019"       # Using 2019 for FAMTOTINC availability
CPS_VOTE_SAMPLE_ID <- "cps2022_11s"  # For Voting/Registration (Nov 2022)
CPS_FSS_SAMPLE_ID  <- "cps2022_12s"  # For Food Security (Dec 2022)
# Add others if needed (e.g., CPS Educ Supp)

print(paste("Target ACS Sample for Borders:", ACS_SAMPLE_ID))

# ====== 1.2.2 Project File Paths & Intermediate Files ======
# Define paths relative to the project root using here::here()

# --- Base Directories ---
# Assumes 'data' and 'output' folders exist at the project root level
data_dir <- here::here("data")
output_dir <- here::here("output")

# --- Subdirectories ---
extract_download_parent_dir <- file.path(data_dir, "ipums_extracts") # Parent folder for extract subdirs
summary_output_dir <- file.path(data_dir, "summary_outputs") # Folder for saved summary RDS files
plot_dir <- file.path(output_dir, "plots")
table_dir <- file.path(output_dir, "tables")

# Ensure directories exist
dir.create(extract_download_parent_dir, showWarnings = FALSE, recursive = TRUE)
dir.create(summary_output_dir, showWarnings = FALSE, recursive = TRUE)
dir.create(plot_dir, showWarnings = FALSE, recursive = TRUE)
dir.create(table_dir, showWarnings = FALSE, recursive = TRUE)

print(paste("Project Root (identified by here package):", here::here()))
print(paste("IPUMS extract parent directory:", extract_download_parent_dir))
print(paste("Summary statistics output directory:", summary_output_dir))

# --- Key Intermediate File Names (Saved to summary_output_dir) ---
income_borders_file  <- file.path(summary_output_dir, "within_tercile_quantile_borders.csv")
main_cutoffs_file    <- file.path(summary_output_dir, "main_tercile_cutoffs.rds")
print(paste("Income borders (within tercile) will be saved to:", income_borders_file))
print(paste("Main tercile cutoffs will be saved to:", main_cutoffs_file))

# --- Placeholder Summary File Paths (for reference, created later) ---
# Define summary file paths using summary_output_dir for consistency
acs_summary_file      <- file.path(summary_output_dir, paste0("acs_", ACS_SAMPLE_ID, "_summary_stats.rds")) # Example naming
cps_asec_summary_file <- file.path(summary_output_dir, paste0("cps_asec_", CPS_ASEC_SAMPLE_ID, "_summary_stats.rds"))
nhis_summary_file     <- file.path(summary_output_dir, paste0("nhis_", NHIS_SAMPLE_ID, "_summary_stats.rds"))
cps_vote_summary_file <- file.path(summary_output_dir, paste0("cps_vote_", CPS_VOTE_SAMPLE_ID, "_summary_stats.rds"))
cps_fss_summary_file  <- file.path(summary_output_dir, paste0("cps_fss_", CPS_FSS_SAMPLE_ID, "_summary_stats.rds"))
# Add placeholders for IIIF, G, H if needed
final_summary_file    <- file.path(summary_output_dir, "final_combined_summary_statistics.rds")


# ====== 1.2.3 Core Variable Names Checklist ======
# Define standard names for key variables used in this script.
HH_INCOME_VAR      <- "HHINCOME"
HH_WEIGHT_VAR      <- "HHWT"
# Need CLUSTER and STRATA for ACS border calculation design
CLUSTER_VAR        <- "CLUSTER" # (*Verify Name for us2023a*)
STRATA_VAR         <- "STRATA"  # (*Verify Name for us2023a*)


# ====== 1.2.4 Analysis Parameters ======
NUM_TERCILES               <- 3
# NUM_FINE_RANGES_PER_TERCILE <- 20 # Not needed directly in this script, defined in assign_income_groups

print("Section 1.2: Global parameters and file paths defined using here().")

# ==== End of Section 1 ====
print("Section 1: Project Setup completed.")


# ==== 2. Define Income Borders ====
# Purpose: Calculate income thresholds for main terciles and finer ranges
#          using minimal primary data (ACS), then save these borders for later use.

# ===== 2.1 Define Minimal Extract for Income Borders =====
# Variables needed ONLY to define income borders from the primary ACS sample
# Also need CLUSTER and STRATA for the survey design object
border_vars <- c(
  HH_INCOME_VAR,
  HH_WEIGHT_VAR,
  CLUSTER_VAR, # (*Verify Name for us2023a*)
  STRATA_VAR   # (*Verify Name for us2023a*)
)
border_vars <- unique(border_vars) # Ensure no duplicates

print(paste("Defining minimal extract for sample:", ACS_SAMPLE_ID))
minimal_extract_def <- define_extract_micro(
  description = "1/3 Country Project - Minimal data for income borders",
  collection = "usa", # Specify the collection for the ACS sample
  samples = ACS_SAMPLE_ID,
  variables = border_vars
)

# ===== 2.2 Verify API Key and Submit Extract =====

# ====== 2.2.1 Check for IPUMS API Key ======
if (Sys.getenv("IPUMS_API_KEY") == "") {
  stop(
    "IPUMS API key not found.\n",
    "Please set your IPUMS API key before proceeding using set_ipums_api_key(\"YOUR_KEY\", save = TRUE) or by setting the IPUMS_API_KEY environment variable.\n",
    "Script execution halted because the API key is missing."
  )
} else {
  print("Section 2.2.1: IPUMS API key found successfully via environment variable.")
}

# ====== 2.2.2 Submit Extract ======
print("Submitting minimal extract request...")
submitted_minimal_extract <- tryCatch({
  submit_extract(minimal_extract_def)
}, error = function(e) {
  stop("Error submitting IPUMS extract request: ", e$message)
})
extract_number_borders <- submitted_minimal_extract$number # Store extract number
print(paste("Minimal extract submitted. Extract Number:", extract_number_borders))


# ===== 2.3 Wait for and Download Extract =====
print(paste("Waiting for minimal extract", extract_number_borders, "to be ready..."))
ready_minimal_extract <- tryCatch({
  wait_for_extract(submitted_minimal_extract)
}, error = function(e) {
  stop("Error waiting for IPUMS extract: ", e$message)
})
print("Minimal extract is ready.")

# --- Create Dataset-Specific Download Directory --- # <<< ADDED SUBDIR LOGIC
download_subdir_name_borders <- paste0("ACS_Border_Extract_", extract_number_borders, "_", Sys.Date())
download_dir_specific_borders <- file.path(extract_download_parent_dir, download_subdir_name_borders)
if (!dir.exists(download_dir_specific_borders)) {
  can_create <- tryCatch({dir.create(download_dir_specific_borders, recursive = TRUE); TRUE},
                         warning = function(w){ print(paste("Warning creating dir:", w)); TRUE },
                         error = function(e){ print(paste("Error creating dir:", e)); FALSE })
  if(!can_create){ stop("FATAL ERROR: Could not create download subdirectory. Check permissions and base path: ", download_dir_specific_borders) }
  print(paste("... Created download subdirectory:", download_dir_specific_borders))
} else {
  print(paste("... Download subdirectory already exists:", download_dir_specific_borders))
}

# --- Download Extract Files ---
print(paste("Downloading minimal extract to:", download_dir_specific_borders))
minimal_files <- tryCatch({
  download_extract(ready_minimal_extract, download_dir = download_dir_specific_borders, overwrite = TRUE)
}, error = function(e) {
  stop("Error downloading IPUMS extract: ", e$message)
})
print(paste("Download function returned:", paste(basename(minimal_files), collapse=", ")))


# ===== 2.4 Load Downloaded IPUMS Data =====
print("Attempting to load IPUMS data using the DDI file...")

# --- Find DDI File Path --- # <<< UPDATED DDI FIND LOGIC
ddi_file_path_borders <- minimal_files[grep("\\.xml$", minimal_files)]
if (length(ddi_file_path_borders) == 0) {
  ddi_file_path_borders <- list.files(path = download_dir_specific_borders, pattern = paste0("usa", "_", sprintf("%05d", extract_number_borders), "\\.xml$"), full.names = TRUE)
  if (length(ddi_file_path_borders) == 0) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Could not find downloaded DDI (.xml) file in directory:", download_dir_specific_borders)) }
  else { print("... Found DDI file via directory search.") }
}
if (length(ddi_file_path_borders) > 1) { warning("Multiple DDI files found, using the first one."); ddi_file_path_borders <- ddi_file_path_borders[1] }
print(paste("... DDI file path identified:", ddi_file_path_borders))

# --- Load Data ---
ipums_data <- tryCatch({
  read_ipums_micro(ddi_file_path_borders, verbose = FALSE) # Use verbose=TRUE for debugging load if needed
}, error = function(e) {
  stop("Error reading IPUMS data with read_ipums_micro: ", e$message)
})
print("Successfully loaded IPUMS data into 'ipums_data'.")
print(paste("Dimensions of loaded data:", paste(dim(ipums_data), collapse = " x ")))


# ===== 2.4.5 Clean HHINCOME Variable =====
print("===== 2.4.5 Cleaning HHINCOME Variable =====")
if (!HH_INCOME_VAR %in% names(ipums_data)) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Required income variable", HH_INCOME_VAR, "not found.")) }

# Define missing/NIU codes to convert to NA - VERIFY FOR us2023a
income_codes_to_na_borders <- c(9999999, 9999998) # Example: Common IPUMS missing codes
# Treat 0 or negative as NA for border calculation (usually appropriate)
ipums_data <- ipums_data %>%
  mutate(
    !!HH_INCOME_VAR := as.numeric(!!sym(HH_INCOME_VAR)),
    !!HH_INCOME_VAR := if_else(!!sym(HH_INCOME_VAR) %in% income_codes_to_na_borders | !!sym(HH_INCOME_VAR) <= 0, NA_real_, !!sym(HH_INCOME_VAR))
  )
n_income_na_after_borders <- sum(is.na(ipums_data[[HH_INCOME_VAR]]))
print(paste("... HHINCOME NAs after cleaning (including <=0):", n_income_na_after_borders))
print("===== HHINCOME Cleaning Completed =====")


# ===== 2.5 Calculate Income Borders =====

# ====== 2.5.0 Create Survey Design Object ======
required_design_vars <- c(CLUSTER_VAR, STRATA_VAR, HH_WEIGHT_VAR, HH_INCOME_VAR)
missing_design_vars <- setdiff(required_design_vars, names(ipums_data))
if (length(missing_design_vars) > 0) { stop("FATAL ERROR: Missing required variables for survey design: ", paste(missing_design_vars, collapse=", ")) }

print("Creating survey design object using variables from loaded data...")
ipums_data <- ipums_data %>%
  mutate(across(all_of(c(CLUSTER_VAR, STRATA_VAR, HH_WEIGHT_VAR)), as.numeric)) # Ensure design vars are numeric

# Filter out cases with missing weights or income needed for quantile calculation
ipums_data_design <- ipums_data %>%
  filter(!is.na(!!sym(HH_WEIGHT_VAR)) & !!sym(HH_WEIGHT_VAR) > 0 & !is.na(!!sym(HH_INCOME_VAR)))

if(nrow(ipums_data_design) == 0) { stop("FATAL ERROR: No valid observations remaining for survey design after filtering NA/non-positive weights or NA income.")}

survey_design <- tryCatch({
  svydesign(
    ids = as.formula(paste0("~", CLUSTER_VAR)),
    strata = as.formula(paste0("~", STRATA_VAR)),
    weights = as.formula(paste0("~", HH_WEIGHT_VAR)),
    data = ipums_data_design, # Use filtered data
    nest = TRUE
  )
}, error = function(e) { stop("Error creating survey design object: ", e$message) })
print("Survey design object created.")


# ====== 2.5.1 Calculate Main Tercile Borders ======
print("Calculating main tercile borders using survey::svyquantile...")
hh_income_sym <- rlang::sym(HH_INCOME_VAR)
main_tercile_probs <- c(1/3, 2/3)

quantile_result_svy <- tryCatch({
  survey::svyquantile(
    x = stats::as.formula(paste0("~", rlang::as_string(hh_income_sym))),
    design = survey_design,
    quantiles = main_tercile_probs,
    na.rm = TRUE, # Should be handled by filter above, but keep just in case
    ci = FALSE
  )
}, error = function(e) { stop("Error calculating main tercile quantiles with svyquantile: ", e$message) })

# Extract the quantile values (more robust extraction)
quantile_values <- quantile_result_svy[[1]][1,] # Access the matrix inside the list

if (!is.null(quantile_values) && is.numeric(quantile_values) && length(quantile_values) == length(main_tercile_probs)) {
  main_tercile_cutoffs_srvyr <- quantile_values %>% t() %>% as_tibble(.name_repair = "minimal")
  # Assign meaningful names based on probabilities
  names(main_tercile_cutoffs_srvyr) <- paste0("p", round(main_tercile_probs, 3))
  print("Main tercile cutoffs calculation complete.")
  print(main_tercile_cutoffs_srvyr)
  
  # --- SAVE MAIN CUTOFFS ---
  print("...Extracting and saving main cutoff values...")
  cutoff1_save <- main_tercile_cutoffs_srvyr[[1]] # First cutoff
  cutoff2_save <- main_tercile_cutoffs_srvyr[[2]] # Second cutoff
  main_cutoffs_to_save <- list(main_cutoff1 = cutoff1_save, main_cutoff2 = cutoff2_save)
  saveRDS(main_cutoffs_to_save, file = main_cutoffs_file) # Use here-based path
  print(paste("... Main cutoffs saved to:", main_cutoffs_file))
  
} else {
  stop("Main tercile quantile values could not be correctly extracted. Examine 'quantile_result_svy'.")
}


# ====== 2.5.2 Define Function to Calculate Within-Tercile Borders ======
# (Function definition remains the same as provided before)
calculate_within_borders <- function(tercile_design, income_sym, tercile_label) {
  results <- list()
  n_obs <- 0
  tercile_data <- NULL
  if (!is.null(tercile_design) && inherits(tercile_design, c("survey.design", "tbl_svy"))) {
    tercile_data <- try(tercile_design$variables, silent = TRUE)
    if(inherits(tercile_data, "try-error") || is.null(tercile_data)) {
      tercile_data <- try(model.frame(tercile_design), silent = TRUE)
    }
    if (!inherits(tercile_data, "try-error") && !is.null(tercile_data)) {
      n_obs <- nrow(tercile_data)
    } else { warning(paste("Could not extract data frame to get N for", tercile_label)) }
  } else { warning(paste("Input for", tercile_label, "is not a survey design object.")) }
  
  quantile_defs <- list(
    "Groups_3" = list(n = 3, probs = c(1/3, 2/3), label = "Tercile"),
    "Groups_4" = list(n = 4, probs = c(1/4, 2/4, 3/4), label = "Quartile"),
    "Groups_5" = list(n = 5, probs = c(1/5, 2/5, 3/5, 4/5), label = "Quintile"),
    "Groups_10" = list(n = 10, probs = seq(0.1, 0.9, by = 0.1), label = "Decile"),
    "Groups_20" = list(n = 20, probs = seq(0.05, 0.95, by = 0.05), label = "Ventile"), # 5th percentiles
    "Groups_50" = list(n = 50, probs = seq(0.02, 0.98, by = 0.02), label = "Percentile_2") # 2nd percentiles
  )
  print(paste("--- Calculating borders for main tercile:", tercile_label, "(N =", n_obs, ") ---"))
  if (is.null(tercile_design) || !inherits(tercile_design, c("survey.design", "tbl_svy")) || n_obs == 0) {
    warning(paste("Invalid/empty design for", tercile_label, ". Skipping within-border calc."))
    for (q_name in names(quantile_defs)) { results[[q_name]] <- NA }
    return(results)
  }
  for (q_name in names(quantile_defs)) {
    q_info <- quantile_defs[[q_name]]
    label <- q_info$label
    if (n_obs < q_info$n * 2) {
      warning(paste("Skipping", label, "for", tercile_label, "due to small N (", n_obs,")"))
      results[[q_name]] <- NA
      next
    }
    print(paste("... calculating", label, "borders"))
    q_values_raw <- tryCatch({
      survey::svyquantile(
        x = stats::as.formula(paste0("~", rlang::as_string(income_sym))),
        design = tercile_design,
        quantiles = q_info$probs,
        na.rm = TRUE,
        ci = FALSE
      )
    }, error = function(e) {
      warning(paste("Error calculating", label, "for", tercile_label, ":", e$message))
      NULL
    })
    extracted_values <- NULL
    if (!is.null(q_values_raw)) {
      var_name_string <- rlang::as_string(income_sym)
      # Handle potential nested list structure from svyquantile
      if (is.list(q_values_raw) && length(q_values_raw) > 0) {
        result_matrix <- q_values_raw[[1]] # Assume first element holds the results
        if (is.matrix(result_matrix) && nrow(result_matrix) >= 1) {
          extracted_values <- result_matrix[1, ]
        } else if (is.numeric(result_matrix)) { # If it returns a simple vector
          extracted_values <- result_matrix
        }
      }
    }
    if (!is.null(extracted_values) && is.numeric(extracted_values) && length(extracted_values) == length(q_info$probs)) {
      results[[q_name]] <- extracted_values
    } else {
      warning(paste("Could not extract valid", label, "values for", tercile_label))
      results[[q_name]] <- NA
    }
  }
  return(results)
}


# ====== 2.5.3 Assign Main Tercile Groups and Create Subsets ======
cutoff1 <- main_cutoffs_to_save$main_cutoff1 # Use saved values
cutoff2 <- main_cutoffs_to_save$main_cutoff2 # Use saved values

print(paste("Using Cutoff 1 (End of T1):", cutoff1))
print(paste("Using Cutoff 2 (End of T2):", cutoff2))

# Add the main tercile group variable to the original design object
# Use the design object that still has all the data (survey_design)
survey_design_with_terciles <- survey_design %>%
  as_survey() %>% # Ensure it's a srvyr object if not already
  mutate(
    income_tercile = case_when(
      !!hh_income_sym <= cutoff1 ~ "Tercile 1 (Bottom)",
      !!hh_income_sym > cutoff1 & !!hh_income_sym <= cutoff2 ~ "Tercile 2 (Middle)",
      !!hh_income_sym > cutoff2 ~ "Tercile 3 (Top)",
      TRUE ~ NA_character_ # Handles NA income values
    ),
    income_tercile = factor(income_tercile, levels = c("Tercile 1 (Bottom)", "Tercile 2 (Middle)", "Tercile 3 (Top)"))
  )

print("Added 'income_tercile' variable to survey design.")

# Create subsets using the design object with terciles
print("Creating subset survey designs for each tercile...")
tercile1_design <- survey_design_with_terciles %>% filter(income_tercile == "Tercile 1 (Bottom)")
tercile2_design <- survey_design_with_terciles %>% filter(income_tercile == "Tercile 2 (Middle)")
tercile3_design <- survey_design_with_terciles %>% filter(income_tercile == "Tercile 3 (Top)")
print("Subset designs created.")


# ====== 2.5.4 Calculate Within-Tercile Borders for Each Group ======
print("Running calculation for Tercile 1...")
results_t1 <- calculate_within_borders(tercile_design = tercile1_design, income_sym = hh_income_sym, tercile_label = "Tercile 1 (Bottom)")
print("Running calculation for Tercile 2...")
results_t2 <- calculate_within_borders(tercile_design = tercile2_design, income_sym = hh_income_sym, tercile_label = "Tercile 2 (Middle)")
print("Running calculation for Tercile 3...")
results_t3 <- calculate_within_borders(tercile_design = tercile3_design, income_sym = hh_income_sym, tercile_label = "Tercile 3 (Top)")
print("Within-tercile calculations complete.")


# ====== 2.5.5 Combine and Export Within-Tercile Borders ======
# library(purrr) # Already loaded

print("Combining results from all terciles...")
all_results <- list(
  "Tercile 1 (Bottom)" = results_t1,
  "Tercile 2 (Middle)" = results_t2,
  "Tercile 3 (Top)"    = results_t3
)

all_borders_df <- purrr::map_dfr(all_results, .id = "MainTercile", function(tercile_result_list) {
  purrr::map_dfr(tercile_result_list, .id = "QuantileGroup", function(quantile_vector) {
    if (is.null(quantile_vector) || all(is.na(quantile_vector)) || !is.numeric(quantile_vector)) {
      # Return tibble with correct types even if NA
      return(tibble(QuantileLabel = NA_character_, CutoffValue = NA_real_))
    }
    # Ensure names are character for enframe
    names(quantile_vector) <- as.character(names(quantile_vector))
    tibble::enframe(quantile_vector, name = "QuantileLabel", value = "CutoffValue")
  })
})

# Clean up QuantileLabel and reorder
# Handle potential parsing issues if QuantileLabel isn't directly numeric probability string
all_borders_df <- all_borders_df %>%
  filter(!is.na(QuantileLabel)) %>% # Remove rows where calculation might have failed
  mutate(QuantileProbability = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(QuantileLabel))) %>% # Attempt conversion, ignore warnings
  select(MainTercile, QuantileGroup, QuantileProbability, CutoffValue)

print("Combined borders data frame created:")
print(head(all_borders_df))

# Export the combined data frame to CSV (using path defined in 1.2.2)
print(paste("Exporting within-tercile borders to:", income_borders_file))
# Ensure the directory exists (created in 1.2.2)
readr::write_csv(all_borders_df, income_borders_file)

print("Export of within-tercile borders complete.")

# ====== 2.6 Clean Up Memory from File 1 ======
print("Cleaning up large objects from File 1...")
objects_to_remove_f1 <- c("ipums_data", "ipums_data_design", "survey_design", "survey_design_with_terciles", # Added ipums_data_design
                          "tercile1_design", "tercile2_design", "tercile3_design",
                          "quantile_result_svy", "results_t1", "results_t2", "results_t3",
                          "all_results", "main_tercile_cutoffs_srvyr", "main_cutoffs_to_save",
                          "all_borders_df", "minimal_extract_def", "submitted_minimal_extract", # Added more objects
                          "ready_minimal_extract", "minimal_files")
objects_exist_f1 <- objects_to_remove_f1[objects_to_remove_f1 %in% ls()]
if(length(objects_exist_f1) > 0) {
  rm(list = objects_exist_f1)
  print(paste("Removed objects:", paste(objects_exist_f1, collapse=", ")))
} else {
  print("No large objects specified for removal found.")
}
gc() # Explicit garbage collection
print("Memory cleanup attempted.")

print("==== File I processing finished. ====")

# ==== 1. START OF FUNCTION DEFINITIONS ====

#' Assign Income Groups Based on Pre-calculated Borders and Main Cutoffs (Corrected v3)
#'
#' Adds two new factor columns to the survey object's underlying data:
#' 1. `income_tercile`: Categorical variable for main income terciles.
#' 2. `fine_income_group`: Categorical variable for detailed income groups
#'    (e.g., Ventiles) calculated *within* each main tercile using findInterval.
#'
#' @param survey_obj A survey design object (e.g., from `svydesign` or `as_survey`).
#'                   The underlying data must contain the income variable.
#' @param borders_df A data frame containing the pre-calculated **within-tercile** income border
#'                   definitions. Expected columns include: `MainTercile`,
#'                   `QuantileGroup`, `QuantileProbability`, `CutoffValue`.
#'                   This is typically loaded from the CSV generated by File I.
#' @param income_var_name Character string. The name of the household income
#'                        variable in `survey_obj`'s data (e.g., "HHINCOME").
#' @param detail_level Character string. The specific `QuantileGroup` label from
#'                     `borders_df` to use for the fine income groups
#'                     (e.g., "Groups_10" for Deciles, "Groups_20" for Ventiles).
#' @param main_cutoff1 Numeric. The income value defining the border between the
#'                     bottom (T1) and middle (T2) main terciles (e.g., 33rd percentile).
#'                     This value should be calculated across the *entire* population in File I.
#' @param main_cutoff2 Numeric. The income value defining the border between the
#'                     middle (T2) and top (T3) main terciles (e.g., 67th percentile).
#'                     This value should be calculated across the *entire* population in File I.
#'
#' @return The input `survey_obj` with two additional factor columns (`income_tercile`,
#'         `fine_income_group`) added to its underlying data frame (`survey_obj$variables`).
#' @export
#'
assign_income_groups <- function(survey_obj, borders_df, income_var_name, detail_level, main_cutoff1, main_cutoff2) {
  
  # --- Load Required Libraries ---
  if (!requireNamespace("dplyr", quietly = TRUE)) stop("Package 'dplyr' needed but not installed.")
  if (!requireNamespace("tidyr", quietly = TRUE)) stop("Package 'tidyr' needed but not installed.")
  if (!requireNamespace("rlang", quietly = TRUE)) stop("Package 'rlang' needed but not installed.")
  if (!requireNamespace("tibble", quietly = TRUE)) stop("Package 'tibble' needed but not installed.")
  if (!requireNamespace("stringr", quietly = TRUE)) stop("Package 'stringr' needed but not installed.") # Added
  
  # --- Input Validation ---
  print("--- Running Input Validation ---")
  # (Keep existing validation logic - omitted here for brevity)
  if (!inherits(survey_obj, c("survey.design", "svyrep.design", "tbl_svy"))) {
    stop("Error in assign_income_groups: 'survey_obj' must be a survey design object.")
  }
  if (!is.data.frame(borders_df)) {
    stop("Error in assign_income_groups: 'borders_df' must be a data frame.")
  }
  required_border_cols <- c("MainTercile", "QuantileGroup", "QuantileProbability", "CutoffValue")
  if (!all(required_border_cols %in% names(borders_df))) {
    stop("Error in assign_income_groups: 'borders_df' is missing required columns: ", paste(setdiff(required_border_cols, names(borders_df)), collapse=", "))
  }
  if (!is.character(income_var_name) || length(income_var_name) != 1) {
    stop("Error in assign_income_groups: 'income_var_name' must be a single character string.")
  }
  # Check if income_var_name exists in the survey object's data
  if (!income_var_name %in% names(survey_obj$variables)) {
    stop("Error in assign_income_groups: Income variable '", income_var_name, "' not found in survey object's data.")
  }
  if (!is.character(detail_level) || length(detail_level) != 1) {
    stop("Error in assign_income_groups: 'detail_level' must be a single character string.")
  }
  if (!detail_level %in% unique(borders_df$QuantileGroup)) {
    stop("Error in assign_income_groups: 'detail_level' value '", detail_level, "' not found in the 'QuantileGroup' column of borders_df.")
  }
  if (!is.numeric(main_cutoff1) || length(main_cutoff1) != 1 || is.na(main_cutoff1)) {
    stop("Error in assign_income_groups: 'main_cutoff1' must be a single, non-NA numeric value.")
  }
  if (!is.numeric(main_cutoff2) || length(main_cutoff2) != 1 || is.na(main_cutoff2)) {
    stop("Error in assign_income_groups: 'main_cutoff2' must be a single, non-NA numeric value.")
  }
  if (main_cutoff1 >= main_cutoff2) {
    stop("Error in assign_income_groups: 'main_cutoff1' must be less than 'main_cutoff2'.")
  }
  print("--- Input Validation Passed ---")
  
  # --- Extract Data and Prepare ---
  print("--- Extracting data from survey object ---")
  data_to_process <- survey_obj$variables # Access underlying data frame
  income_sym <- rlang::sym(income_var_name) # Convert string name to symbol for dplyr
  
  # --- 1. Assign Main Income Tercile ---
  print(paste("--- Assigning Main Income Tercile using cutoffs:", main_cutoff1, "and", main_cutoff2, "---"))
  data_to_process <- data_to_process %>%
    dplyr::mutate(
      income_tercile_num = dplyr::case_when(
        is.na(!!income_sym) ~ NA_integer_, # Handle NA income first
        !!income_sym <= main_cutoff1 ~ 1L,
        !!income_sym > main_cutoff1 & !!income_sym <= main_cutoff2 ~ 2L,
        !!income_sym > main_cutoff2 ~ 3L,
        TRUE ~ NA_integer_
      ),
      income_tercile = factor(
        income_tercile_num,
        levels = 1:3,
        labels = c("Tercile 1 (Bottom)", "Tercile 2 (Middle)", "Tercile 3 (Top)")
      )
    )
  n_t1 <- sum(data_to_process$income_tercile_num == 1, na.rm = TRUE)
  n_t2 <- sum(data_to_process$income_tercile_num == 2, na.rm = TRUE)
  n_t3 <- sum(data_to_process$income_tercile_num == 3, na.rm = TRUE)
  n_na <- sum(is.na(data_to_process$income_tercile_num))
  print(paste("... Assigned main terciles: T1=", format(n_t1, big.mark=","),
              " T2=", format(n_t2, big.mark=","),
              " T3=", format(n_t3, big.mark=","),
              " NA=", format(n_na, big.mark=",")))
  
  
  # --- 2. Prepare Within-Tercile Borders for Assignment ---
  print(paste("--- Preparing within-tercile cutoffs for detail level:", detail_level, "---"))
  # Extract only the necessary cutoffs for the selected detail_level
  borders_filtered <- borders_df %>%
    dplyr::filter(QuantileGroup == detail_level) %>%
    # Add tercile number for easier processing
    dplyr::mutate(income_tercile_num = dplyr::case_when(
      stringr::str_detect(MainTercile, "1|Bottom") ~ 1L,
      stringr::str_detect(MainTercile, "2|Middle") ~ 2L,
      stringr::str_detect(MainTercile, "3|Top") ~ 3L,
      TRUE ~ NA_integer_
    )) %>%
    dplyr::filter(!is.na(income_tercile_num)) %>%
    dplyr::select(income_tercile_num, CutoffValue) %>%
    dplyr::arrange(income_tercile_num, CutoffValue) # Ensure cutoffs are sorted within tercile
  
  # Check if we have the expected number of cutoffs per tercile
  n_cutoffs_per_tercile <- borders_filtered %>% count(income_tercile_num)
  expected_n_cutoffs <- as.integer(stringr::str_extract(detail_level, "\\d+")) - 1
  if (!all(n_cutoffs_per_tercile$n == expected_n_cutoffs)) {
    warning("Unexpected number of cutoffs found per tercile for detail level '", detail_level, "'. Check borders file.")
    print(n_cutoffs_per_tercile)
  } else {
    print(paste("... Found expected", expected_n_cutoffs, "cutoffs per tercile for", detail_level))
  }
  
  # --- 3. Assign Fine Income Group using findInterval (Revised v3) --- # <<< REVISED LOGIC AGAIN
  print("--- Assigning Fine Income Group using findInterval (Revised v3) ---")
  
  # Apply the findInterval logic group by tercile
  data_to_process <- data_to_process %>%
    dplyr::group_by(income_tercile_num) %>%
    dplyr::mutate(
      # Calculate fine_group_num only for rows where income_tercile_num is not NA
      fine_group_num = if_else(
        !is.na(income_tercile_num),
        {
          # Get the current tercile number for this group
          # Use the first value of income_tercile_num within the group
          current_tercile <- income_tercile_num[1]
          
          # Get the specific cutoffs for this tercile from the pre-filtered borders
          tercile_cutoffs <- borders_filtered %>%
            dplyr::filter(income_tercile_num == current_tercile) %>%
            dplyr::pull(CutoffValue)
          
          if (length(tercile_cutoffs) == 0) {
            warning("No cutoffs found for tercile ", current_tercile, " and detail level ", detail_level)
            NA_integer_ # Return NA if no cutoffs
          } else {
            # Use findInterval with the correct cutoffs for this group
            # Use !!income_sym to correctly refer to the income column passed as argument
            interval_index <- findInterval(!!income_sym, tercile_cutoffs, rightmost.closed = FALSE, all.inside = FALSE)
            fine_group_number <- interval_index + 1L
            fine_group_number # Return the calculated group number
          }
        },
        NA_integer_ # Assign NA if tercile itself is NA
      )
    ) %>%
    dplyr::ungroup() %>% # Ungroup after calculation
    # Create the T#-G## label
    dplyr::mutate(
      fine_income_group = if_else(
        !is.na(fine_group_num) & !is.na(income_tercile_num), # Check both are non-NA
        paste0("T", income_tercile_num, "-G", stringr::str_pad(fine_group_num, 2, pad = "0")),
        NA_character_ # Assign NA if assignment failed or tercile was NA
      )
    )
  
  print("... Fine income groups assigned using findInterval (Revised v3).")
  print("... Example distribution of fine groups (check counts):")
  print(head(sort(table(data_to_process$fine_income_group)), 25)) # Print more levels
  print("Total number of distinct fine_income_groups created:")
  print(n_distinct(data_to_process$fine_income_group, na.rm = TRUE))
  
  
  # --- 4. Update Survey Object and Return ---
  print("--- Updating survey object with new group variables ---")
  # Select necessary columns, remove intermediate ones
  final_data <- data_to_process %>%
    dplyr::select(-fine_group_num) # Remove the intermediate numeric group
  
  survey_obj$variables <- final_data # Replace data frame in survey object
  
  print("--- assign_income_groups function completed ---")
  return(survey_obj)
} # End of corrected function definition


# ==== Function to Generate Text Codebook from IPUMS DDI ====
# (generate_codebook_from_ddi function remains the same as before)
#' Generate a Human-Readable Text Codebook from an IPUMS DDI File
#'
#' Reads an IPUMS DDI file and extracts metadata (label, description, codes, value labels)
#' for a specified list of variables, writing the output to a text file.
#'
#' @param ddi_path Character string. The full path to the .xml DDI file.
#' @param vars_list Character vector. A list of variable names to include in the codebook.
#' @param output_txt_path Character string. The full path where the output .txt codebook file should be saved.
#'
#' @return Invisibly returns TRUE if successful, FALSE otherwise. Prints status messages to console.
#' @export
#'
generate_codebook_from_ddi <- function(ddi_path, vars_list, output_txt_path) {
  # Check for necessary packages
  if (!requireNamespace("ipumsr", quietly = TRUE)) { stop("Package 'ipumsr' needed for this function to work.") }
  if (!requireNamespace("dplyr", quietly = TRUE)) { stop("Package 'dplyr' needed for this function to work.") }
  if (!requireNamespace("purrr", quietly = TRUE)) { stop("Package 'purrr' needed for this function to work.") }
  if (!requireNamespace("tibble", quietly = TRUE)) { stop("Package 'tibble' needed for this function to work.") } # Needed for enframe if used
  
  # Check if DDI file exists
  if (!file.exists(ddi_path)) {
    warning(paste("DDI file not found at:", ddi_path))
    return(invisible(FALSE))
  }
  
  tryCatch({
    print(paste("--- Generating Codebook from:", basename(ddi_path), "---"))
    # Read the DDI file
    ddi_obj <- ipumsr::read_ipums_ddi(ddi_path)
    # Get info for all variables in the DDI
    var_info_all <- ipumsr::ipums_var_info(ddi_obj)
    
    # Filter for requested variables that are actually present in the DDI
    vars_in_ddi <- intersect(vars_list, var_info_all$var_name)
    if(length(vars_in_ddi) < length(vars_list)){
      warning("Some requested variables not found in DDI: ", paste(setdiff(vars_list, vars_in_ddi), collapse=", "))
    }
    if(length(vars_in_ddi) == 0){
      warning("None of the requested variables found in the DDI. Skipping codebook generation.")
      return(invisible(FALSE))
    }
    
    # --- Write to Text File ---
    # Open file connection using sink()
    sink(output_txt_path)
    
    # Write Header
    cat(paste("IPUMS Data Extract Codebook\n"))
    cat(paste("Source DDI:", basename(ddi_path), "\n"))
    cat(paste("Generated on:", Sys.time(), "\n"))
    cat(paste("Variables Included:", length(vars_in_ddi), "\n"))
    cat("========================================\n\n")
    
    # Loop through variables found in the DDI
    for (var in vars_in_ddi) {
      var_info_single <- var_info_all %>% dplyr::filter(var_name == var)
      
      # Write Variable Header
      cat(paste0("## Variable: ", var, " ##\n"))
      cat(paste("Label:", var_info_single$var_label %||% "N/A", "\n"))
      # Limit description length slightly
      desc_short <- substr(var_info_single$var_desc %||% "N/A", 1, 500)
      if (nchar(var_info_single$var_desc %||% "") > 500) desc_short <- paste0(desc_short, "...")
      cat(paste("Description:", desc_short, "\n"))
      
      # Get and Write Value Labels (Codes)
      val_labels_df <- ipumsr::ipums_val_labels(ddi_obj, var = !!var)
      
      if (nrow(val_labels_df) > 0) {
        cat("Codes and Labels:\n")
        # Format labels nicely for text output
        # Ensure val column is character for consistent spacing if needed
        val_labels_df$val_chr <- as.character(val_labels_df$val)
        max_val_width <- max(nchar(val_labels_df$val_chr), na.rm = TRUE)
        formatted_labels <- paste0("  ", format(val_labels_df$val_chr, width = max_val_width), ": ", val_labels_df$lbl)
        cat(paste(formatted_labels, collapse = "\n"))
        cat("\n")
      } else {
        cat("Codes and Labels: (No value labels found - likely continuous or string variable)\n")
      }
      cat("----------------------------------------\n\n")
    }
    
    # Close file connection
    sink()
    print(paste("... Codebook successfully written to:", output_txt_path))
    return(invisible(TRUE))
    
  }, error = function(e) {
    warning(paste("Error generating codebook for", basename(ddi_path), ":", e$message))
    # Ensure sink is closed if error occurred during writing
    if(sink.number() > 0) { sink() }
    return(invisible(FALSE))
  })
}


# Helper for default value if NULL (used with %||%)
`%||%` <- function(a, b) {
  if (is.null(a) || length(a) == 0 || all(is.na(a))) b else a
}

# Purpose:
# This R script performs the complete processing for American Community Survey
# (ACS) data (sample us2023a). It cleans data, assigns income groups based on
# external borders, calculates derived variables (including a mutually exclusive
# Hispanic category and CORRECTED non-citizen status), calculates summary statistics
# (including proportions for the new race/ethnicity categories), and saves the results.
# USER MUST VERIFY HARDCODED VALUES/CODES IN CLEANING/DERIVED VARS AGAINST THEIR DDI.

# NOTE: This is File IIIA of V+ (Dataset Processing - ACS) - FINAL

# Workflow Steps Covered (Sections in this script):

# IIIA.1: Get Data (IPUMS Download - Optional)
# IIIA.2: Initial Clean & Prep (Load Data, Clean Vars - REVISED CITIZEN CLEANING)
# IIIA.3: Create Survey Design (Skipped - Using Weighted Means)
# IIIA.4: Load Income Borders (External Borders from ipums_extracts/)
# IIIA.5: Load Main Tercile Cutoffs (External Borders from ipums_extracts/)
# IIIA.6: Assign Income Groups (Using ACS HHINCOME & External Borders)
# IIIA.7: Calculate Derived Variables (Implementing Data Plan - UPDATED RACE/ETHNICITY & CORRECTED NON-CITIZEN)
# IIIA.8: Calculate Grouped Summary Statistics (Implementing Data Plan - REVISED w/ HWSEI)
# IIIA.8.5: Calculate Race/Ethnicity-Specific Summary Statistics (UPDATED CATEGORIES)
# IIIA.9: Save Summary Table (Using Original Filename)
# IIIA.10: Clean Up Memory

# ==== IIIA.0 SETUP & PARAMETERS ====

# ===== IIIA.0.1 Load Libraries =====
library(ipumsr)
library(dplyr)
library(readr)
library(stringr)
library(rlang)
library(tibble)
library(purrr)
library(survey)
library(here)
library(tidyr)

print("... Libraries loaded.")

# ===== IIIA.0.2 Source Shared Functions =====
shared_functions_file <- here::here("II_Shared_Functions.R")
if (file.exists(shared_functions_file)) {
  source(shared_functions_file)
  print("... Shared functions sourced successfully.")
} else {
  stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Shared functions file not found:", shared_functions_file))
}

# ===== IIIA.0.3 Define Parameters =====
# --- Optional: Provide path to existing DDI to skip download ---
ddi_file_path_input <- NULL
# Example: ddi_file_path_input <- here::here("data", "ipums_extracts", "ACS_Extract_XX_Date", "usa_XXXXX.xml") # <<< UPDATE FILENAME

# --- IPUMS Extract Definition ---
ipums_collection <- "usa"
acs_sample_id <- "us2023a" # Make sure this is your intended ACS sample for borders & analysis
print(paste("... Target ACS sample:", acs_sample_id))

# --- File Paths using here() ---
extract_download_parent_dir <- here::here("data", "ipums_extracts")
summary_output_dir <- here::here("data", "summary_outputs")
print(paste("... IPUMS extract parent directory:", extract_download_parent_dir))
print(paste("... Summary statistics output directory:", summary_output_dir))
dir.create(extract_download_parent_dir, showWarnings = FALSE, recursive = TRUE)
dir.create(summary_output_dir, showWarnings = FALSE, recursive = TRUE)

# --- Input Border Filenames ---
borders_csv_file <- file.path(summary_output_dir, "within_tercile_quantile_borders.csv")
main_cutoffs_rds_file <- file.path(summary_output_dir, "main_tercile_cutoffs.rds")
print(paste("... Path to EXTERNAL within-tercile borders:", borders_csv_file))
print(paste("... Path to EXTERNAL main cutoffs:", main_cutoffs_rds_file))

# --- Variables Needed ---
acs_variables_needed <- c(
  "YEAR", "SAMPLE", "SERIAL", "HHWT", "PERWT", "CLUSTER", "STRATA", "HHINCOME",
  "OWNCOST", "RENTGRS", "AGE", "RACE", "HISPAN", "CITIZEN", "SEX", "EDUCD",
  "EMPSTAT", "POVERTY", "HWSEI", "OCC", "OWNERSHP", "BEDROOMS", "NUMPREC",
  "KITCHEN", "HOTWATER", "VEHICLES", "UNITSSTR", "MOVEDIN", "METRO",
  "TRANTIME", "TRANWORK", "CIHISPEED", "CINETHH", "CILAPTOP", "CISMRTPHN",
  "CITABLET", "HCOVANY"
)
acs_variables_needed <- unique(acs_variables_needed)
print("... Defined list of required ACS variables.")

# --- Analysis Settings IIIA.2 ---
fine_group_detail_level <- "Groups_4" # MODIFIED: Changed from "Groups_20" to "Groups_4" for Quartiles
print(paste("... Fine group detail level (for assignment) set to:", fine_group_detail_level))

# ==== IIIA.1 GET DATA (IPUMS Download - Optional) ====

if (is.null(ddi_file_path_input) || !file.exists(ddi_file_path_input)) {
  
  print("--- DDI file path not provided or invalid, proceeding with IPUMS download ---")
  
  # ===== IIIA.1.1 Define Extract =====
  extract_description <- paste("1/3 Country Project - ACS Data", acs_sample_id, "Final Vars FINAL -", Sys.Date()) # FINAL flag
  if (!is.character(acs_variables_needed)) { stop("FATAL ERROR: 'acs_variables_needed' must be a character vector.") }
  if (any(is.na(acs_variables_needed)) || any(acs_variables_needed == "")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: 'acs_variables_needed' contains NA or empty strings.") }
  
  acs_extract_def <- define_extract_micro(
    collection = ipums_collection,
    samples = acs_sample_id,
    variables = acs_variables_needed,
    description = extract_description
  )
  print("... IPUMS ACS extract definition created.")
  
  # ===== IIIA.1.2 Submit & Download =====
  submitted_extract <- tryCatch({
    submit_extract(acs_extract_def)
  }, error = function(e) {
    message("IPUMS API Error during submission:")
    message(e$message); stop("Extract submission failed. Check variable/sample names for ", acs_sample_id," and API key.")
  })
  
  extract_number <- submitted_extract$number
  print(paste("... ACS extract submitted. Number:", extract_number))
  print("... Waiting for extract preparation (this may take several minutes)...")
  Sys.sleep(10) # Wait for IPUMS servers
  downloadable_extract <- wait_for_extract(submitted_extract)
  print("... ACS extract is ready for download.")
  
  # --- Create Dataset-Specific Download Directory ---
  download_subdir_name <- paste0("ACS_Extract_", extract_number, "_", Sys.Date()) # Corrected name
  download_dir_specific <- file.path(extract_download_parent_dir, download_subdir_name)
  if (!dir.exists(download_dir_specific)) {
    can_create <- tryCatch({dir.create(download_dir_specific, recursive = TRUE); TRUE},
                           warning = function(w){ print(paste("Warning creating dir:", w)); TRUE },
                           error = function(e){ print(paste("Error creating dir:", e)); FALSE })
    if(!can_create){ stop("FATAL ERROR: Could not create download subdirectory. Check permissions and path: ", download_dir_specific) }
    print(paste("... Created download subdirectory:", download_dir_specific))
  } else {
    print(paste("... Download subdirectory already exists:", download_dir_specific))
  }
  
  # --- Download Extract Files ---
  print(paste("... Downloading extract files to:", download_dir_specific))
  downloaded_files <- tryCatch({
    download_extract(downloadable_extract, download_dir = download_dir_specific, overwrite = TRUE)
  }, error = function(e) {
    stop(paste("FATAL ERROR during file download:", e$message))
  })
  print("... ACS data (.dat.gz) and DDI (.xml) files downloaded:")
  print(downloaded_files)
  
  # --- Find DDI File Path ---
  ddi_file_path <- downloaded_files[grep("\\.xml$", downloaded_files)]
  if (length(ddi_file_path) == 0) {
    # Fallback search if direct path not found in downloaded_files
    ddi_file_path <- list.files(path = download_dir_specific, pattern = paste0(ipums_collection, "_", sprintf("%05d", extract_number), "\\.xml$"), full.names = TRUE)
    if (length(ddi_file_path) == 0) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Could not find downloaded DDI (.xml) file in directory:", download_dir_specific)) }
    else { print("... Found DDI file via directory search.") }
  }
  if (length(ddi_file_path) > 1) { warning("Multiple DDI files found, using the first one."); ddi_file_path <- ddi_file_path[1] }
  print(paste("... DDI file path identified:", ddi_file_path))
  
  # --- Generate Text Codebook ---
  codebook_output_filename <- file.path(
    download_dir_specific,
    paste0(gsub("\\.xml$", "", basename(ddi_file_path)), "_codebook.txt")
  )
  if(exists("generate_codebook_from_ddi") && is.function(generate_codebook_from_ddi)){
    generate_codebook_from_ddi(
      ddi_path = ddi_file_path,
      vars_list = acs_variables_needed, # Use the full list of requested vars
      output_txt_path = codebook_output_filename
    )
  } else {
    warning("generate_codebook_from_ddi function not found (is II_Shared_Functions.R sourced?). Skipping codebook generation.")
  }
  
  print("==== Data Download Complete ====")
  
} else {
  print(paste("--- Skipping IPUMS download. Using provided DDI file:", ddi_file_path_input, "---"))
  ddi_file_path <- ddi_file_path_input
  if (!file.exists(ddi_file_path)){
    stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Provided DDI file path does not exist:", ddi_file_path))
  }
  # Optionally generate codebook even if DDI is provided
  codebook_output_filename <- file.path(
    dirname(ddi_file_path), # Save in same dir as DDI
    paste0(gsub("\\.xml$", "", basename(ddi_file_path)), "_codebook.txt")
  )
  if(exists("generate_codebook_from_ddi") && is.function(generate_codebook_from_ddi)){
    generate_codebook_from_ddi(
      ddi_path = ddi_file_path,
      vars_list = acs_variables_needed,
      output_txt_path = codebook_output_filename
    )
  } else {
    warning("generate_codebook_from_ddi function not found (is II_Shared_Functions.R sourced?). Skipping codebook generation.")
  }
}


# ==== IIIA.2 INITIAL CLEAN & PREP (REVISED CITIZEN CLEANING) ====

# ===== IIIA.2.0 Load Data Using DDI =====
print("--- Loading ACS Data using DDI ---")
if (!exists("ddi_file_path") || !file.exists(ddi_file_path)) {
  stop("FATAL ERROR: ddi_file_path not defined or file does not exist.")
}
raw_data_acs <- read_ipums_micro(ddi = ddi_file_path, verbose = FALSE)
print("... ACS Data loaded successfully using DDI.")
print(paste("Dimensions of raw ACS data:", paste(dim(raw_data_acs), collapse = " x ")))

clean_data_acs <- raw_data_acs 
rm(raw_data_acs); gc() 

# ===== IIIA.2.1 Handle Missing/Special Values (REVISED CITIZEN CLEANING) =====
print("--- Cleaning ACS Data (REVISED CITIZEN CLEANING) ---")
hhincome_na_codes <- c(9999999)
if ("HHINCOME" %in% names(clean_data_acs)) {
  clean_data_acs <- clean_data_acs %>% mutate(HHINCOME = if_else(HHINCOME %in% hhincome_na_codes, NA_real_, as.numeric(HHINCOME)))
} else { warning("HHINCOME variable not found.") }
poverty_codes_to_na <- c(0)
if ("POVERTY" %in% names(clean_data_acs)) {
  clean_data_acs <- clean_data_acs %>% mutate(POVERTY = if_else(POVERTY %in% poverty_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(POVERTY)))
} else { warning("POVERTY variable not found.")}
empstat_codes_to_na <- c(0, 9)
if ("EMPSTAT" %in% names(clean_data_acs)) {
  clean_data_acs <- clean_data_acs %>% mutate(EMPSTAT = if_else(EMPSTAT %in% empstat_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(EMPSTAT)))
} else { warning("EMPSTAT variable not found.")}
hispan_codes_to_na <- c(9)
if ("HISPAN" %in% names(clean_data_acs)) {
  clean_data_acs <- clean_data_acs %>% mutate(HISPAN = if_else(HISPAN %in% hispan_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(HISPAN)))
} else { warning("HISPAN variable not found.") }
citizen_codes_to_na <- c(0, 8, 9) 
if ("CITIZEN" %in% names(clean_data_acs)) {
  print(paste("... Cleaning CITIZEN - Setting codes", paste(citizen_codes_to_na, collapse=", "), "to NA. Code 5 (Non-Citizen) is kept valid."))
  clean_data_acs <- clean_data_acs %>% mutate(CITIZEN = if_else(CITIZEN %in% citizen_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(CITIZEN)))
} else { warning("CITIZEN variable not found.")}
housing_codes_to_na_generic <- c(0) 
if ("KITCHEN" %in% names(clean_data_acs)) clean_data_acs <- clean_data_acs %>% mutate(KITCHEN = if_else(KITCHEN %in% housing_codes_to_na_generic, NA_integer_, as.integer(KITCHEN))) 
if ("HOTWATER" %in% names(clean_data_acs)) clean_data_acs <- clean_data_acs %>% mutate(HOTWATER = if_else(HOTWATER %in% housing_codes_to_na_generic, NA_integer_, as.integer(HOTWATER)))
vehicles_codes_to_na <- c(0) 
if ("VEHICLES" %in% names(clean_data_acs)) clean_data_acs <- clean_data_acs %>% mutate(VEHICLES = if_else(VEHICLES %in% vehicles_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(VEHICLES)))
if ("UNITSSTR" %in% names(clean_data_acs)) clean_data_acs <- clean_data_acs %>% mutate(UNITSSTR = if_else(UNITSSTR %in% housing_codes_to_na_generic, NA_integer_, as.integer(UNITSSTR)))
if ("OWNERSHP" %in% names(clean_data_acs)) clean_data_acs <- clean_data_acs %>% mutate(OWNERSHP = if_else(OWNERSHP %in% housing_codes_to_na_generic, NA_integer_, as.integer(OWNERSHP)))
if ("METRO" %in% names(clean_data_acs)) clean_data_acs <- clean_data_acs %>% mutate(METRO = if_else(METRO %in% housing_codes_to_na_generic, NA_integer_, as.integer(METRO))) 
if ("BEDROOMS" %in% names(clean_data_acs)) clean_data_acs <- clean_data_acs %>% mutate(BEDROOMS = if_else(BEDROOMS == 0, NA_integer_, as.integer(BEDROOMS)))
cihispeed_codes_to_na <- c(0, 88)
if ("CIHISPEED" %in% names(clean_data_acs)) clean_data_acs <- clean_data_acs %>% mutate(CIHISPEED = if_else(CIHISPEED %in% cihispeed_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(CIHISPEED)))
cinethh_codes_to_na <- c(0)
if ("CINETHH" %in% names(clean_data_acs)) clean_data_acs <- clean_data_acs %>% mutate(CINETHH = if_else(CINETHH %in% cinethh_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(CINETHH)))
tech_codes_to_na_laptop_etc <- c(0) 
if ("CILAPTOP" %in% names(clean_data_acs)) clean_data_acs <- clean_data_acs %>% mutate(CILAPTOP = if_else(CILAPTOP %in% tech_codes_to_na_laptop_etc, NA_integer_, as.integer(CILAPTOP)))
if ("CISMRTPHN" %in% names(clean_data_acs)) clean_data_acs <- clean_data_acs %>% mutate(CISMRTPHN = if_else(CISMRTPHN %in% tech_codes_to_na_laptop_etc, NA_integer_, as.integer(CISMRTPHN)))
if ("CITABLET" %in% names(clean_data_acs)) clean_data_acs <- clean_data_acs %>% mutate(CITABLET = if_else(CITABLET %in% tech_codes_to_na_laptop_etc, NA_integer_, as.integer(CITABLET)))
hcovany_codes_to_na <- c(0) 
if ("HCOVANY" %in% names(clean_data_acs)) clean_data_acs <- clean_data_acs %>% mutate(HCOVANY = if_else(HCOVANY %in% hcovany_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(HCOVANY)))
trantime_codes_to_na <- c(0, 888) 
if ("TRANTIME" %in% names(clean_data_acs)) clean_data_acs <- clean_data_acs %>% mutate(TRANTIME = if_else(TRANTIME %in% trantime_codes_to_na, NA_real_, as.numeric(TRANTIME)))
owncost_codes_to_na <- c(99999) 
if ("OWNCOST" %in% names(clean_data_acs)) clean_data_acs <- clean_data_acs %>% mutate(OWNCOST = if_else(OWNCOST %in% owncost_codes_to_na, NA_real_, as.numeric(OWNCOST)))
rentgrs_codes_to_na <- c(0, 1) 
if ("RENTGRS" %in% names(clean_data_acs)) clean_data_acs <- clean_data_acs %>% mutate(RENTGRS = if_else(RENTGRS %in% rentgrs_codes_to_na, NA_real_, as.numeric(RENTGRS)))
if ("MOVEDIN" %in% names(clean_data_acs)) clean_data_acs <- clean_data_acs %>% mutate(MOVEDIN = if_else(MOVEDIN == 0, NA_integer_, as.integer(MOVEDIN))) 
if ("EDUCD" %in% names(clean_data_acs)) clean_data_acs <- clean_data_acs %>% mutate(EDUCD = if_else(EDUCD == 0, NA_integer_, as.integer(EDUCD))) 
if ("HWSEI" %in% names(clean_data_acs)) clean_data_acs <- clean_data_acs %>% mutate(HWSEI = if_else(HWSEI == 0, NA_real_, as.numeric(HWSEI))) 

# ===== IIIA.2.2 Ensure Numeric Types =====
numeric_vars_acs <- c("HHWT", "PERWT", "HHINCOME", "OWNCOST", "RENTGRS", "AGE", "NUMPREC", "TRANTIME", "HWSEI", "POVERTY") 
numeric_vars_acs <- intersect(numeric_vars_acs, names(clean_data_acs)) 
clean_data_acs <- clean_data_acs %>% mutate(across(all_of(numeric_vars_acs), as.numeric))
if ("HHWT" %in% names(clean_data_acs) && any(clean_data_acs$HHWT <= 0, na.rm = TRUE)) warning("Non-positive HHWT values found after cleaning.")
if ("PERWT" %in% names(clean_data_acs) && any(clean_data_acs$PERWT <= 0, na.rm = TRUE)) warning("Non-positive PERWT values found after cleaning.")

# ===== IIIA.2.3 Store Unfiltered Data & Apply Age Filter for Main Analysis =====
clean_data_acs_unfiltered_age <- clean_data_acs
age_var <- "AGE"
if (age_var %in% names(clean_data_acs)) {
  n_before_filter <- nrow(clean_data_acs)
  clean_data_acs <- clean_data_acs %>% filter(.data[[age_var]] >= 18)
  n_after_filter <- nrow(clean_data_acs)
  print(paste("... Applied filter:", age_var, ">= 18 for main analysis.", n_before_filter - n_after_filter, "records removed."))
} else {
  warning(paste("Age variable", age_var, "not found for filtering main analysis dataset."))
}
print("... ACS Data cleaned. Main analysis dataset filtered for AGE >= 18.")

# ==== IIIA.3 CREATE SURVEY DESIGN ====
print("... Skipping survey design object creation (using weighted means directly for summaries).")

# ==== IIIA.4 LOAD INCOME BORDERS (External Borders) ====
print("--- Loading External Income Borders ---")
if (!file.exists(borders_csv_file)) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: EXTERNAL Within-tercile borders file not found:", borders_csv_file)) }
borders_df <- readr::read_csv(borders_csv_file, show_col_types = FALSE)
print("... EXTERNAL Within-tercile borders loaded.")
print("WARNING: Using income borders calculated from a different dataset/year (File I).")

# ==== IIIA.5 LOAD MAIN TERCILE CUTOFFS (External Borders) ====
print("--- Loading External Main Tercile Cutoffs ---")
if (!file.exists(main_cutoffs_rds_file)) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: EXTERNAL Main tercile cutoffs file not found:", main_cutoffs_rds_file)) }
loaded_main_cutoffs <- readRDS(main_cutoffs_rds_file)
if (is.list(loaded_main_cutoffs) && all(c("main_cutoff1", "main_cutoff2") %in% names(loaded_main_cutoffs))) {
  main_cutoff1_val <- loaded_main_cutoffs$main_cutoff1
  main_cutoff2_val <- loaded_main_cutoffs$main_cutoff2
  print(paste("... EXTERNAL Main cutoffs loaded successfully: Cutoff1 =", main_cutoff1_val, ", Cutoff2 =", main_cutoff2_val))
  print("WARNING: Using income cutoffs calculated from a different dataset/year (File I).")
} else { stop("FATAL ERROR: Loaded main cutoffs object does not have the expected structure.") }

# ==== IIIA.6 ASSIGN INCOME GROUPS (Using ACS HHINCOME & External Borders) ====
print("--- Assigning Income Groups ---")
income_variable_name <- "HHINCOME"
weight_variable_name <- "HHWT" 
if (!income_variable_name %in% names(clean_data_acs_unfiltered_age)) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Income variable '", income_variable_name, "' not found in unfiltered ACS data.")) }
if (!weight_variable_name %in% names(clean_data_acs_unfiltered_age)) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Household weight variable", weight_variable_name, "needed for temp design not found in unfiltered ACS data.")) }
data_for_design_unfiltered <- clean_data_acs_unfiltered_age %>%
  filter(!is.na(!!sym(weight_variable_name)) & !!sym(weight_variable_name) > 0 & !is.na(!!sym(income_variable_name)))
if(nrow(data_for_design_unfiltered) == 0) stop("FATAL ERROR: No rows remaining in unfiltered data after filtering for weights/income for group assignment.")
if (!exists("assign_income_groups")) stop("assign_income_groups function not found. Ensure II_Shared_Functions.R is sourced.")
temp_design_for_groups_unfiltered <- survey::svydesign(ids=~1, data=data_for_design_unfiltered, weights=as.formula(paste0("~", weight_variable_name)))
print(paste("... Calling assign_income_groups function with detail level:", fine_group_detail_level, "and income var:", income_variable_name, "on unfiltered data."))
temp_design_with_groups_unfiltered <- assign_income_groups(
  survey_obj = temp_design_for_groups_unfiltered,
  borders_df = borders_df,
  income_var_name = income_variable_name,
  detail_level = fine_group_detail_level,
  main_cutoff1 = main_cutoff1_val,
  main_cutoff2 = main_cutoff2_val
)
data_with_groups_unfiltered <- temp_design_with_groups_unfiltered$variables 
original_cols <- names(clean_data_acs_unfiltered_age)
group_data_to_merge <- data_with_groups_unfiltered %>%
  distinct(SERIAL, income_tercile, fine_income_group) 
data_final <- left_join(clean_data_acs, group_data_to_merge, by = "SERIAL")
clean_data_acs_unfiltered_age <- left_join(clean_data_acs_unfiltered_age, group_data_to_merge, by = "SERIAL")
rm(temp_design_for_groups_unfiltered, temp_design_with_groups_unfiltered, borders_df, loaded_main_cutoffs, data_for_design_unfiltered, group_data_to_merge, data_with_groups_unfiltered, clean_data_acs)
gc()
print("... Income groups assigned and merged to person-level data.")

# ==== IIIA.7 CALCULATE DERIVED VARIABLES (UPDATED RACE/ETHNICITY & CORRECTED NON-CITIZEN) ====
# ... (Derived variable calculation logic remains the same) ...
print("--- Calculating Derived Variables for ACS (Updated Race/Ethnicity & Corrected Non-Citizen) ---")
if (!exists("data_final")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: 'data_final' (adult data with groups) not found.") }
if (!exists("clean_data_acs_unfiltered_age")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: 'clean_data_acs_unfiltered_age' (unfiltered data with groups) not found.") }
age_var <- "AGE"; race_var <- "RACE"; hisp_var <- "HISPAN"; citizen_var <- "CITIZEN"
empstat_var <- "EMPSTAT"; poverty_var <- "POVERTY"; occ_var <- "OCC"; ownershp_var <- "OWNERSHP"
owncost_var <- "OWNCOST"; rentgrs_var <- "RENTGRS"; hhincome_var <- "HHINCOME" 
movedin_var <- "MOVEDIN"; bedrooms_var <- "BEDROOMS"; numprec_var <- "NUMPREC" 
kitchen_var <- "KITCHEN"; hotwater_var <- "HOTWATER"; vehicles_var <- "VEHICLES"
unitsstr_var <- "UNITSSTR"; metro_var <- "METRO"; trantime_var <- "TRANTIME"
tranwork_var <- "TRANWORK"; hcovany_var <- "HCOVANY"; cihispeed_var <- "CIHISPEED"
cinethh_var <- "CINETHH"; cilaptop_var <- "CILAPTOP"; cismrtphn_var <- "CISMRTPHN"
citablet_var <- "CITABLET"; hwsei_var <- "HWSEI"
calculate_derived_vars_acs_v2 <- function(df) {
  required_input_vars <- c(age_var, race_var, hisp_var, citizen_var, empstat_var, poverty_var, ownershp_var,
                           owncost_var, rentgrs_var, hhincome_var, movedin_var, bedrooms_var, numprec_var,
                           kitchen_var, hotwater_var, vehicles_var, unitsstr_var, metro_var, trantime_var,
                           hcovany_var, cihispeed_var, cinethh_var, cilaptop_var, cismrtphn_var, citablet_var, hwsei_var)
  missing_inputs <- setdiff(required_input_vars, names(df))
  if(length(missing_inputs) > 0) {
    warning("Input data frame is missing columns required for derived variable calculation: ", paste(missing_inputs, collapse=", "))
  }
  df <- df %>% mutate(
    race_ethnicity_plot = case_when(
      !is.na(.data[[hisp_var]]) & .data[[hisp_var]] != 0 ~ "Hispanic",        
      !is.na(.data[[hisp_var]]) & .data[[hisp_var]] == 0 & .data[[race_var]] == 1 ~ "White_NH",     
      !is.na(.data[[hisp_var]]) & .data[[hisp_var]] == 0 & .data[[race_var]] == 2 ~ "Black_NH",     
      !is.na(.data[[hisp_var]]) & .data[[hisp_var]] == 0 & .data[[race_var]] == 3 ~ "Indigenous_NH",
      !is.na(.data[[hisp_var]]) & .data[[hisp_var]] == 0 & .data[[race_var]] %in% c(4,5,6) ~ "Asian_NH", 
      TRUE ~ NA_character_ 
    ),
    race_category = case_when(
      !is.na(.data[[hisp_var]]) & .data[[hisp_var]] != 0 ~ "Hispanic",
      !is.na(.data[[hisp_var]]) & .data[[hisp_var]] == 0 & .data[[race_var]] == 1 ~ "White",
      !is.na(.data[[hisp_var]]) & .data[[hisp_var]] == 0 & .data[[race_var]] == 2 ~ "Black",
      !is.na(.data[[hisp_var]]) & .data[[hisp_var]] == 0 & .data[[race_var]] == 3 ~ "Indigenous", 
      !is.na(.data[[hisp_var]]) & .data[[hisp_var]] == 0 & (.data[[race_var]] == 4 | .data[[race_var]] == 5) ~ "Asian", 
      !is.na(.data[[hisp_var]]) & .data[[hisp_var]] == 0 & .data[[race_var]] == 6 ~ "Other",    
      !is.na(.data[[hisp_var]]) & .data[[hisp_var]] == 0 & .data[[race_var]] %in% c(7,8,9) ~ "Two_Plus", 
      TRUE ~ NA_character_
    ),
    is_employed = if_else(.data[[empstat_var]] == 1, 1, 0, missing = NA_real_), 
    is_below_poverty = if_else(!is.na(.data[[poverty_var]]) & .data[[poverty_var]] <= 100, 1, 0, missing = 0), 
    poverty_level_capped_500 = ifelse(!is.na(POVERTY), pmin(POVERTY, 500), NA_real_),
    is_owner = if_else(.data[[ownershp_var]] == 1, 1, 0, missing = 0), 
    is_renter = if_else(.data[[ownershp_var]] == 2, 1, 0, missing = 0),
    cost_burden = if_else(is_owner == 1 & !is.na(.data[[hhincome_var]]) & .data[[hhincome_var]] > 0 & !is.na(.data[[owncost_var]]), (.data[[owncost_var]] * 12) / .data[[hhincome_var]], NA_real_),
    is_burdened_owner = if_else(is_owner == 1 & !is.na(cost_burden) & cost_burden > 0.30, 1, 0, missing = 0),
    rent_burden = if_else(is_renter == 1 & !is.na(.data[[hhincome_var]]) & .data[[hhincome_var]] > 0 & !is.na(.data[[rentgrs_var]]), (.data[[rentgrs_var]] * 12) / .data[[hhincome_var]], NA_real_),
    is_burdened_renter = if_else(is_renter == 1 & !is.na(rent_burden) & rent_burden > 0.30, 1, 0, missing = 0),
    movedin_est_years = case_when(
      .data[[movedin_var]] == 1 ~ 0.5, 
      .data[[movedin_var]] == 2 ~ (1+4)/2, 
      .data[[movedin_var]] == 3 ~ (5+9)/2, 
      .data[[movedin_var]] == 4 ~ (10+19)/2,
      .data[[movedin_var]] == 5 ~ (20+29)/2,
      .data[[movedin_var]] == 6 ~ (30+39)/2,
      .data[[movedin_var]] == 7 ~ 45.0,    
      TRUE ~ NA_real_
    ),
    bedrooms_numeric = case_when(
      .data[[bedrooms_var]] == 1 ~ 0,
      .data[[bedrooms_var]] >= 2 & .data[[bedrooms_var]] <= 5 ~ .data[[bedrooms_var]] - 1, 
      .data[[bedrooms_var]] == 6 ~ 5, 
      TRUE ~ NA_real_
    ),
    persons_per_bedroom = case_when(
      !is.na(bedrooms_numeric) & bedrooms_numeric > 0 & !is.na(.data[[numprec_var]]) ~ .data[[numprec_var]] / bedrooms_numeric,
      !is.na(bedrooms_numeric) & bedrooms_numeric == 0 & !is.na(.data[[numprec_var]]) & .data[[numprec_var]] > 0 ~ 99, 
      !is.na(bedrooms_numeric) & bedrooms_numeric == 0 & !is.na(.data[[numprec_var]]) & .data[[numprec_var]] == 0 ~ 0, 
      TRUE ~ NA_real_
    ),
    has_hot_cold_water = if_else(.data[[hotwater_var]] == 4, 1, 0, missing = 0),
    has_cold_water_only = if_else(.data[[hotwater_var]] == 3, 1, 0, missing = 0),
    lacks_kitchen = if_else(.data[[kitchen_var]] == 3, 1, 0, missing = NA_real_), 
    vehicle_group = case_when(
      .data[[vehicles_var]] == 9 ~ "0",
      .data[[vehicles_var]] == 1 ~ "1",
      .data[[vehicles_var]] >= 2 & .data[[vehicles_var]] <= 7 ~ "2+", 
      TRUE ~ NA_character_
    ),
    metro_status = case_when(
      .data[[metro_var]] %in% c(2, 3, 4) ~ "Metro",
      .data[[metro_var]] == 1 ~ "Non-Metro",
      TRUE ~ NA_character_
    ),
    has_broadband = case_when(
      .data[[cihispeed_var]] >= 10 & .data[[cihispeed_var]] <= 17 ~ 1, 
      .data[[cihispeed_var]] == 20 ~ 0, 
      !is.na(.data[[cihispeed_var]]) ~ 0, 
      TRUE ~ NA_real_
    ),
    has_internet = case_when(
      .data[[cinethh_var]] %in% c(1, 2) ~ 1, 
      .data[[cinethh_var]] == 3 ~ 0,        
      TRUE ~ NA_real_
    ),
    has_computer = if_else(.data[[cilaptop_var]] == 1, 1, 0, missing = NA_real_), 
    has_smartphone = if_else(.data[[cismrtphn_var]] == 1, 1, 0, missing = NA_real_),
    has_tablet = if_else(.data[[citablet_var]] == 1, 1, 0, missing = NA_real_),
    has_any_device = case_when(
      has_smartphone == 1 | has_tablet == 1 | has_computer == 1 ~ 1,
      is.na(has_smartphone) & is.na(has_tablet) & is.na(has_computer) ~ NA_real_, 
      TRUE ~ 0 
    ),
    has_insurance = if_else(.data[[hcovany_var]] == 2, 1, 0, missing = 0), 
    is_non_citizen = case_when(
      .data[[citizen_var]] == 5 ~ 1,             
      .data[[citizen_var]] %in% c(1, 2, 3, 4) ~ 0, 
      TRUE ~ NA_real_                            
    ),
    is_employed_25_65 = if_else(AGE >= 25 & AGE <= 65, is_employed, NA_integer_),
    is_below_poverty_child = if_else(AGE < 18, is_below_poverty, NA_integer_), 
    TRANTIME_metro_worker = if_else(metro_status == "Metro" & is_employed == 1, .data[[trantime_var]], NA_real_)
  )
  return(df)
}
data_final <- calculate_derived_vars_acs_v2(data_final)
clean_data_acs_unfiltered_age <- calculate_derived_vars_acs_v2(clean_data_acs_unfiltered_age) 
print("... Derived variables calculated (using race_category and corrected non-citizen logic).")

# ==== IIIA.8 CALCULATE GROUPED SUMMARY STATISTICS ====
# ... (Summary statistics calculation logic remains the same) ...
print("--- Calculating Grouped Summary Statistics for ACS ---")
grouping_vars_fine <- c("income_tercile", "fine_income_group")
hhwt_var <- "HHWT"  
perwt_var <- "PERWT" 
if (!all(grouping_vars_fine %in% names(data_final))) {
  stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Fine grouping variables not found in data_final:", paste(setdiff(grouping_vars_fine, names(data_final)), collapse=", ")))
}
if (!hhwt_var %in% names(data_final)) stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Household weight variable", hhwt_var, "not found in data_final."))
if (!perwt_var %in% names(data_final)) stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Person weight variable", perwt_var, "not found in data_final."))
weighted_median_approx <- function(x, w, na.rm = TRUE) {
  if (na.rm) {
    valid_indices <- !is.na(x) & !is.na(w) & w > 0
    w <- w[valid_indices]
    x <- x[valid_indices]
  }
  if (length(x) == 0 || sum(w, na.rm = TRUE) == 0) return(NA_real_) 
  order_x <- order(x)
  x_sorted <- x[order_x]
  w_sorted <- w[order_x]
  cum_w_prop <- cumsum(w_sorted) / sum(w_sorted, na.rm = TRUE)
  median_val_index <- min(which(cum_w_prop >= 0.5))
  if (is.infinite(median_val_index)) return(NA_real_) 
  median_val <- x_sorted[median_val_index]
  return(median_val)
}
summary_stats_acs_main <- data_final %>%
  filter(!is.na(income_tercile) & !is.na(fine_income_group)) %>% 
  group_by(income_tercile, fine_income_group, .drop = FALSE) %>%
  summarise(
    n_unweighted_persons = sum(!is.na(!!sym(perwt_var)) & !!sym(perwt_var) > 0 & AGE >=18), 
    n_unweighted_households = n_distinct(SERIAL[!is.na(!!sym(hhwt_var)) & !!sym(hhwt_var) > 0]),
    avg_age = weighted.mean(AGE, w = !!sym(perwt_var), na.rm = TRUE),
    prop_non_citizen = weighted.mean(is_non_citizen, w = !!sym(perwt_var), na.rm = TRUE), 
    employment_rate = weighted.mean(is_employed_25_65, w = !!sym(perwt_var), na.rm = TRUE), 
    avg_HWSEI = weighted.mean(if_else(is_employed == 1, HWSEI, NA_real_), w = !!sym(perwt_var), na.rm = TRUE), 
    prop_below_poverty = weighted.mean(is_below_poverty, w = !!sym(perwt_var), na.rm = TRUE), 
    avg_poverty_level_capped_500 = weighted.mean(poverty_level_capped_500, w = !!sym(perwt_var), na.rm = TRUE),
    median_commute_time = weighted_median_approx(TRANTIME_metro_worker, w = !!sym(perwt_var), na.rm = TRUE),
    prop_has_insurance = weighted.mean(has_insurance, w = !!sym(perwt_var), na.rm = TRUE),
    avg_hhincome = weighted.mean(HHINCOME, w = !!sym(hhwt_var), na.rm = TRUE), 
    prop_owner = weighted.mean(is_owner, w = !!sym(hhwt_var), na.rm = TRUE),
    prop_renter = weighted.mean(is_renter, w = !!sym(hhwt_var), na.rm = TRUE),
    prop_burdened_owner = weighted.mean(is_burdened_owner, w = !!sym(hhwt_var), na.rm = TRUE),
    prop_burdened_renter = weighted.mean(is_burdened_renter, w = !!sym(hhwt_var), na.rm = TRUE),
    avg_est_years_residence = weighted.mean(movedin_est_years, w = !!sym(hhwt_var), na.rm = TRUE),
    avg_persons_per_bedroom = weighted.mean(persons_per_bedroom, w = !!sym(hhwt_var), na.rm = TRUE), 
    prop_lacks_kitchen = weighted.mean(lacks_kitchen, w = !!sym(hhwt_var), na.rm = TRUE),
    prop_piped_hot_cold = weighted.mean(has_hot_cold_water, w = !!sym(hhwt_var), na.rm = TRUE),
    prop_piped_cold_only = weighted.mean(has_cold_water_only, w = !!sym(hhwt_var), na.rm = TRUE),
    prop_veh_0 = weighted.mean(vehicle_group == "0", w = !!sym(hhwt_var), na.rm = TRUE),
    prop_veh_1 = weighted.mean(vehicle_group == "1", w = !!sym(hhwt_var), na.rm = TRUE),
    prop_veh_2plus = weighted.mean(vehicle_group == "2+", w = !!sym(hhwt_var), na.rm = TRUE),
    prop_broadband = weighted.mean(has_broadband, w = !!sym(hhwt_var), na.rm = TRUE),
    prop_any_internet = weighted.mean(has_internet, w = !!sym(hhwt_var), na.rm = TRUE),
    prop_computer = weighted.mean(has_computer, w = !!sym(hhwt_var), na.rm = TRUE),
    prop_any_device = weighted.mean(has_any_device, w = !!sym(hhwt_var), na.rm = TRUE),
    .groups = "drop"
  )
if (exists("clean_data_acs_unfiltered_age") &&
    "is_below_poverty_child" %in% names(clean_data_acs_unfiltered_age) &&
    all(c("income_tercile", "fine_income_group", perwt_var) %in% names(clean_data_acs_unfiltered_age))) {
  summary_stats_child_poverty <- clean_data_acs_unfiltered_age %>%
    filter(!is.na(income_tercile) & !is.na(fine_income_group)) %>% 
    filter(AGE < 18) %>% 
    filter(!is.na(is_below_poverty_child)) %>% 
    group_by(income_tercile, fine_income_group, .drop = FALSE) %>%
    summarise(
      prop_child_below_poverty = weighted.mean(is_below_poverty_child, w = !!sym(perwt_var), na.rm = TRUE),
      .groups = "drop"
    )
  summary_stats_acs_main <- left_join(summary_stats_acs_main, summary_stats_child_poverty, by = c("income_tercile", "fine_income_group"))
  rm(summary_stats_child_poverty)
} else {
  warning("Could not calculate child poverty. Unfiltered data, 'is_below_poverty_child', or grouping/weight vars might be missing.")
  summary_stats_acs_main$prop_child_below_poverty <- NA_real_ 
}
print("... Main summary statistics calculated.")

# ==== IIIA.8.5 Calculate Race/Ethnicity-Specific Summary Statistics ====
# ... (Race/Ethnicity summary statistics calculation logic remains the same) ...
print("--- Calculating Race/Ethnicity-Specific Summary Statistics (Using race_category) ---")
race_eth_var <- "race_category" 
if(exists("clean_data_acs_unfiltered_age") &&
   all(c(perwt_var, race_eth_var, "income_tercile", "fine_income_group") %in% names(clean_data_acs_unfiltered_age))) {
  summary_stats_total_pop <- clean_data_acs_unfiltered_age %>%
    filter(!is.na(income_tercile) & !is.na(fine_income_group)) %>%
    filter(!is.na(!!sym(perwt_var)) & !!sym(perwt_var) > 0) %>%
    group_by(income_tercile, fine_income_group) %>%
    summarise(total_weighted_pop_group = sum(!!sym(perwt_var), na.rm = TRUE), .groups = "drop") %>%
    filter(total_weighted_pop_group > 0)
  if(nrow(summary_stats_total_pop) == 0) {
    warning("Calculation of total weighted population for race/ethnicity yielded zero rows. Race proportions might be NA.")
    summary_stats_acs <- summary_stats_acs_main 
  } else {
    summary_stats_race_eth_comp_long <- clean_data_acs_unfiltered_age %>%
      filter(!is.na(income_tercile) & !is.na(fine_income_group)) %>%
      filter(!is.na(!!sym(race_eth_var))) %>% 
      filter(!is.na(!!sym(perwt_var)) & !!sym(perwt_var) > 0) %>%
      group_by(income_tercile, fine_income_group, !!sym(race_eth_var)) %>%
      summarise(
        weighted_pop_race_eth = sum(!!sym(perwt_var), na.rm = TRUE),
        .groups = "drop"
      )
    if(nrow(summary_stats_race_eth_comp_long) == 0) warning("Calculation of weighted pop per race/ethnicity group yielded zero rows.")
    summary_stats_race_eth_comp <- summary_stats_race_eth_comp_long %>%
      inner_join(summary_stats_total_pop, by = c("income_tercile", "fine_income_group")) %>%
      mutate(prop_race_ethnicity = weighted_pop_race_eth / total_weighted_pop_group) %>%
      select(income_tercile, fine_income_group, !!sym(race_eth_var), prop_race_ethnicity)
    print("... Race/Ethnicity composition calculated.")
    summary_stats_diversity <- summary_stats_race_eth_comp %>%
      group_by(income_tercile, fine_income_group) %>%
      summarise(
        diversity_index = if(n() > 0 && sum(prop_race_ethnicity^2, na.rm = TRUE) <= 1.0001) { 
          1 - sum(prop_race_ethnicity^2, na.rm = TRUE)
        } else { NA_real_ },
        .groups = "drop"
      )
    print("... Racial/Ethnic diversity index calculated.")
    summary_stats_race_eth_wide <- summary_stats_race_eth_comp %>%
      mutate(race_eth_col_name = paste0("prop_race_", make.names(!!sym(race_eth_var)))) %>% 
      select(income_tercile, fine_income_group, race_eth_col_name, prop_race_ethnicity) %>%
      tidyr::pivot_wider(
        names_from = race_eth_col_name,
        values_from = prop_race_ethnicity,
        values_fill = 0 
      )
    print("... Race/Ethnicity composition reshaped to wide format.")
    summary_stats_acs <- left_join(summary_stats_acs_main, summary_stats_diversity, by = c("income_tercile", "fine_income_group"))
    summary_stats_acs <- left_join(summary_stats_acs, summary_stats_race_eth_wide, by = c("income_tercile", "fine_income_group"))
    print("... Diversity index and wide race/ethnicity proportions joined to main ACS summary.")
    rm(summary_stats_race_eth_comp_long, summary_stats_race_eth_comp, summary_stats_diversity, summary_stats_race_eth_wide, summary_stats_total_pop)
  }
} else {
  warning("Could not calculate race/ethnicity composition/diversity index due to missing base columns in unfiltered data.")
  summary_stats_acs <- summary_stats_acs_main 
}
if(!exists("summary_stats_acs")) { summary_stats_acs <- summary_stats_acs_main }
if(exists("summary_stats_acs_main") && exists("summary_stats_acs") && !identical(summary_stats_acs, summary_stats_acs_main) && all(names(summary_stats_acs_main) %in% names(summary_stats_acs))) {
  rm(summary_stats_acs_main)
}
expected_race_prop_cols <- paste0("prop_race_", make.names(c("Hispanic", "White", "Black", "Indigenous", "Asian", "Other", "Two_Plus")))
race_eth_prop_cols_final <- intersect(expected_race_prop_cols, colnames(summary_stats_acs))
if(length(race_eth_prop_cols_final) > 0) {
  na_in_props <- summary_stats_acs %>% select(all_of(race_eth_prop_cols_final)) %>% is.na() %>% sum()
  if(na_in_props > 0) { warning(paste("Problem:", na_in_props, "NA values detected in final race/ethnicity proportion columns.")) }
  else { print("... Final check: No NA values found in calculated race/ethnicity proportion columns.") }
} else { warning("Final check: No race/ethnicity proportion columns found with expected naming convention (prop_race_...).") }
print("First few rows of final summary_stats_acs:")
print(head(summary_stats_acs))
print("Structure of final summary_stats_acs:")
str(summary_stats_acs)
attr(summary_stats_acs, "analysis_note") <- paste(
  "Stats are weighted means/props. Race/Eth categories (race_category) are mutually exclusive (Hispanic first).",
  "Non-citizen logic corrected. External borders used for income groups. Complex design not adjusted for SEs."
)
print(attr(summary_stats_acs, "analysis_note"))

# ==== IIIA.9 SAVE SUMMARY RESULTS ====
print("--- Saving ACS Summary Results ---")
if (!exists("summary_output_dir")) stop("FATAL ERROR: summary_output_dir not defined.")
if (!dir.exists(summary_output_dir)) dir.create(summary_output_dir, recursive = TRUE)

# MODIFIED FILENAME FOR IV2 COMPATIBILITY
output_rds_filename_acs <- file.path(summary_output_dir, paste0("acs_", acs_sample_id, "_summary_stats_G_groups.rds")) 

if (exists("summary_stats_acs") && !is.null(summary_stats_acs) && nrow(summary_stats_acs) > 0) {
  print(paste("... Saving ACS summary results to:", output_rds_filename_acs))
  tryCatch({ saveRDS(summary_stats_acs, file = output_rds_filename_acs) ; print("... Results saved successfully.") },
           error = function(e) { warning("Error saving summary results to RDS file: ", e$message) })
} else { warning("Summary statistics object 'summary_stats_acs' is empty or does not exist. Skipping save.") }


# ==== IIIA.10 CLEAN UP MEMORY ====
print("--- Cleaning up ACS Memory ---")
objects_to_remove_acs <- c(
  "data_final", "summary_stats_acs", "clean_data_acs_unfiltered_age",
  "acs_extract_def", "submitted_extract", "downloadable_extract", "downloaded_files" 
)
objects_exist_acs <- objects_to_remove_acs[sapply(objects_to_remove_acs, exists)]
if(length(objects_exist_acs) > 0) {
  rm(list = objects_exist_acs)
  print(paste("... Removed objects:", paste(objects_exist_acs, collapse=", ")))
} else {
  print("... No specified large objects found for removal.")
}
gc()
print("==== Memory cleanup attempted. Script IIIA (Filename Corrected) finished. ====")

# Location: Phoenix, Arizona, United States # <<< Current Location

# Purpose:
# This R script performs the complete processing for CPS ASEC data
# (sample cps2023_03s for CY2022 income) as part of Stage 3.
# It ALSO loads the corresponding basic monthly CPS data (cps2023_03b)
# and merges it using MARBASECIDP to obtain variables like UH_DSCWK_B2 needed
# for U-6 unemployment calculations.
# It defines parameters, requests variables from IPUMS, downloads data and DDI
# (optional if DDI path provided), loads data, cleans data based on
# DDI metadata assumptions (USER VERIFICATION REQUIRED), loads external income borders,
# assigns income groups using HHINCOME, calculates derived variables (incl. U-6 components),
# calculates summary statistics (incl. U-3, U-6, and Education Proportions) as per the Master Viz and Data Plan v1,
# and saves the results.
# USER MUST VERIFY HARDCODED VALUES/CODES IN CLEANING/DERIVED VARS AGAINST THEIR DDI.

# NOTE: This is File IIIB of V+ (Dataset Processing - CPS ASEC + Basic Merge)

# Workflow Steps Covered (Sections in this script):
# IIIB.0: Setup & Parameters
# IIIB.1: Get Data (Load ASEC & Basic Monthly CPS)
# IIIB.2: Initial Clean & Prep (ASEC & Basic Monthly Separately)
# IIIB.2.5: Merge ASEC and Basic Monthly Data (Using MARBASECIDP)
# IIIB.3: Create Survey Design (Skipped - Using Weighted Means)
# IIIB.4: Load Income Borders (External Borders from File I)
# IIIB.5: Load Main Tercile Cutoffs (External Borders from File I)
# IIIB.6: Assign Income Groups (Using ASEC HHINCOME & External Borders)
# IIIB.7: Calculate Derived Variables (Corrected U-6 Components)
# IIIB.8: Calculate Grouped Summary Statistics (Main + Refined Educ Calc) 
# IIIB.8.5: Calculate Race-Specific Summary Statistics (Placeholder)
# IIIB.9: Save Summary Table
# IIIB.10: Clean Up Memory

# ==== IIIB.0 SETUP & PARAMETERS ====
