# ==== IIIB.1 GET DATA (Load ASEC & Basic Monthly CPS) ====
download_and_load_ipums <- function(sample_id, variables, collection = "cps", description_suffix, output_parent_dir, provided_ddi_path = NULL) {
  if (!is.null(provided_ddi_path) && file.exists(provided_ddi_path)) {
    print(paste("--- Loading data using provided DDI:", provided_ddi_path, "---"))
    ddi_file_path <- provided_ddi_path
    codebook_output_filename <- file.path(
      dirname(ddi_file_path),
      paste0(gsub("\\.xml$", "", basename(ddi_file_path)), "_codebook.txt")
    )
    if(exists("generate_codebook_from_ddi") && is.function(generate_codebook_from_ddi)){
      generate_codebook_from_ddi(
        ddi_path = ddi_file_path,
        vars_list = variables, 
        output_txt_path = codebook_output_filename
      )
    } else {
      warning("generate_codebook_from_ddi function not found. Skipping codebook generation.")
    }
  } else {
    print(paste("--- DDI not provided or invalid for", sample_id, ". Proceeding with download ---"))
    extract_description <- paste("1/3 Country Project -", description_suffix, "-", Sys.Date())
    print(paste("--- Processing Sample:", sample_id, "(Collection:", collection, ") ---"))
    if (!is.character(variables) || length(variables) == 0) { stop("FATAL ERROR: 'variables' must be a non-empty character vector.") }
    variables <- unique(variables)
    extract_def <- define_extract_micro(
      collection = collection, samples = sample_id, variables = variables, description = extract_description
    )
    print("... IPUMS extract definition created.")
    submitted_extract <- tryCatch({ submit_extract(extract_def) },
                                  error = function(e) { message("IPUMS API Error during submission for ", sample_id, ":"); message(e$message); stop("Extract submission failed.") })
    extract_number <- submitted_extract$number
    print(paste("... Extract submitted. Number:", extract_number))
    print("... Waiting for extract preparation...")
    Sys.sleep(10)
    downloadable_extract <- wait_for_extract(submitted_extract)
    print("... Extract is ready for download.")
    download_subdir_name <- paste0(toupper(collection), "_Extract_", extract_number, "_", Sys.Date())
    download_dir_specific <- file.path(output_parent_dir, download_subdir_name) 
    if (!dir.exists(download_dir_specific)) {
      dir.create(download_dir_specific, recursive = TRUE)
      print(paste("... Created download subdirectory:", download_dir_specific))
    } else {
      print(paste("... Download subdirectory already exists:", download_dir_specific))
    }
    print(paste("... Downloading extract files to:", download_dir_specific))
    downloaded_files <- tryCatch({ download_extract(downloadable_extract, download_dir = download_dir_specific, overwrite = TRUE) },
                                 error = function(e) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR during file download:", e$message)) })
    print("... Files downloaded.")
    ddi_file_path <- downloaded_files[grep("\\.xml$", downloaded_files)]
    if (length(ddi_file_path) == 0) {
      ddi_file_path <- list.files(path = download_dir_specific, pattern = paste0(collection, "_", sprintf("%05d", extract_number), "\\.xml$"), full.names = TRUE)
      if (length(ddi_file_path) == 0) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Could not find downloaded DDI (.xml) file in:", download_dir_specific)) }
    }
    ddi_file_path <- ddi_file_path[1]
    print(paste("... DDI file path identified:", ddi_file_path))
    codebook_output_filename <- file.path(download_dir_specific, paste0(gsub("\\.xml$", "", basename(ddi_file_path)), "_codebook.txt"))
    if(exists("generate_codebook_from_ddi") && is.function(generate_codebook_from_ddi)){
      print("... Generating text codebook...")
      generate_codebook_from_ddi(ddi_path = ddi_file_path, vars_list = variables, output_txt_path = codebook_output_filename)
    } else { warning("generate_codebook_from_ddi function not found. Skipping codebook generation.") }
  } 
  raw_data <- read_ipums_micro(ddi = ddi_file_path, verbose = FALSE)
  print(paste("...", sample_id, "Data loaded successfully."))
  print(paste("Dimensions of raw data:", paste(dim(raw_data), collapse = " x ")))
  return(list(data = raw_data, ddi = ddi_file_path))
}
asec_load_result <- download_and_load_ipums(
  sample_id = cps_asec_sample_id,
  variables = cps_asec_variables_needed,
  collection = ipums_collection,
  description_suffix = paste("ASEC", cps_asec_sample_id),
  output_parent_dir = extract_download_parent_dir,
  provided_ddi_path = ddi_asec_path_input 
)
raw_data_asec <- asec_load_result$data
ddi_asec <- asec_load_result$ddi
basic_load_result <- download_and_load_ipums(
  sample_id = cps_basic_sample_id,
  variables = cps_basic_variables_needed,
  collection = ipums_collection,
  description_suffix = paste("Basic", cps_basic_sample_id),
  output_parent_dir = extract_download_parent_dir,
  provided_ddi_path = ddi_basic_path_input 
)
raw_data_basic <- basic_load_result$data
ddi_basic <- basic_load_result$ddi

# ==== IIIB.2 INITIAL CLEAN & PREP (ASEC & Basic Monthly Separately) ====
# ... (Cleaning logic for ASEC and Basic remains the same) ...
print("--- Cleaning CPS ASEC Data ---")
if (!exists("raw_data_asec")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: raw_data_asec object not found.") }
clean_data_asec <- raw_data_asec
rm(raw_data_asec); gc()
income_vars_to_clean <- c("HHINCOME", "INCWAGE", "INCBUS", "INCFARM", "INCCAPG")
income_vars_present <- intersect(income_vars_to_clean, names(clean_data_asec))
niu_code <- 99999999 
if (length(income_vars_present) > 0) {
  message("... Cleaning continuous income variables: ", paste(income_vars_present, collapse = ", "))
  clean_data_asec <- clean_data_asec %>%
    mutate(across(all_of(income_vars_present),
                  ~ if_else(as.numeric(.) == niu_code, NA_real_, as.numeric(.))))
} else { warning("No specified continuous income variables found for cleaning.") }
empstat_codes_to_na <- c(0); if ("EMPSTAT" %in% names(clean_data_asec)) clean_data_asec <- clean_data_asec %>% mutate(EMPSTAT = if_else(EMPSTAT %in% empstat_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(EMPSTAT))) else warning("EMPSTAT not found.")
whyptlwk_codes_to_na <- c(0); if ("WHYPTLWK" %in% names(clean_data_asec)) clean_data_asec <- clean_data_asec %>% mutate(WHYPTLWK = if_else(WHYPTLWK %in% whyptlwk_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(WHYPTLWK))) else warning("WHYPTLWK not found.")
wkstat_codes_to_na <- c(0); if ("WKSTAT" %in% names(clean_data_asec)) clean_data_asec <- clean_data_asec %>% mutate(WKSTAT = if_else(WKSTAT %in% wkstat_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(WKSTAT))) else warning("WKSTAT not found.")
foodstmp_codes_to_na <- c(0); if ("FOODSTMP" %in% names(clean_data_asec)) clean_data_asec <- clean_data_asec %>% mutate(FOODSTMP = if_else(FOODSTMP %in% foodstmp_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(FOODSTMP))) else warning("FOODSTMP not found.")
diff_vars <- c("DIFFANY", "DIFFMOB", "DIFFREM", "DIFFEYE", "DIFFHEAR", "DIFFCARE"); diff_vars_present <- intersect(diff_vars, names(clean_data_asec)); diff_codes_to_na <- c(0) 
if (length(diff_vars_present) > 0) { clean_data_asec <- clean_data_asec %>% mutate(across(all_of(diff_vars_present), ~ if_else(. %in% diff_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(.)))) } else { warning("No DIFF* variables found.") }
educ_codes_to_na <- c(0, 1, 999); if ("EDUC" %in% names(clean_data_asec)) clean_data_asec <- clean_data_asec %>% mutate(EDUC = if_else(EDUC %in% educ_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(EDUC))) else warning("EDUC not found.")
race_hisp_niu <- c(999); if ("RACE" %in% names(clean_data_asec)) clean_data_asec <- clean_data_asec %>% mutate(RACE = na_if(RACE, race_hisp_niu)); if ("HISPAN" %in% names(clean_data_asec)) clean_data_asec <- clean_data_asec %>% mutate(HISPAN = na_if(HISPAN, race_hisp_niu))
numeric_vars_asec <- c("ASECWT", "ASECWTH", "HHINCOME", "FAMINC", "INCWAGE", "INCBUS", "INCFARM", "INCCAPG", "AGE")
numeric_vars_asec <- intersect(numeric_vars_asec, names(clean_data_asec))
clean_data_asec <- clean_data_asec %>% mutate(across(all_of(numeric_vars_asec), as.numeric))
if ("ASECWT" %in% names(clean_data_asec) && any(clean_data_asec$ASECWT <= 0, na.rm = TRUE)) warning("Non-positive ASECWT values found.")
if ("ASECWTH" %in% names(clean_data_asec) && any(clean_data_asec$ASECWTH <= 0, na.rm = TRUE)) warning("Non-positive ASECWTH values found.")
if (!linking_key %in% names(clean_data_asec)) {
  stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Linking key", linking_key, "not found in ASEC data."))
}
print("... ASEC Data cleaned.")
print("--- Cleaning Basic Monthly CPS Data ---")
if (!exists("raw_data_basic")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: raw_data_basic object not found.") }
clean_data_basic <- raw_data_basic
rm(raw_data_basic); gc()
var_uh_dscwk_b2 <- "UH_DSCWK_B2"
var_link_key_basic <- linking_key 
uh_dscwk_b2_codes_to_na <- c(0) 
if (var_uh_dscwk_b2 %in% names(clean_data_basic)) {
  clean_data_basic <- clean_data_basic %>%
    mutate(!!sym(var_uh_dscwk_b2) := if_else(!!sym(var_uh_dscwk_b2) %in% uh_dscwk_b2_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(!!sym(var_uh_dscwk_b2))))
} else {
  warning(paste(var_uh_dscwk_b2, "not found in basic monthly data. Underemployment calculation will fail."))
}
if (!var_link_key_basic %in% names(clean_data_basic)) {
  stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Linking key", var_link_key_basic, "not found in basic monthly data."))
}
clean_data_basic <- clean_data_basic %>% select(all_of(c(var_link_key_basic, var_uh_dscwk_b2)))
print("... Basic Monthly Data cleaned.")

# ==== IIIB.2.5 Merge ASEC and Basic Monthly Data (Using MARBASECIDP) ==== 
# ... (Merge logic remains the same) ...
print("--- Merging Cleaned ASEC and Basic Monthly Data using MARBASECIDP ---") 
if (!linking_key %in% names(clean_data_asec)) {
  stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Linking key", linking_key, "not found in ASEC data."))
}
if(class(clean_data_asec[[linking_key]]) != class(clean_data_basic[[linking_key]])) {
  warning("Linking key types differ between ASEC and Basic data. Attempting conversion to character.")
  clean_data_asec <- clean_data_asec %>% mutate(!!sym(linking_key) := as.character(!!sym(linking_key)))
  clean_data_basic <- clean_data_basic %>% mutate(!!sym(linking_key) := as.character(!!sym(linking_key)))
}
n_asec_before_merge <- nrow(clean_data_asec)
merged_data_asec <- left_join(clean_data_asec, clean_data_basic, by = linking_key)
n_asec_after_merge <- nrow(merged_data_asec)
if(n_asec_before_merge != n_asec_after_merge) {
  warning("Row count changed during merge (from ", n_asec_before_merge, " to ", n_asec_after_merge, "). Check for duplicate linking keys if unexpected.")
}
if(var_uh_dscwk_b2 %in% names(merged_data_asec)) {
  n_matched <- sum(!is.na(merged_data_asec[[var_uh_dscwk_b2]]))
  n_total_asec <- nrow(merged_data_asec)
  print(paste("... Merge complete.", n_matched, "out of", n_total_asec, "ASEC records received a value for", var_uh_dscwk_b2, "from the basic monthly file."))
  if (n_matched < n_total_asec) {
    print(paste("...", n_total_asec - n_matched, "ASEC records did not match a record in the basic monthly file (or had NA UH_DSCWK_B2). Underemployment will be NA for these."))
  }
} else {
  warning(paste(var_uh_dscwk_b2, "column not found after merge. Underemployment calculation will fail."))
}
rm(clean_data_asec, clean_data_basic); gc()
merged_data_asec_unfiltered_age <- merged_data_asec
rm(merged_data_asec); gc() 
age_var <- "AGE"
print("... Merged ASEC+Basic data stored. Age filter will be applied after derived variable calculation.")

# ==== IIIB.3 CREATE SURVEY DESIGN ====
print("... Skipping survey design object creation (using weighted means directly).")

# ==== IIIB.4 LOAD INCOME BORDERS (External Borders) ====
# ... (Loading borders logic remains the same) ...
print("--- Loading External Income Borders ---")
if (!file.exists(borders_csv_file)) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: EXTERNAL Within-tercile borders file not found:", borders_csv_file)) }
borders_df <- readr::read_csv(borders_csv_file, show_col_types = FALSE)
print("... EXTERNAL Within-tercile borders loaded successfully.")
print("WARNING: Using income borders calculated from a different dataset/year.")

# ==== IIIB.5 LOAD MAIN TERCILE CUTOFFS (External Borders) ====
# ... (Loading cutoffs logic remains the same) ...
print("--- Loading External Main Tercile Cutoffs ---")
if (!file.exists(main_cutoffs_rds_file)) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: EXTERNAL Main tercile cutoffs file not found:", main_cutoffs_rds_file)) }
loaded_main_cutoffs <- readRDS(main_cutoffs_rds_file)
if (is.list(loaded_main_cutoffs) && all(c("main_cutoff1", "main_cutoff2") %in% names(loaded_main_cutoffs))) {
  main_cutoff1_val <- loaded_main_cutoffs$main_cutoff1; main_cutoff2_val <- loaded_main_cutoffs$main_cutoff2
  print(paste("... EXTERNAL Main cutoffs loaded successfully: Cutoff1 =", main_cutoff1_val, ", Cutoff2 =", main_cutoff2_val))
  print("WARNING: Using income cutoffs calculated from a different dataset/year.")
} else { stop("FATAL ERROR: Loaded main cutoffs object does not have the expected structure.") }

# ==== IIIB.6 ASSIGN INCOME GROUPS (Using ASEC HHINCOME & External Borders) ====
# ... (Assign income groups logic remains the same) ...
print("--- Assigning Income Groups ---")
if (!exists("merged_data_asec_unfiltered_age")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: 'merged_data_asec_unfiltered_age' object not found.") } 
if (!exists("borders_df")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: Borders data frame 'borders_df' not found.") }
income_variable_name <- "HHINCOME"
if (!income_variable_name %in% names(merged_data_asec_unfiltered_age)) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Income variable '", income_variable_name, "' not found in merged ASEC data.")) } 
weight_variable_name <- "ASECWTH" 
if (!weight_variable_name %in% names(merged_data_asec_unfiltered_age)) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Household weight variable", weight_variable_name, "needed for temp design not found.")) } 
data_for_design <- merged_data_asec_unfiltered_age %>% 
  filter(!is.na(!!sym(weight_variable_name)) & !!sym(weight_variable_name) > 0 & !is.na(!!sym(income_variable_name)))
if(nrow(data_for_design) < nrow(merged_data_asec_unfiltered_age)){ 
  warning("Removed ", format(nrow(merged_data_asec_unfiltered_age) - nrow(data_for_design), big.mark=","), " rows with missing/non-positive weights or missing income before creating temp design.")
}
if(nrow(data_for_design) == 0) { stop("FATAL ERROR: No rows remaining after filtering for weights/income for group assignment.")}
if (!exists("assign_income_groups")) {
  stop("assign_income_groups function not found. Check sourcing.")
}
temp_design_for_groups <- survey::svydesign(ids=~1, data=data_for_design, weights=as.formula(paste0("~", weight_variable_name)))
print(paste("... Calling assign_income_groups function with detail level:", fine_group_detail_level, "and income var:", income_variable_name))
print("... Applying EXTERNAL borders to ASEC income.")
temp_design_with_groups <- assign_income_groups(
  survey_obj = temp_design_for_groups,
  borders_df = borders_df,
  income_var_name = income_variable_name,
  detail_level = fine_group_detail_level,
  main_cutoff1 = main_cutoff1_val,
  main_cutoff2 = main_cutoff2_val
)
data_with_groups <- temp_design_with_groups$variables
new_cols <- c("income_tercile", "fine_income_group")
if (all(new_cols %in% names(data_with_groups))) {
  print("... Successfully added 'income_tercile' and 'fine_income_group' columns (based on external borders).")
} else {
  warning("Could not find expected new columns ('income_tercile', 'fine_income_group'). Check assign_income_groups function.")
}
if (age_var %in% names(merged_data_asec_unfiltered_age)) { 
  data_final_temp <- merged_data_asec_unfiltered_age %>% filter(.data[[age_var]] >= 18) 
  print(paste("... Applied filter:", age_var, ">= 18 to create adult dataset."))
} else {
  warning(paste("Age variable", age_var, "not found for filtering. Using unfiltered data for main analysis."))
  data_final_temp <- merged_data_asec_unfiltered_age 
}
merge_keys <- intersect(names(data_final_temp), names(data_with_groups))
merge_keys <- merge_keys[!(merge_keys %in% c("income_tercile", "fine_income_group"))]
group_data_to_merge <- data_with_groups %>% distinct(across(all_of(c(merge_keys, "income_tercile", "fine_income_group"))))
data_final <- left_join(data_final_temp,
                        group_data_to_merge,
                        by = merge_keys)
print("... Merged income group info onto filtered (adult) merged ASEC data.") 
rm(temp_design_for_groups, temp_design_with_groups, borders_df, loaded_main_cutoffs, data_for_design, group_data_to_merge, data_final_temp, data_with_groups, merged_data_asec_unfiltered_age); gc() 

# ==== IIIB.7 CALCULATE DERIVED VARIABLES (Corrected U-6 Components) ==== 
# ... (Derived variable calculation logic remains the same) ...
print("--- Calculating Derived Variables for CPS ASEC (Corrected U-6 Components) ---") 
if (!exists("data_final")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: 'data_final' object not found.") }
age_var <- "AGE"
educ_var <- "EDUC"
race_var <- "RACE"
hisp_var <- "HISPAN"
empstat_var <- "EMPSTAT"
whyptlwk_var <- "WHYPTLWK"
foodstmp_var <- "FOODSTMP"
diffany_var <- "DIFFANY"
diffmob_var <- "DIFFMOB"
diffrem_var <- "DIFFREM"
diffeye_var <- "DIFFEYE"
diffhear_var <- "DIFFHEAR"
diffcare_var <- "DIFFCARE"
hhincome_var <- "HHINCOME"
incwage_var <- "INCWAGE"
incbus_var <- "INCBUS"
incfarm_var <- "INCFARM"
inccapg_var <- "INCCAPG"
wkstat_var <- "WKSTAT"
uh_dscwk_b2_var <- "UH_DSCWK_B2" 
calculate_derived_vars_asec <- function(df) {
  required_vars_for_mutate <- c(age_var, educ_var, race_var, hisp_var, empstat_var, whyptlwk_var,
                                foodstmp_var, diffany_var, diffmob_var, diffrem_var, diffeye_var, diffhear_var, diffcare_var,
                                hhincome_var, incwage_var, incbus_var,
                                wkstat_var, uh_dscwk_b2_var, 
                                if(incfarm_var %in% names(df)) incfarm_var else NULL,
                                if(inccapg_var %in% names(df)) inccapg_var else NULL
  )
  required_vars_for_mutate <- Filter(Negate(is.null), required_vars_for_mutate)
  missing_vars <- required_vars_for_mutate[!required_vars_for_mutate %in% names(df)]
  if(length(missing_vars) > 0) {
    warning("The following variables needed for mutate are missing: ", paste(missing_vars, collapse=", "), ". Calculations using them will result in NA.")
  }
  df <- df %>% mutate(
    race_ethnicity = case_when(
      !is.na(.data[[hisp_var]]) & .data[[hisp_var]] != 0 ~ "Hispanic",
      !is.na(.data[[hisp_var]]) & .data[[hisp_var]] == 0 & .data[[race_var]] == 100 ~ "White, Non-Hispanic",
      !is.na(.data[[hisp_var]]) & .data[[hisp_var]] == 0 & .data[[race_var]] == 200 ~ "Black, Non-Hispanic",
      !is.na(.data[[hisp_var]]) & .data[[hisp_var]] == 0 & .data[[race_var]] == 651 ~ "Asian, Non-Hispanic",
      !is.na(.data[[hisp_var]]) & .data[[hisp_var]] == 0 & .data[[race_var]] == 300 ~ "AIAN, Non-Hispanic",
      !is.na(.data[[hisp_var]]) & .data[[hisp_var]] == 0 ~ "Other/Multi, Non-Hispanic",
      TRUE ~ NA_character_
    ),
    educ_level = case_when(
      .data[[educ_var]] %in% 2:71 ~ "Less than HS",
      .data[[educ_var]] == 73 ~ "High School Grad / GED",
      .data[[educ_var]] %in% c(80, 81, 90, 100) ~ "Some College",
      .data[[educ_var]] %in% c(91, 92) ~ "Associate's Degree",
      .data[[educ_var]] == 111 ~ "Bachelor's Degree",
      .data[[educ_var]] == 123 ~ "Master's Degree",
      .data[[educ_var]] %in% c(124, 125) ~ "Doctorate/Professional",
      TRUE ~ NA_character_
    ),
    educ_ba_plus = case_when(
      educ_level %in% c("Bachelor's Degree", "Master's Degree", "Doctorate/Professional") ~ "BA Degree or Higher",
      !is.na(educ_level) ~ "Less than BA Degree",
      TRUE ~ NA_character_
    ),
    educ_ba_plus_ind = if_else(educ_ba_plus == "BA Degree or Higher", 1, 0, missing = NA_real_),
    is_employed = if_else(.data[[empstat_var]] %in% c(1, 10, 12), 1, 0, missing = NA_real_),
    is_unemployed = if_else(.data[[empstat_var]] %in% c(20, 21, 22), 1, 0, missing = NA_real_),
    is_in_labor_force = if_else(.data[[empstat_var]] %in% c(1, 10, 12, 20, 21, 22), 1, 0, missing = NA_real_),
    is_part_time_economic = if (wkstat_var %in% names(.) && whyptlwk_var %in% names(.)) {
      if_else(
        .data[[wkstat_var]] %in% c(11, 12, 13, 14, 15) & 
          .data[[whyptlwk_var]] %in% c(1, 2, 3, 4),        
        1, 0, missing = 0 
      )
    } else { NA_real_ },
    is_marginally_attached = if (uh_dscwk_b2_var %in% names(.)) {
      if_else(
        .data[[uh_dscwk_b2_var]] %in% 1:2, 
        1, 0, missing = 0 
      )
    } else { NA_real_ }, 
    share_INCWAGE = if(incwage_var %in% names(.)) if_else(!is.na(.data[[hhincome_var]]) & .data[[hhincome_var]] > 0, .data[[incwage_var]] / .data[[hhincome_var]], NA_real_) else NA_real_,
    INCBUSFARM = (if(incbus_var %in% names(.)) coalesce(.data[[incbus_var]], 0) else 0) + (if(incfarm_var %in% names(.)) coalesce(.data[[incfarm_var]], 0) else 0),
    share_INCBUSFARM = if_else(!is.na(.data[[hhincome_var]]) & .data[[hhincome_var]] > 0, INCBUSFARM / .data[[hhincome_var]], NA_real_),
    share_INCCAPG = if(inccapg_var %in% names(.)) if_else(!is.na(.data[[hhincome_var]]) & .data[[hhincome_var]] > 0, .data[[inccapg_var]] / .data[[hhincome_var]], NA_real_) else NA_real_,
    has_snap = if_else(.data[[foodstmp_var]] == 2, 1, 0, missing = 0),
    has_disability_any = if_else(.data[[diffany_var]] == 2, 1, 0, missing = 0),
    has_disability_mob = if_else(.data[[diffmob_var]] == 2, 1, 0, missing = 0),
    has_disability_rem = if_else(.data[[diffrem_var]] == 2, 1, 0, missing = 0),
    has_disability_eye = if_else(.data[[diffeye_var]] == 2, 1, 0, missing = 0),
    has_disability_hear = if_else(.data[[diffhear_var]] == 2, 1, 0, missing = 0),
    has_disability_care = if_else(.data[[diffcare_var]] == 2, 1, 0, missing = 0),
    is_in_labor_force_25_65 = if_else(AGE >= 25 & AGE <= 65, is_in_labor_force, NA_integer_)
  ) 
  return(df)
}
data_final <- calculate_derived_vars_asec(data_final)
print("... Derived variables calculated (incl. U-6 components).")

# ==== IIIB.8 CALCULATE GROUPED SUMMARY STATISTICS (Corrected U-6 Rate) ==== 
# ... (Summary statistics calculation logic remains the same) ...
print("--- Calculating Grouped Summary Statistics for CPS ASEC (Corrected U-6 Rate) ---") 
if (!exists("data_final")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: data_final object not found.") }
grouping_vars_fine <- c("income_tercile", "fine_income_group")
if (!all(grouping_vars_fine %in% names(data_final))) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Fine grouping variables not found")) }
asecwth_var <- "ASECWTH"
asecwt_var <- "ASECWT"
if(!asecwth_var %in% names(data_final)) stop("ASECWTH not found.")
if(!asecwt_var %in% names(data_final)) stop("ASECWT not found.")
summary_stats_asec_main <- data_final %>%
  filter(!is.na(income_tercile) & !is.na(fine_income_group)) %>%
  group_by(income_tercile, fine_income_group, .drop = FALSE) %>%
  summarise(
    n_unweighted_persons = sum(!is.na(!!sym(asecwt_var)) & !!sym(asecwt_var) > 0),
    n_unweighted_households = n_distinct(SERIAL[!is.na(!!sym(asecwth_var)) & !!sym(asecwth_var) > 0]),
    avg_age_asec = weighted.mean(AGE, w = !!sym(asecwt_var), na.rm = TRUE),
    prop_labor_force_participation = weighted.mean(if_else(AGE >= 25 & AGE <= 65, is_in_labor_force, NA_real_), w = !!sym(asecwt_var), na.rm = TRUE),
    employment_rate = weighted.mean(if_else(AGE >= 25 & AGE <= 65, is_employed, NA_real_), w = !!sym(asecwt_var), na.rm = TRUE),
    unemployment_rate = sum(if_else(AGE >= 25, is_unemployed == 1, 0) * !!sym(asecwt_var), na.rm = TRUE) /
      sum(if_else(AGE >= 25, is_in_labor_force == 1, 0) * !!sym(asecwt_var), na.rm = TRUE), 
    u6_rate = {
      unemployed_wt = sum(if_else(AGE >= 25 & is_unemployed == 1, !!sym(asecwt_var), 0), na.rm = TRUE)
      ptecon_wt = sum(if_else(AGE >= 25 & is_part_time_economic == 1, !!sym(asecwt_var), 0), na.rm = TRUE)
      margatt_wt = sum(if_else(AGE >= 25 & is_marginally_attached == 1, !!sym(asecwt_var), 0), na.rm = TRUE)
      laborforce_wt = sum(if_else(AGE >= 25 & is_in_labor_force == 1, !!sym(asecwt_var), 0), na.rm = TRUE)
      u6_denominator_wt = laborforce_wt + margatt_wt
      u6_numerator_wt = unemployed_wt + ptecon_wt + margatt_wt
      if (u6_denominator_wt > 0) u6_numerator_wt / u6_denominator_wt else NA_real_
    }, 
    avg_share_INCWAGE = weighted.mean(share_INCWAGE, w = !!sym(asecwt_var), na.rm = TRUE),
    avg_share_INCBUSFARM = weighted.mean(share_INCBUSFARM, w = !!sym(asecwt_var), na.rm = TRUE),
    avg_share_INCCAPG = weighted.mean(share_INCCAPG, w = !!sym(asecwt_var), na.rm = TRUE),
    prop_diff_any = weighted.mean(has_disability_any, w = !!sym(asecwt_var), na.rm = TRUE),
    prop_diff_mob = weighted.mean(has_disability_mob, w = !!sym(asecwt_var), na.rm = TRUE),
    prop_diff_rem = weighted.mean(has_disability_rem, w = !!sym(asecwt_var), na.rm = TRUE),
    prop_diff_eye = weighted.mean(has_disability_eye, w = !!sym(asecwt_var), na.rm = TRUE),
    prop_diff_hear = weighted.mean(has_disability_hear, w = !!sym(asecwt_var), na.rm = TRUE),
    prop_diff_care = weighted.mean(has_disability_care, w = !!sym(asecwt_var), na.rm = TRUE),
    avg_hhincome_asec = weighted.mean(HHINCOME, w = !!sym(asecwth_var), na.rm = TRUE),
    prop_snap_receipt = weighted.mean(has_snap, w = !!sym(asecwth_var), na.rm = TRUE),
    .groups = "drop"
  ) %>%
  mutate(unemployment_rate = if_else(is.infinite(unemployment_rate) | is.nan(unemployment_rate), NA_real_, unemployment_rate))
if ("educ_level" %in% names(data_final)) {
  print("... Calculating Education Distribution Proportions...")
  educ_counts <- data_final %>%
    filter(!is.na(income_tercile) & !is.na(fine_income_group) & AGE >= 25 & !is.na(educ_level)) %>%
    group_by(income_tercile, fine_income_group, educ_level) %>%
    summarise(weighted_pop_educ = sum(!!sym(asecwt_var), na.rm = TRUE), .groups = "drop")
  educ_total_pop <- educ_counts %>%
    group_by(income_tercile, fine_income_group) %>%
    summarise(total_weighted_pop_group_educ = sum(weighted_pop_educ, na.rm = TRUE), .groups = "drop") %>%
    filter(total_weighted_pop_group_educ > 0) 
  summary_stats_asec_educ_dist <- educ_counts %>%
    inner_join(educ_total_pop, by = c("income_tercile", "fine_income_group")) %>%
    mutate(proportion = weighted_pop_educ / total_weighted_pop_group_educ) %>%
    select(income_tercile, fine_income_group, educ_level, proportion) %>%
    tidyr::pivot_wider(
      names_from = educ_level,
      values_from = proportion,
      names_glue = "prop_educ_level_{make.names(educ_level)}", 
      values_fill = 0 
    )
  print("... Education distribution calculated and reshaped.")
  summary_stats_asec_main <- left_join(summary_stats_asec_main,
                                       summary_stats_asec_educ_dist,
                                       by = c("income_tercile", "fine_income_group"))
  print("... Education proportions joined to main summary.")
  rm(educ_counts, educ_total_pop, summary_stats_asec_educ_dist) 
} else {
  warning("Could not calculate education distribution because 'educ_level' column not found.")
}
print("... Weighted summary statistics calculated using dplyr.")

# ==== IIIB.8.5 Calculate Race-Specific Summary Statistics ====
print("--- Calculating Race-Specific Summary Statistics (Placeholder) ---")

# ==== IIIB.9 SAVE SUMMARY RESULTS ====
print("--- Saving CPS ASEC Summary Results ---")
if (!exists("summary_output_dir")) stop("FATAL ERROR: summary_output_dir not defined.")
if (!dir.exists(summary_output_dir)) dir.create(summary_output_dir, recursive = TRUE)

# MODIFIED FILENAME FOR IV2 COMPATIBILITY
output_rds_filename_asec <- file.path(summary_output_dir, paste0("cps_asec_", cps_asec_sample_id, "_summary_stats_G_groups.rds"))

if (exists("summary_stats_asec_main") && !is.null(summary_stats_asec_main) && nrow(summary_stats_asec_main) > 0) {
  print(paste("... Saving ASEC main summary results to:", output_rds_filename_asec))
  tryCatch({ saveRDS(summary_stats_asec_main, file = output_rds_filename_asec) ; print("... Results saved successfully.") },
           error = function(e) { warning("Error saving summary results to RDS file: ", e$message) })
} else { warning("Summary statistics object 'summary_stats_asec_main' is empty or does not exist. Skipping save.") }

# ==== IIIB.10 CLEAN UP MEMORY ====
print("--- Cleaning up CPS ASEC Memory ---")
objects_to_remove_asec <- c(
  "data_final", "summary_stats_asec_main",
  "merged_data_asec_unfiltered_age", 
  "asec_load_result", "basic_load_result", 
  "loaded_main_cutoffs"
)
objects_exist_asec <- objects_to_remove_asec[sapply(objects_to_remove_asec, exists)]
if(length(objects_exist_asec) > 0) {
  rm(list = objects_exist_asec)
  print(paste("... Removed objects:", paste(objects_exist_asec, collapse=", ")))
} else {
  print("... No large objects specified for removal found.")
}
gc()
print("==== Memory cleanup attempted. Script IIIB (Filename Corrected) finished. ====")

# ==== 0. ABOUT ====
# Project: 1/3 Country Project Visualizations
# Script:  IIIC_Process_NHIS_Revised_V6_CodebookFix.R 
# Version: 6.0.1 # <<< MODIFIED: Output filename changed for IV2 compatibility
# Author:  Maxwell Goshert / Janica Murphy / Gemini
# Date:    2025-04-21 19:25:00 (MST) # <<< Updated Timestamp
# Location: Phoenix, Arizona, United States # <<< Current Location

# Purpose:
# This R script processes the National Health Interview Survey (NHIS) data
# (sample ih2019) as part of Stage 3.
# It uses the continuous FAMTOTINC variable, adjusting it for inflation.
# For values BELOW the top code ($220k in 2019$), it uses the adjusted value directly.
# For values AT the top code, it imputes a value by drawing from the upper tail
# of the March 2023 ASEC HHINCOME distribution (donor imputation for top code).
# The final variable is HHINCOME_IMP_2023.
# It loads NHIS & ASEC data, cleans them using codes verified from codebooks,
# performs the top-code imputation, assigns income groups based on HHINCOME_IMP_2023
# and pre-calculated EXTERNAL borders (from File I), calculates derived variables
# (demographics, health), calculates summary statistics (weighted means/proportions)
# according to the Master Viz & Data Plan v1, saves the results, and cleans up memory.
# USER SHOULD STILL DOUBLE-CHECK VARIABLE NAME DEFAULTS IF NEEDED.
# NOTE: This is File IIIC of V+ (Dataset Processing - NHIS with Top Code Imputation)

# Workflow Steps Covered (Sections in this script):
# IIIC.0: Setup & Parameters
# IIIC.1: Get Data (Load NHIS ih2019 & ASEC cps2023_03s, Codebook Generation)
# IIIC.2: Initial Clean & Prep (Separate for NHIS & ASEC - Updated Codes)
# IIIC.2.5: Adjust Income Variables for Inflation
# IIIC.3: Prepare Donor Pool from ASEC (Specifically for Top Code)
# IIIC.4: Perform Imputation (Only for Top-Coded NHIS Values) -> Creates HHINCOME_IMP_2023
# IIIC.5: Load Income Borders (External Borders from File I)
# IIIC.6: Assign Income Groups (Using Imputed Income & External Borders)
# IIIC.7: Calculate Derived Variables (Master Plan Specs - Updated Codes/Logic)
# IIIC.8: Calculate Grouped Summary Statistics (Master Plan Specs - Weighted Means/Proportions)
# IIIC.9: Save Summary Table
# IIIC.10: Clean Up Memory

# ==== IIIC.0 SETUP & PARAMETERS ====

# ==== IIIC.1 GET DATA (Load NHIS ih2019 & ASEC cps2023_03s, Codebook Generation) ====
download_and_load_ipums <- function(sample_id, variables, collection, description_suffix, output_parent_dir) {
  extract_description <- paste("1/3 Country Project -", description_suffix, "-", Sys.Date())
  print(paste("--- Processing Sample:", sample_id, "(Collection:", collection, ") ---"))
  if (!is.character(variables) || length(variables) == 0) { stop("FATAL ERROR: 'variables' must be a non-empty character vector.") }
  variables <- unique(variables)
  extract_def <- define_extract_micro(
    collection = collection, samples = sample_id, variables = variables, description = extract_description
  )
  print("... IPUMS extract definition created.")
  submitted_extract <- tryCatch({ submit_extract(extract_def) },
                                error = function(e) { message("IPUMS API Error during submission for ", sample_id, ":"); message(e$message); stop("Extract submission failed.") })
  extract_number <- submitted_extract$number
  print(paste("... Extract submitted. Number:", extract_number))
  print("... Waiting for extract preparation...")
  Sys.sleep(10)
  downloadable_extract <- wait_for_extract(submitted_extract)
  print("... Extract is ready for download.")
  download_subdir_name <- paste0(toupper(collection), "_Extract_", extract_number, "_", Sys.Date())
  download_dir_specific <- file.path(output_parent_dir, download_subdir_name) 
  if (!dir.exists(download_dir_specific)) {
    dir.create(download_dir_specific, recursive = TRUE)
    print(paste("... Created download subdirectory:", download_dir_specific))
  } else {
    print(paste("... Download subdirectory already exists:", download_dir_specific))
  }
  print(paste("... Downloading extract files to:", download_dir_specific))
  downloaded_files <- tryCatch({ download_extract(downloadable_extract, download_dir = download_dir_specific, overwrite = TRUE) },
                               error = function(e) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR during file download:", e$message)) })
  print("... Files downloaded.")
  ddi_file_path <- downloaded_files[grep("\\.xml$", downloaded_files)]
  if (length(ddi_file_path) == 0) {
    ddi_file_path <- list.files(path = download_dir_specific, pattern = paste0(collection, "_", sprintf("%05d", extract_number), "\\.xml$"), full.names = TRUE)
    if (length(ddi_file_path) == 0) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Could not find downloaded DDI (.xml) file in:", download_dir_specific)) }
  }
  ddi_file_path <- ddi_file_path[1]
  print(paste("... DDI file path identified:", ddi_file_path))
  codebook_output_filename <- file.path(download_dir_specific, paste0(gsub("\\.xml$", "", basename(ddi_file_path)), "_codebook.txt"))
  if(exists("generate_codebook_from_ddi") && is.function(generate_codebook_from_ddi)){
    print("... Generating text codebook...")
    generate_codebook_from_ddi(ddi_path = ddi_file_path, vars_list = variables, output_txt_path = codebook_output_filename)
  } else { warning("generate_codebook_from_ddi function not found. Skipping codebook generation.") }
  raw_data <- read_ipums_micro(ddi = ddi_file_path, verbose = FALSE)
  print(paste("...", sample_id, "Data loaded successfully."))
  print(paste("Dimensions of raw data:", paste(dim(raw_data), collapse = " x ")))
  return(list(data = raw_data, ddi = ddi_file_path))
}
nhis_load_result <- download_and_load_ipums(
  sample_id = nhis_sample_id,
  variables = nhis_variables_needed,
  collection = ipums_collection_nhis,
  description_suffix = paste("NHIS", nhis_sample_id),
  output_parent_dir = extract_download_parent_dir 
)
raw_data_nhis <- nhis_load_result$data
ddi_nhis <- nhis_load_result$ddi
asec_load_result <- download_and_load_ipums(
  sample_id = cps_asec_sample_id,
  variables = asec_variables_needed,
  collection = ipums_collection_asec,
  description_suffix = paste("ASEC", cps_asec_sample_id, "Donor Pool"),
  output_parent_dir = extract_download_parent_dir 
)
raw_data_asec <- asec_load_result$data
ddi_asec <- asec_load_result$ddi

print("--- Cleaning NHIS Data (Codes Updated based on Codebook Review) ---")
if (!exists("raw_data_nhis")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: raw_data_nhis object not found.") }
clean_data_nhis <- raw_data_nhis
rm(raw_data_nhis); gc()

  clean_data_nhis <- clean_data_nhis %>% mutate(!!sym(var_faminc_nhis) := if_else(!!sym(var_faminc_nhis) %in% faminc_codes_to_drop, NA_real_, as.numeric(!!sym(var_faminc_nhis))))
} else { warning(paste(var_faminc_nhis, "not found.")) }
race_codes_to_na <- c(997, 998, 999) 
if (var_race_nhis %in% names(clean_data_nhis)) {
  print(paste("... Cleaning", var_race_nhis, "- Setting codes", paste(race_codes_to_na, collapse=", "), "to NA."))
  clean_data_nhis <- clean_data_nhis %>% mutate(!!sym(var_race_nhis) := if_else(!!sym(var_race_nhis) %in% race_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(!!sym(var_race_nhis))))
} else { warning(paste("Variable '", var_race_nhis, "' not found.", sep="")) }

  print(paste("... Cleaning", var_hispan_nhis, "- Setting codes", paste(hispan_codes_to_na, collapse=", "), "to NA."))
  clean_data_nhis <- clean_data_nhis %>% mutate(!!sym(var_hispan_nhis) := if_else(!!sym(var_hispan_nhis) %in% hispan_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(!!sym(var_hispan_nhis))))
} else { warning(paste("Variable '", var_hispan_nhis,"' not found.", sep="")) }

  print(paste("... Cleaning", var_health_nhis, "- Setting codes", paste(health_codes_to_na, collapse=", "), "to NA."))
  clean_data_nhis <- clean_data_nhis %>% mutate(!!sym(var_health_nhis) := if_else(!!sym(var_health_nhis) %in% health_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(!!sym(var_health_nhis))))
} else { warning(paste(var_health_nhis, "not found.")) }

if (var_cheartdiev_nhis %in% names(clean_data_nhis)) {
  print(paste("... Cleaning", var_cheartdiev_nhis, "- Setting codes", paste(cheartdiev_codes_to_na, collapse=", "), "to NA."))
  clean_data_nhis <- clean_data_nhis %>% mutate(!!sym(var_cheartdiev_nhis) := if_else(!!sym(var_cheartdiev_nhis) %in% cheartdiev_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(!!sym(var_cheartdiev_nhis))))
} else { warning(paste(var_cheartdiev_nhis, "not found.")) }

  print(paste("... Cleaning", var_bmicat_nhis, "- Setting codes", paste(bmicat_codes_to_na, collapse=", "), "to NA."))
  clean_data_nhis <- clean_data_nhis %>% mutate(!!sym(var_bmicat_nhis) := if_else(!!sym(var_bmicat_nhis) %in% bmicat_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(!!sym(var_bmicat_nhis))))

  print(paste("... Cleaning", var_smokestat_nhis, "- Setting codes", paste(smokestat_codes_to_na, collapse=", "), "to NA."))
  clean_data_nhis <- clean_data_nhis %>% mutate(!!sym(var_smokestat_nhis) := if_else(!!sym(var_smokestat_nhis) %in% smokestat_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(!!sym(var_smokestat_nhis))))
} else { warning(paste(var_smokestat_nhis, "not found.")) }
cigsday_codes_to_na <- c(0, 97, 98, 99) 
if (var_cigsday1_nhis %in% names(clean_data_nhis)) {
  print(paste("... Cleaning", var_cigsday1_nhis))
  clean_data_nhis <- clean_data_nhis %>% mutate(!!sym(var_cigsday1_nhis) := if_else(!!sym(var_cigsday1_nhis) %in% cigsday_codes_to_na, NA_real_, as.numeric(!!sym(var_cigsday1_nhis))))
} else { warning(paste(var_cigsday1_nhis, "not found.")) }
if (var_cigsday2_nhis %in% names(clean_data_nhis)) {
  print(paste("... Cleaning", var_cigsday2_nhis))
  clean_data_nhis <- clean_data_nhis %>% mutate(!!sym(var_cigsday2_nhis) := if_else(!!sym(var_cigsday2_nhis) %in% cigsday_codes_to_na, NA_real_, as.numeric(!!sym(var_cigsday2_nhis))))
} else { warning(paste(var_cigsday2_nhis, "not found.")) }
age_codes_to_na <- c(997, 998, 999) 
if (var_age_nhis %in% names(clean_data_nhis)) {
  print(paste("... Cleaning", var_age_nhis, "- Setting codes", paste(age_codes_to_na, collapse=", "), "to NA."))
  clean_data_nhis <- clean_data_nhis %>% mutate(!!sym(var_age_nhis) := if_else(!!sym(var_age_nhis) %in% age_codes_to_na, NA_real_, as.numeric(!!sym(var_age_nhis))))
} else { warning(paste("Age variable '", var_age_nhis,"' not found.", sep="")) }
sex_codes_to_na <- c(7, 8, 9) 
if (var_sex_nhis %in% names(clean_data_nhis)) {
  print(paste("... Cleaning", var_sex_nhis, "- Setting codes", paste(sex_codes_to_na, collapse=", "), "to NA."))
  clean_data_nhis <- clean_data_nhis %>% mutate(!!sym(var_sex_nhis) := if_else(!!sym(var_sex_nhis) %in% sex_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(!!sym(var_sex_nhis))))
} else { warning(paste("Sex variable '", var_sex_nhis,"' not found.", sep="")) }
if (var_sampweight_nhis %in% names(clean_data_nhis)) {
  print(paste("... Ensuring", var_sampweight_nhis, "is numeric."))
  clean_data_nhis <- clean_data_nhis %>% mutate(!!sym(var_sampweight_nhis) := as.numeric(!!sym(var_sampweight_nhis)))
  if (any(clean_data_nhis[[var_sampweight_nhis]] <= 0, na.rm = TRUE)) { warning(paste("Non-positive values found in weight variable:", var_sampweight_nhis)) }
} else { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Weight variable", var_sampweight_nhis, "not found.")) }
print("... NHIS Cleaning complete using codes updated from codebook review.")
print("--- Cleaning ASEC Donor Pool Data ---")
if (!exists("raw_data_asec")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: raw_data_asec object not found.") }
clean_data_asec <- raw_data_asec
rm(raw_data_asec); gc()
var_hhincome_asec <- "HHINCOME"
var_asecwt <- "ASECWT"
income_codes_to_na_asec <- c(99999999) 
if (var_hhincome_asec %in% names(clean_data_asec)) {
  print(paste("... Cleaning ASEC", var_hhincome_asec, "- Setting code", paste(income_codes_to_na_asec, collapse=", "), "to NA."))
  clean_data_asec <- clean_data_asec %>% mutate(!!sym(var_hhincome_asec) := if_else(!!sym(var_hhincome_asec) %in% income_codes_to_na_asec, NA_real_, as.numeric(!!sym(var_hhincome_asec))))
} else { stop("FATAL ERROR: HHINCOME variable not found in ASEC data.") }
if (var_asecwt %in% names(clean_data_asec)) {
  print(paste("... Ensuring ASEC", var_asecwt, "is numeric."))
  clean_data_asec <- clean_data_asec %>% mutate(!!sym(var_asecwt) := as.numeric(!!sym(var_asecwt)))
  if (any(clean_data_asec[[var_asecwt]] <= 0, na.rm = TRUE)) { warning(paste("Non-positive values found in weight variable:", var_asecwt)) }
} else { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: ASEC Weight variable", var_asecwt, "not found.")) }
clean_data_asec <- clean_data_asec %>%
  select(all_of(c(var_hhincome_asec, var_asecwt))) %>% 
  filter(!is.na(!!sym(var_asecwt)) & !!sym(var_asecwt) > 0 & !is.na(!!sym(var_hhincome_asec)))
print("... ASEC Donor Pool data cleaned and filtered.")

# ==== IIIC.2.5 Adjust Income Variables for Inflation ====
# ... (Inflation adjustment logic remains the same) ...
print(paste("--- Adjusting Income Variables to", REFERENCE_YEAR, "Dollars ---"))
if (var_faminc_nhis %in% names(clean_data_nhis)) {
  clean_data_nhis <- clean_data_nhis %>%
    mutate(FAMTOTINC_ADJ = !!sym(var_faminc_nhis) * inflation_factor_nhis)
  print("... Created inflation-adjusted NHIS variable 'FAMTOTINC_ADJ'.")
  nhis_famtotinc_top_code_adjusted <- nhis_famtotinc_top_code_nominal * inflation_factor_nhis
} else {
  warning("NHIS FAMTOTINC not found, skipping its inflation adjustment.")
}
if (var_hhincome_asec %in% names(clean_data_asec)) {
  clean_data_asec <- clean_data_asec %>%
    mutate(HHINCOME_ADJ = !!sym(var_hhincome_asec) * inflation_factor_asec)
  print("... Created inflation-adjusted ASEC variable 'HHINCOME_ADJ'.")
} else {
  stop("ASEC HHINCOME not found, cannot proceed.")
}

# ==== IIIC.3 Prepare Donor Pool from ASEC (Specifically for Top Code) ====
# ... (Donor pool preparation logic remains the same) ...
print("--- Preparing Top-Code Imputation Pool from Adjusted ASEC Data ---")
if (!"HHINCOME_ADJ" %in% names(clean_data_asec)) { stop("Adjusted ASEC income HHINCOME_ADJ not found.")}
if (!var_asecwt %in% names(clean_data_asec)) { stop(paste("ASEC weight", var_asecwt,"not found."))}
top_code_pool_asec <- clean_data_asec %>%
  filter(HHINCOME_ADJ >= nhis_famtotinc_top_code_adjusted)
if (nrow(top_code_pool_asec) > 0 && sum(top_code_pool_asec[[var_asecwt]]) > 0) {
  top_code_pool_values <- top_code_pool_asec %>% select(HHINCOME_ADJ, !!sym(var_asecwt))
  print(paste("... Created ASEC donor pool for top-coded values. Pool size:", nrow(top_code_pool_values)))
} else {
  top_code_pool_values <- NULL
  warning(paste("No ASEC records found (or zero total weight) at or above the adjusted NHIS top code threshold of", round(nhis_famtotinc_top_code_adjusted), ". Imputation for top-coded NHIS values will result in NA."))
}
rm(clean_data_asec, top_code_pool_asec); gc() 

# ==== IIIC.4 Perform Imputation (Only for Top-Coded NHIS Values) -> Creates HHINCOME_IMP_2023 ====
# ... (Imputation logic remains the same) ...
print("--- Performing Imputation for Top-Coded NHIS Values ---")
if (!"FAMTOTINC_ADJ" %in% names(clean_data_nhis)) { stop("Adjusted NHIS income FAMTOTINC_ADJ not found.")}
nhis_is_top_coded <- !is.na(clean_data_nhis[[var_faminc_nhis]]) & clean_data_nhis[[var_faminc_nhis]] == nhis_famtotinc_top_code_nominal
n_top_coded <- sum(nhis_is_top_coded)
print(paste("... Number of NHIS individuals identified at the top code (", nhis_famtotinc_top_code_nominal, "):", n_top_coded))
imputed_values_for_top_code <- rep(NA_real_, nrow(clean_data_nhis)) 
if (n_top_coded > 0) {
  if (!is.null(top_code_pool_values) && nrow(top_code_pool_values) > 0) {
    print("... Imputing top-coded values using weighted random draw from ASEC top-code pool...")
    imputed_values_for_top_code[nhis_is_top_coded] <- sample(
      x = top_code_pool_values$HHINCOME_ADJ,
      size = n_top_coded,
      replace = TRUE,
      prob = top_code_pool_values[[var_asecwt]]
    )
    n_imputed_success <- sum(!is.na(imputed_values_for_top_code))
    print(paste("... Successfully imputed values for", n_imputed_success, "top-coded individuals."))
    if(n_imputed_success < n_top_coded) {
      warning("... Could not impute values for ", n_top_coded - n_imputed_success, " top-coded individuals (likely due to issues in sampling). They will have NA imputed income.")
    }
  } else {
    warning("... Skipping imputation for top-coded values because ASEC donor pool was empty or had zero weight.")
  }
} else {
  print("... No top-coded individuals found, skipping imputation step.")
}
clean_data_nhis <- clean_data_nhis %>%
  mutate(
    HHINCOME_IMP_2023 = if_else(
      condition = nhis_is_top_coded,
      true = imputed_values_for_top_code, 
      false = FAMTOTINC_ADJ 
    )
  )
n_final_imputed_na <- sum(is.na(clean_data_nhis$HHINCOME_IMP_2023))
print(paste("... Created final 'HHINCOME_IMP_2023' variable. Total NAs:", n_final_imputed_na))
if (n_final_imputed_na > sum(is.na(clean_data_nhis$FAMTOTINC_ADJ)) ) {
  print("... Note: Increase in NAs likely due to failed imputation for some top-coded individuals.")
}
rm(top_code_pool_values); gc()
n_before_filter <- nrow(clean_data_nhis)
clean_data_nhis <- clean_data_nhis %>% filter(!is.na(HHINCOME_IMP_2023))
n_after_filter <- nrow(clean_data_nhis)
if (n_after_filter < n_before_filter) {
  print(paste("... Removed", n_before_filter - n_after_filter, "records with NA final imputed income before grouping."))
}
if (n_after_filter == 0) { stop("FATAL ERROR: Zero rows remaining after filtering out failed imputations/NA income.")}

# ==== IIIC.5 LOAD INCOME BORDERS (External Borders from File I) ====
# ... (Loading borders logic remains the same) ...
print("--- Loading External Income Borders ---")
if (!file.exists(borders_csv_file)) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: EXTERNAL Within-tercile borders file not found:", borders_csv_file)) }
borders_df <- readr::read_csv(borders_csv_file, show_col_types = FALSE)
print("... EXTERNAL Within-tercile borders loaded successfully.")
print("WARNING: Using income borders calculated from a different dataset/year (File I).")

# ==== IIIC.5.5 LOAD MAIN TERCILE CUTOFFS (External Borders from File I) ====
# ... (Loading cutoffs logic remains the same) ...
print("--- Loading External Main Tercile Cutoffs ---")
if (!file.exists(main_cutoffs_rds_file)) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: EXTERNAL Main tercile cutoffs file not found:", main_cutoffs_rds_file)) }
loaded_main_cutoffs <- readRDS(main_cutoffs_rds_file)
if (is.list(loaded_main_cutoffs) && all(c("main_cutoff1", "main_cutoff2") %in% names(loaded_main_cutoffs))) {
  main_cutoff1_val <- loaded_main_cutoffs$main_cutoff1
  main_cutoff2_val <- loaded_main_cutoffs$main_cutoff2
  print(paste("... EXTERNAL Main cutoffs loaded successfully: Cutoff1 =", format(round(main_cutoff1_val), big.mark=","), ", Cutoff2 =", format(round(main_cutoff2_val), big.mark=",")))
  print("WARNING: Using income cutoffs calculated from a different dataset/year (File I).")
} else { stop("FATAL ERROR: Loaded main cutoffs object does not have the expected structure.") }

# ==== IIIC.6 ASSIGN INCOME GROUPS (Using Imputed Income & External Borders) ====
# ... (Assign income groups logic remains the same) ...
print("--- Assigning Income Groups using Imputed Income ---")
if (!exists("clean_data_nhis")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: 'clean_data_nhis' object not found.") }
if (!exists("borders_df")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: Borders data frame 'borders_df' not found.") }
income_variable_final <- "HHINCOME_IMP_2023" 
if (!income_variable_final %in% names(clean_data_nhis)) {
  stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Final imputed income variable '", income_variable_final, "' not found."))
}
if (!var_sampweight_nhis %in% names(clean_data_nhis)) {
  stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Weight variable '", var_sampweight_nhis, "' not found."))
}
data_for_design <- clean_data_nhis %>% filter(!is.na(!!sym(var_sampweight_nhis)) & !!sym(var_sampweight_nhis) > 0)
if(nrow(data_for_design) < nrow(clean_data_nhis)){
  warning("Filtered out rows with NA/non-positive weights before creating temp design for group assignment.")
}
if(nrow(data_for_design) == 0){ stop("FATAL ERROR: No rows with valid weights remaining for group assignment.")}
temp_design_for_groups_nhis <- survey::svydesign(ids=~1, data=data_for_design, weights=as.formula(paste0("~", var_sampweight_nhis)))
print(paste("... Calling assign_income_groups function with detail level:", fine_group_detail_level, "and income var:", income_variable_final))
print("... Applying EXTERNAL borders to imputed NHIS income.")
if (!exists("assign_income_groups")) {
  stop("assign_income_groups function not found. Check sourcing of II_Shared_Functions.R.")
}
temp_design_with_groups_nhis <- assign_income_groups(
  survey_obj = temp_design_for_groups_nhis,
  borders_df = borders_df,
  income_var_name = income_variable_final, 
  detail_level = fine_group_detail_level,
  main_cutoff1 = main_cutoff1_val,
  main_cutoff2 = main_cutoff2_val
)
data_with_groups <- temp_design_with_groups_nhis$variables
new_cols <- c("income_tercile", "fine_income_group")
if (all(new_cols %in% names(data_with_groups))) {
  print("... Successfully added 'income_tercile' and 'fine_income_group' columns.")
} else {
  warning("Could not find expected new columns ('income_tercile', 'fine_income_group'). Check assign_income_groups function.")
}
rm(temp_design_for_groups_nhis, temp_design_with_groups_nhis, borders_df, loaded_main_cutoffs, data_for_design, clean_data_nhis); gc()

# ==== IIIC.7 CALCULATE DERIVED VARIABLES (Master Plan Specs - Updated Codes/Logic) ====
# ... (Derived variable calculation logic remains the same) ...
print("--- Calculating Derived Variables for NHIS (Master Plan - Updated Codes/Logic) ---")
if (!exists("data_with_groups")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: 'data_with_groups' data frame not found.") }
data_final <- data_with_groups 
rm(data_with_groups); gc()
var_age <- "AGE"
var_sex <- "SEX"
var_race <- "RACENEW"
var_hispan <- "HISPYN"
var_health <- "HEALTH"
var_cheartdiev <- "CHEARTDIEV"
var_bmicat <- "BMICAT"
var_smokestat <- "SMOKESTATUS2"
var_cigsday1 <- "CIGSDAY1"
var_cigsday2 <- "CIGSDAY2"
var_hhincome_imp <- "HHINCOME_IMP_2023" 
required_vars_derived <- c(var_age, var_sex, var_race, var_hispan, var_health, var_cheartdiev, var_bmicat, var_smokestat, var_cigsday1, var_cigsday2, var_hhincome_imp)
missing_vars <- required_vars_derived[!required_vars_derived %in% names(data_final)]
if(length(missing_vars) > 0) {
  stop("FATAL ERROR: The following required variables for derived calculations are missing from data_final: ", paste(missing_vars, collapse=", "), ". Verify names and earlier steps.")
}
data_final <- data_final %>% mutate(
  race_ethnicity = case_when(
    !is.na(!!sym(var_hispan)) & !!sym(var_hispan) == 2 ~ "Hispanic", 
    !is.na(!!sym(var_hispan)) & !!sym(var_hispan) == 1 & !!sym(var_race) == 100 ~ "White, Non-Hispanic", 
    !is.na(!!sym(var_hispan)) & !!sym(var_hispan) == 1 & !!sym(var_race) == 200 ~ "Black, Non-Hispanic", 
    !is.na(!!sym(var_hispan)) & !!sym(var_hispan) == 1 & !!sym(var_race) == 400 ~ "Asian, Non-Hispanic", 
    !is.na(!!sym(var_hispan)) & !!sym(var_hispan) == 1 & !!sym(var_race) == 300 ~ "AIAN, Non-Hispanic", 
    !is.na(!!sym(var_hispan)) & !!sym(var_hispan) == 1 & !!sym(var_race) == 510 ~ "Other/Multi, Non-Hispanic (510)", 
    !is.na(!!sym(var_hispan)) & !!sym(var_hispan) == 1 ~ "Other/Multi, Non-Hispanic", 
    TRUE ~ NA_character_
  ),
  health_score = case_when(
    !!sym(var_health) == 1 ~ 5, 
    !!sym(var_health) == 2 ~ 4, 
    !!sym(var_health) == 3 ~ 3, 
    !!sym(var_health) == 4 ~ 2, 
    !!sym(var_health) == 5 ~ 1, 
    TRUE ~ NA_real_ 
  ),
  has_heart_disease = case_when(
    !!sym(var_cheartdiev) == 2 ~ 1, 
    !!sym(var_cheartdiev) == 1 ~ 0, 
    TRUE ~ NA_real_ 
  ),
  is_obese = case_when(
    !!sym(var_bmicat) == 4 ~ 1, 
    !is.na(!!sym(var_bmicat)) ~ 0, 
    TRUE ~ NA_real_ 
  ),
  is_current_smoker = case_when(
    !!sym(var_smokestat) %in% c(10, 11, 12) ~ 1, 
    !is.na(!!sym(var_smokestat)) ~ 0, 
    TRUE ~ NA_real_ 
  ),
  cigs_per_day_all_smokers = case_when(
    is_current_smoker == 1 & !!sym(var_smokestat) == 11 ~ !!sym(var_cigsday1),
    is_current_smoker == 1 & !!sym(var_smokestat) == 12 & var_cigsday2 %in% names(.) ~ !!sym(var_cigsday2),
    is_current_smoker == 1 ~ NA_real_,
    TRUE ~ NA_real_
  )
)
print("... Derived variables calculated using codes updated from codebook review.")

# ==== IIIC.8 CALCULATE GROUPED SUMMARY STATISTICS (Master Plan Specs - Weighted Means/Proportions) ====
# ... (Summary statistics calculation logic remains the same) ...
print("--- Calculating Grouped Summary Statistics for NHIS (Master Plan) ---")
if (!exists("data_final")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: data_final object not found.") }
grouping_vars_fine <- c("income_tercile", "fine_income_group")
if (!all(grouping_vars_fine %in% names(data_final))) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Fine grouping variables not found")) }
var_sampweight <- "SAMPWEIGHT" 
if(!var_sampweight %in% names(data_final)) stop(paste("Weight variable", var_sampweight, "not found in data_final."))
print("... Calculating weighted means/proportions grouped by External Main Tercile and Fine Group...")
summary_stats_nhis <- data_final %>%
  filter(!is.na(income_tercile) & !is.na(fine_income_group)) %>%
  group_by(income_tercile, fine_income_group, .drop = FALSE) %>%
  summarise(
    n_unweighted_persons = n(),
    n_weighted_persons = sum(!!sym(var_sampweight), na.rm = TRUE),
    avg_hhincome_imp_2023 = weighted.mean(HHINCOME_IMP_2023, w = !!sym(var_sampweight), na.rm = TRUE),
    avg_health_score = weighted.mean(health_score, w = !!sym(var_sampweight), na.rm = TRUE),
    prop_heart_disease = weighted.mean(has_heart_disease, w = !!sym(var_sampweight), na.rm = TRUE),
    prop_obese = weighted.mean(is_obese, w = !!sym(var_sampweight), na.rm = TRUE),
    prop_current_smoker = weighted.mean(is_current_smoker, w = !!sym(var_sampweight), na.rm = TRUE),
    avg_cigs_per_day_all_smokers = {
      current_group_data <- cur_data()
      smokers_with_data <- !is.na(current_group_data$is_current_smoker) &
        current_group_data$is_current_smoker == 1 &
        !is.na(current_group_data$cigs_per_day_all_smokers)
      weights_subset <- current_group_data[[var_sampweight]][smokers_with_data]
      values_subset <- current_group_data$cigs_per_day_all_smokers[smokers_with_data]
      if(sum(smokers_with_data, na.rm = TRUE) == 0 || sum(weights_subset, na.rm = TRUE) <= 0) {
        NA_real_
      } else {
        weighted.mean(values_subset, w = weights_subset, na.rm = TRUE)
      }
    },
    .groups = "drop" 
  )
print("... Weighted summary statistics calculated using dplyr.")
print("First few rows of final summary_stats_nhis:")
print(head(summary_stats_nhis))
print("Structure of final summary_stats_nhis:")
str(summary_stats_nhis)
attr(summary_stats_nhis, "analysis_note") <- paste(
  "Statistics are weighted means/proportions (dplyr).",
  "Income groups based on EXTERNAL borders (File I) applied to NHIS ih2019 FAMTOTINC.",
  "FAMTOTINC adjusted for inflation (2019->2023); top code ($220k nominal) imputed via weighted draw from ASEC cps2023_03s upper tail.",
  "Derived variables based on codes verified from codebook review.",
  "Standard errors NOT adjusted for complex survey design."
)
print(attr(summary_stats_nhis, "analysis_note"))

# ==== IIIC.9 SAVE SUMMARY RESULTS ====
print("--- Saving NHIS Summary Results ---")
if (!exists("summary_output_dir")) stop("FATAL ERROR: summary_output_dir not defined.")
if (!dir.exists(summary_output_dir)) dir.create(summary_output_dir, recursive = TRUE)

# MODIFIED FILENAME FOR IV2 COMPATIBILITY
output_rds_filename_nhis <- file.path(summary_output_dir, paste0("nhis_", nhis_sample_id, "_summary_stats_G_groups.rds")) 

if (exists("summary_stats_nhis") && !is.null(summary_stats_nhis) && nrow(summary_stats_nhis) > 0) {
  print(paste("... Saving NHIS summary results to:", output_rds_filename_nhis))
  tryCatch({ saveRDS(summary_stats_nhis, file = output_rds_filename_nhis) ; print("... Results saved successfully.") },
           error = function(e) { warning("Error saving summary results to RDS file: ", e$message) })
} else { warning("Summary statistics object 'summary_stats_nhis' is empty or does not exist. Skipping save.") }

# ==== IIIC.10 CLEAN UP MEMORY ====
print("--- Cleaning up NHIS Memory ---")
objects_to_remove_nhis <- c(
  "data_final", "summary_stats_nhis", 
  "nhis_load_result", "asec_load_result" 
)
objects_exist_nhis <- objects_to_remove_nhis[objects_to_remove_nhis %in% ls()]
if(length(objects_exist_nhis) > 0) {
  rm(list = objects_exist_nhis)
  print(paste("... Removed objects:", paste(objects_exist_nhis, collapse=", ")))
} else {
  print("... No large objects specified for removal found.")
}
gc()
print("==== Memory cleanup attempted. Script IIIC (Filename Corrected) finished. ====")

# Project: 1/3 Country Project Visualizations
# Script:  IIID_Process_CPS_Vote_Revised_V3_CodebookFix.R 
# Version: 3.0.1 # <<< MODIFIED: Output filename changed for IV2 compatibility
# Author:  Maxwell Goshert / Janica Murphy / Gemini
# Date:    2025-04-21 19:38:00 (MST) # <<< Current Timestamp
# Location: Phoenix, Arizona, United States # <<< Current Location

# Purpose:
# This R script processes the CPS November 2022 Voting Supplement (cps2022_11s).
# Since continuous HHINCOME is not directly available, it estimates a continuous
# income value (HHINCOME_IMP_2023) for Nov 2022 respondents based on their
# reported FAMINC range and the distribution of HHINCOME within corresponding
# ranges from the March 2023 ASEC (cps2023_03s), adjusted to 2023 dollars.
# A random seed is set for reproducibility.
# It then assigns income groups based on this imputed, inflation-adjusted income
# using pre-calculated EXTERNAL borders (from File I).
# It calculates summary statistics (voting rates among citizens, avg imputed income)
# for the Nov 2022 respondents using the Nov 2022 Voting Supplement weight (VOSUPPWT),
# aligned with the Master Viz & Data Plan v1. Code cleaning and derived variable
# logic have been updated based on codebook review.
# USER SHOULD STILL DOUBLE-CHECK VARIABLE NAMES IF NEEDED.

# NOTE: This is File IIID of V+ (Dataset Processing - CPS Voting w/ Imputed Income)

# Workflow Steps Covered (Sections in this script):
# IIID.0: Setup & Parameters
# IIID.1: Get Data (Load Nov 2022 Voting & Mar 2023 ASEC, Codebook)
# IIID.2: Initial Clean & Prep (Separate for each dataset - Updated Codes)
# IIID.2.5: Adjust ASEC Income & FAMINC Ranges for Inflation (to CY2023 dollars)
# IIID.3: Prepare Imputation Pools from ASEC Data
# IIID.4: Perform Probabilistic Imputation for Nov 2022 Data
# IIID.5: Load Income Borders (External Borders from File I)
# IIID.6: Assign Income Groups (Using Imputed Income & External Borders)
# IIID.7: Calculate Derived Variables (Demographics, Citizenship, Voting - Updated Codes/Logic)
# IIID.8: Calculate Grouped Summary Statistics (Master Plan Specs - Using VOSUPPWT)
# IIID.9: Save Summary Table
# IIID.10: Clean Up Memory

# ==== IIID.0 SETUP & PARAMETERS ====
