# ==== IIID.1 GET DATA (Load Nov 2022 Voting & Mar 2023 ASEC, Codebook) ====
# ... (download_and_load_ipums function and data loading calls remain the same) ...
download_and_load_ipums <- function(sample_id, variables, collection = "cps", description_suffix, output_parent_dir) {
  extract_description <- paste("1/3 Country Project -", description_suffix, "-", Sys.Date())
  print(paste("--- Processing Sample:", sample_id, "(Collection:", collection, ") ---"))
  if (!is.character(variables) || length(variables) == 0) { stop("FATAL ERROR: 'variables' must be a non-empty character vector.") }
  variables <- unique(variables)
  extract_def <- define_extract_micro(
    collection = collection, samples = sample_id, variables = variables, description = extract_description
  )
  print("... IPUMS extract definition created.")
  submitted_extract <- tryCatch({ submit_extract(extract_def) },
                                error = function(e) { message("IPUMS API Error during submission for ", sample_id, ":"); message(e$message); stop("Extract submission failed.") })
  extract_number <- submitted_extract$number
  print(paste("... Extract submitted. Number:", extract_number))
  print("... Waiting for extract preparation...")
  Sys.sleep(10)
  downloadable_extract <- wait_for_extract(submitted_extract)
  print("... Extract is ready for download.")
  download_subdir_name <- paste0(toupper(collection), "_Extract_", extract_number, "_", Sys.Date())
  download_dir_specific <- file.path(output_parent_dir, download_subdir_name) 
  if (!dir.exists(download_dir_specific)) {
    dir.create(download_dir_specific, recursive = TRUE)
    print(paste("... Created download subdirectory:", download_dir_specific))
  } else {
    print(paste("... Download subdirectory already exists:", download_dir_specific))
  }
  print(paste("... Downloading extract files to:", download_dir_specific))
  downloaded_files <- tryCatch({ download_extract(downloadable_extract, download_dir = download_dir_specific, overwrite = TRUE) },
                               error = function(e) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR during file download:", e$message)) })
  print("... Files downloaded.")
  ddi_file_path <- downloaded_files[grep("\\.xml$", downloaded_files)]
  if (length(ddi_file_path) == 0) {
    ddi_file_path <- list.files(path = download_dir_specific, pattern = paste0(collection, "_", sprintf("%05d", extract_number), "\\.xml$"), full.names = TRUE)
    if (length(ddi_file_path) == 0) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Could not find downloaded DDI (.xml) file in:", download_dir_specific)) }
  }
  ddi_file_path <- ddi_file_path[1]
  print(paste("... DDI file path identified:", ddi_file_path))
  codebook_output_filename <- file.path(download_dir_specific, paste0(gsub("\\.xml$", "", basename(ddi_file_path)), "_codebook.txt"))
  if(exists("generate_codebook_from_ddi") && is.function(generate_codebook_from_ddi)){
    print("... Generating text codebook...")
    generate_codebook_from_ddi(ddi_path = ddi_file_path, vars_list = variables, output_txt_path = codebook_output_filename)
  } else { warning("generate_codebook_from_ddi function not found. Skipping codebook generation.") }
  raw_data <- read_ipums_micro(ddi = ddi_file_path, verbose = FALSE)
  print(paste("...", sample_id, "Data loaded successfully."))
  print(paste("Dimensions of raw data:", paste(dim(raw_data), collapse = " x ")))
  return(list(data = raw_data, ddi = ddi_file_path))
}
cps_voting_load_result <- download_and_load_ipums(
  sample_id = cps_voting_sample_id,
  variables = cps_voting_variables_needed,
  description_suffix = paste("CPS", cps_voting_sample_id, "Voting Supp"),
  output_parent_dir = extract_download_parent_dir
)
raw_data_cps_voting <- cps_voting_load_result$data
ddi_cps_voting <- cps_voting_load_result$ddi
asec_load_result <- download_and_load_ipums(
  sample_id = cps_asec_sample_id,
  variables = cps_asec_variables_needed,
  description_suffix = paste("CPS", cps_asec_sample_id, "ASEC Donor"),
  output_parent_dir = extract_download_parent_dir
)
raw_data_cps_asec <- asec_load_result$data
ddi_asec <- asec_load_result$ddi

# ==== IIID.2 INITIAL CLEAN & PREP (Separate for each dataset) ====
# ... (Cleaning logic for Voting and ASEC data remains the same) ...
print("--- Cleaning Nov 2022 Voting Data (Codes Updated from Codebook Review) ---")
if (!exists("raw_data_cps_voting")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: raw_data_cps_voting object not found.") }
clean_data_voting <- raw_data_cps_voting
rm(raw_data_cps_voting); gc()
var_voreg <- "VOREG"
var_voted <- "VOTED"
var_faminc <- "FAMINC"
var_citizen <- "CITIZEN"
var_age <- "AGE"
var_sex <- "SEX" 
var_vosuppwt <- "VOSUPPWT"
voting_codes_to_na <- c(96, 97, 98, 99) 
if (var_voreg %in% names(clean_data_voting)) {
  print(paste("... Cleaning", var_voreg, "- Setting codes", paste(voting_codes_to_na, collapse=", "), "to NA."))
  clean_data_voting <- clean_data_voting %>% mutate(!!sym(var_voreg) := if_else(!!sym(var_voreg) %in% voting_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(!!sym(var_voreg))))
} else { warning(paste(var_voreg, "not found.")) }
if (var_voted %in% names(clean_data_voting)) {
  print(paste("... Cleaning", var_voted, "- Setting codes", paste(voting_codes_to_na, collapse=", "), "to NA."))
  clean_data_voting <- clean_data_voting %>% mutate(!!sym(var_voted) := if_else(!!sym(var_voted) %in% voting_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(!!sym(var_voted))))
} else { warning(paste(var_voted, "not found.")) }
faminc_codes_to_na <- c(995, 996, 997, 999) 
if (var_faminc %in% names(clean_data_voting)) {
  print(paste("... Cleaning", var_faminc, "- Setting codes", paste(faminc_codes_to_na, collapse=", "), "to NA."))
  clean_data_voting <- clean_data_voting %>% mutate(!!sym(var_faminc) := if_else(!!sym(var_faminc) %in% faminc_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(!!sym(var_faminc))))
} else { stop("FATAL ERROR: FAMINC variable not found in Nov 2022 data.")}
citizen_codes_to_na <- c(9) 
if (var_citizen %in% names(clean_data_voting)) {
  print(paste("... Cleaning", var_citizen, "- Setting code", paste(citizen_codes_to_na, collapse=", "), "to NA."))
  clean_data_voting <- clean_data_voting %>% mutate(!!sym(var_citizen) := if_else(!!sym(var_citizen) %in% citizen_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(!!sym(var_citizen))))
} else { warning(paste(var_citizen, "not found. Citizenship filter will fail.")) }
sex_codes_to_na <- c(9) 
if (var_sex %in% names(clean_data_voting)) {
  print(paste("... Cleaning", var_sex, "- Setting code", paste(sex_codes_to_na, collapse=", "), "to NA."))
  clean_data_voting <- clean_data_voting %>% mutate(!!sym(var_sex) := if_else(!!sym(var_sex) %in% sex_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(!!sym(var_sex))))
} else { warning(paste(var_sex, "not found.")) }
if (var_vosuppwt %in% names(clean_data_voting)) {
  print(paste("... Ensuring", var_vosuppwt, "is numeric."))
  clean_data_voting <- clean_data_voting %>% mutate(!!sym(var_vosuppwt) := as.numeric(!!sym(var_vosuppwt)))
  if (any(clean_data_voting[[var_vosuppwt]] <= 0, na.rm = TRUE)) { warning(paste("Non-positive values found in weight variable:", var_vosuppwt)) }
} else { stop(paste("Weight variable", var_vosuppwt, "not found in Nov 2022 data."))}
if (var_age %in% names(clean_data_voting)) {
  print(paste("... Ensuring", var_age, "is numeric."))
  clean_data_voting <- clean_data_voting %>% mutate(!!sym(var_age) := as.numeric(!!sym(var_age)))
} else {
  warning(paste("Age variable", var_age, "not found. Age filter might fail."))
}
if (var_age %in% names(clean_data_voting)) {
  n_before_filter <- nrow(clean_data_voting)
  clean_data_voting <- clean_data_voting %>% filter(.data[[var_age]] >= 18)
  n_after_filter <- nrow(clean_data_voting)
  print(paste("... Applied filter:", var_age, ">= 18.", n_before_filter - n_after_filter, "records removed."))
}
clean_data_voting <- clean_data_voting %>% select(any_of(cps_voting_variables_needed))
print("... Nov 2022 Voting data cleaned.")
print("--- Cleaning Mar 2023 ASEC Data ---")
if (!exists("raw_data_cps_asec")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: raw_data_cps_asec object not found.") }
clean_data_asec <- raw_data_cps_asec
rm(raw_data_cps_asec); gc()
var_hhincome_asec <- "HHINCOME"
var_asecwt <- "ASECWT"
income_codes_to_na_asec <- c(99999999) 
if (var_hhincome_asec %in% names(clean_data_asec)) {
  print(paste("... Cleaning ASEC", var_hhincome_asec, "- Setting code", paste(income_codes_to_na_asec, collapse=", "), "to NA."))
  clean_data_asec <- clean_data_asec %>% mutate(!!sym(var_hhincome_asec) := if_else(!!sym(var_hhincome_asec) %in% income_codes_to_na_asec, NA_real_, as.numeric(!!sym(var_hhincome_asec))))
} else { stop("FATAL ERROR: HHINCOME variable not found in ASEC data.") }
if (var_asecwt %in% names(clean_data_asec)) {
  print(paste("... Ensuring ASEC", var_asecwt, "is numeric."))
  clean_data_asec <- clean_data_asec %>% mutate(!!sym(var_asecwt) := as.numeric(!!sym(var_asecwt)))
  if (any(clean_data_asec[[var_asecwt]] <= 0, na.rm = TRUE)) { warning(paste("Non-positive values found in weight variable:", var_asecwt)) }
} else { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: ASEC Weight variable", var_asecwt, "not found.")) }
clean_data_asec <- clean_data_asec %>%
  select(all_of(c(var_hhincome_asec, var_asecwt))) %>% 
  filter(!is.na(!!sym(var_asecwt)) & !!sym(var_asecwt) > 0 & !is.na(!!sym(var_hhincome_asec)))
print("... Mar 2023 ASEC data cleaned and filtered.")

# ==== IIID.2.5 Adjust ASEC Income & FAMINC Ranges for Inflation (to CY2023 dollars) ====
# ... (Inflation adjustment logic remains the same) ...
print(paste("... Adjusting ASEC HHINCOME (CY2022) and FAMINC ranges for inflation using factor:", round(inflation_factor_2022_to_2023, 5)))
if ("HHINCOME" %in% names(clean_data_asec)) {
  clean_data_asec <- clean_data_asec %>%
    mutate(HHINCOME_2023 = HHINCOME * inflation_factor_2022_to_2023) %>%
    select(-HHINCOME) 
  print("... Created inflation-adjusted ASEC variable 'HHINCOME_2023'.")
} else {
  stop("HHINCOME not found in ASEC data, cannot proceed.")
}
faminc_ranges_adj <- lapply(faminc_ranges, function(range) {
  min_adj <- range[1] * inflation_factor_2022_to_2023
  max_adj <- ifelse(is.infinite(range[2]), Inf, range[2] * inflation_factor_2022_to_2023)
  return(c(min_adj, max_adj))
})
print("... Adjusted FAMINC range boundaries to 2023 dollars.")

# ==== IIID.3 Prepare Imputation Pools from ASEC Data ====
# ... (Imputation pool preparation logic remains the same) ...
print("--- Preparing imputation pools from adjusted ASEC data ---")
if (!"HHINCOME_2023" %in% names(clean_data_asec)) { stop("Adjusted ASEC income HHINCOME_2023 not found.")}
if (!"ASECWT" %in% names(clean_data_asec)) { stop("ASECWT not found in cleaned ASEC data.")}
imputation_pools <- list()
valid_faminc_codes <- names(faminc_ranges_adj)
for (code in valid_faminc_codes) {
  range_adj <- faminc_ranges_adj[[code]]
  min_val <- range_adj[1]
  max_val <- range_adj[2]
  if (is.infinite(max_val)) {
    asec_subset <- clean_data_asec %>% filter(HHINCOME_2023 >= min_val)
  } else {
    asec_subset <- clean_data_asec %>% filter(HHINCOME_2023 >= min_val & HHINCOME_2023 < max_val)
  }
  if (nrow(asec_subset) > 0 && sum(asec_subset$ASECWT) > 0) {
    imputation_pools[[code]] <- asec_subset %>% select(HHINCOME_2023, ASECWT)
  } else {
    imputation_pools[[code]] <- NULL
    warning("No ASEC records found (or zero total weight) for FAMINC code ", code, " in adjusted range [", round(min_val), ", ", round(max_val), "). Imputation will result in NA for this group.")
  }
}
rm(clean_data_asec); gc() 
print("... Imputation pools created.")

# ==== IIID.4 Perform Probabilistic Imputation for Nov 2022 Data ====
# ... (Imputation logic remains the same) ...
print("--- Performing probabilistic imputation ---")
if (!var_faminc %in% names(clean_data_voting)) { stop("FAMINC column not found in voting data for imputation.")}
impute_income <- function(faminc_code) {
  if (is.na(faminc_code)) return(NA_real_)
  pool_data <- imputation_pools[[as.character(faminc_code)]]
  if (is.null(pool_data) || nrow(pool_data) == 0 || sum(pool_data$ASECWT) <= 0) {
    return(NA_real_)
  } else {
    imputed_value <- sample(pool_data$HHINCOME_2023, size = 1, replace = TRUE, prob = pool_data$ASECWT)
    return(imputed_value)
  }
}
print("... Applying imputation function (may take time)...")
imputed_values <- sapply(clean_data_voting[[var_faminc]], impute_income) 
clean_data_voting$HHINCOME_IMP_2023 <- imputed_values
n_imputed <- sum(!is.na(clean_data_voting$HHINCOME_IMP_2023))
n_total <- nrow(clean_data_voting)
print(paste("... Imputation complete.", n_imputed, "out of", n_total, "records received an imputed income value."))
if (n_imputed < n_total) {
  print(paste("...", n_total - n_imputed, "records have NA imputed income (due to NA FAMINC or empty/zero-weight ASEC pool)."))
}
n_before_filter <- nrow(clean_data_voting)
clean_data_voting <- clean_data_voting %>% filter(!is.na(HHINCOME_IMP_2023))
n_after_filter <- nrow(clean_data_voting)
if (n_after_filter < n_before_filter) {
  print(paste("... Removed", n_before_filter - n_after_filter, "records with NA imputed income before grouping."))
}
if (n_after_filter == 0) { stop("FATAL ERROR: Zero rows remaining after filtering out failed imputations.")}

# ==== IIID.5 LOAD INCOME BORDERS (External Borders from File I) ====
# ... (Loading borders logic remains the same) ...
print("--- Loading External Income Borders ---")
if (!file.exists(borders_csv_file)) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: EXTERNAL Within-tercile borders file not found:", borders_csv_file)) }
borders_df <- readr::read_csv(borders_csv_file, show_col_types = FALSE)
print("... EXTERNAL Within-tercile borders loaded successfully.")
print("WARNING: Using income borders calculated from a different dataset/year (File I).")
if (!file.exists(main_cutoffs_rds_file)) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: EXTERNAL Main tercile cutoffs file not found:", main_cutoffs_rds_file)) }
loaded_main_cutoffs <- readRDS(main_cutoffs_rds_file)
if (is.list(loaded_main_cutoffs) && all(c("main_cutoff1", "main_cutoff2") %in% names(loaded_main_cutoffs))) {
  main_cutoff1_val <- loaded_main_cutoffs$main_cutoff1; main_cutoff2_val <- loaded_main_cutoffs$main_cutoff2
  print(paste("... EXTERNAL Main cutoffs loaded successfully: Cutoff1 =", main_cutoff1_val, ", Cutoff2 =", main_cutoff2_val))
} else { stop("FATAL ERROR: Loaded main cutoffs object does not have the expected structure.") }

# ==== IIID.6 ASSIGN INCOME GROUPS (Using Imputed Income & External Borders) ====
# ... (Assign income groups logic remains the same) ...
print("--- Assigning Income Groups ---")
if (!exists("clean_data_voting")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: 'clean_data_voting' object not found.") }
if (!exists("borders_df")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: Borders data frame 'borders_df' not found.") }
income_variable_name <- "HHINCOME_IMP_2023" 
if (!income_variable_name %in% names(clean_data_voting)) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Imputed income variable '", income_variable_name, "' not found.")) }
weight_variable_name <- "VOSUPPWT"
if (!weight_variable_name %in% names(clean_data_voting)) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Voting supplement weight variable", weight_variable_name, "needed for temp design not found.")) }
data_for_design <- clean_data_voting %>% filter(!is.na(!!sym(weight_variable_name)) & !!sym(weight_variable_name) > 0)
if(nrow(data_for_design) < nrow(clean_data_voting)){
  warning("Removed ", nrow(clean_data_voting) - nrow(data_for_design), " rows with missing or non-positive weights before creating temp design.")
}
if(nrow(data_for_design) == 0) { stop("FATAL ERROR: No rows remaining after filtering for positive weights.")}
temp_design_for_groups <- survey::svydesign(ids=~1, data=data_for_design, weights=as.formula(paste0("~", weight_variable_name)))
print(paste("... Calling assign_income_groups function with detail level:", fine_group_detail_level, "and income var:", income_variable_name))
print("... Applying EXTERNAL borders to imputed Nov 2022 income.")
temp_design_with_groups <- assign_income_groups(
  survey_obj = temp_design_for_groups,
  borders_df = borders_df, 
  income_var_name = income_variable_name, 
  detail_level = fine_group_detail_level, 
  main_cutoff1 = main_cutoff1_val, 
  main_cutoff2 = main_cutoff2_val  
)
data_with_groups <- temp_design_with_groups$variables
new_cols <- c("income_tercile", "fine_income_group")
if (all(new_cols %in% names(data_with_groups))) {
  print("... Successfully added 'income_tercile' and 'fine_income_group' columns (based on external borders).")
} else {
  warning("Could not find expected new columns ('income_tercile', 'fine_income_group'). Check assign_income_groups function.")
}
rm(temp_design_for_groups, temp_design_with_groups, borders_df, loaded_main_cutoffs, data_for_design, clean_data_voting); gc()

# ==== IIID.7 CALCULATE DERIVED VARIABLES (Demographics, Citizenship, Voting - Updated Codes/Logic) ====
# ... (Derived variable calculation logic remains the same) ...
print("--- Calculating Derived Variables (Codes Updated from Codebook Review) ---")
if (!exists("data_with_groups")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: 'data_with_groups' data frame not found.") }
data_final <- data_with_groups 
rm(data_with_groups); gc()
var_age <- "AGE"
var_race <- "RACE"
var_hispan <- "HISPAN"
var_citizen <- "CITIZEN"
var_voreg <- "VOREG"
var_voted <- "VOTED"
var_sex <- "SEX" 
required_vars_derived <- c(var_age, var_race, var_hispan, var_citizen, var_voreg, var_voted, var_sex)
missing_vars <- required_vars_derived[!required_vars_derived %in% names(data_final)]
if(length(missing_vars) > 0) {
  stop("FATAL ERROR: The following required variables for derived calculations are missing from data_final: ", paste(missing_vars, collapse=", "), ". Verify names in IIID.0.3.")
}
data_final <- data_final %>% mutate(
  race_ethnicity = case_when(
    !is.na(!!sym(var_hispan)) & !!sym(var_hispan) != 0 ~ "Hispanic", 
    !is.na(!!sym(var_hispan)) & !!sym(var_hispan) == 0 & !!sym(var_race) == 100 ~ "White, Non-Hispanic", 
    !is.na(!!sym(var_hispan)) & !!sym(var_hispan) == 0 & !!sym(var_race) == 200 ~ "Black, Non-Hispanic", 
    !is.na(!!sym(var_hispan)) & !!sym(var_hispan) == 0 & !!sym(var_race) == 651 ~ "Asian, Non-Hispanic", 
    !is.na(!!sym(var_hispan)) & !!sym(var_hispan) == 0 & !!sym(var_race) == 300 ~ "AIAN, Non-Hispanic", 
    !is.na(!!sym(var_hispan)) & !!sym(var_hispan) == 0 ~ "Other/Multi, Non-Hispanic", 
    TRUE ~ NA_character_
  ),
  is_citizen = case_when(
    !!sym(var_citizen) %in% c(1, 2, 3, 4) ~ 1, 
    !!sym(var_citizen) == 5 ~ 0,             
    TRUE ~ NA_real_ 
  ),
  is_registered = case_when(
    !!sym(var_voreg) == 2 ~ 1, 
    !!sym(var_voreg) == 1 ~ 0, 
    TRUE ~ NA_real_ 
  ),
  is_voter = case_when(
    !!sym(var_voted) == 2 ~ 1, 
    !!sym(var_voted) == 1 ~ 0, 
    TRUE ~ NA_real_ 
  )
)
print("... Derived variables calculated using codes updated from codebook review.")

# ==== IIID.8 CALCULATE GROUPED SUMMARY STATISTICS (Master Plan Specs - Using VOSUPPWT) ====
# ... (Summary statistics calculation logic remains the same) ...
print("--- Calculating Grouped Summary Statistics (Master Plan Specs) ---")
if (!exists("data_final")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: data_final object not found.") }
grouping_vars_fine <- c("income_tercile", "fine_income_group")
if (!all(grouping_vars_fine %in% names(data_final))) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Fine grouping variables not found")) }
var_vosuppwt <- "VOSUPPWT"
if(!var_vosuppwt %in% names(data_final)) stop(paste("Weight variable", var_vosuppwt, "not found in data_final."))
print("... Calculating weighted means/proportions grouped by External Main Tercile and Fine Group...")
summary_stats_cps_voting <- data_final %>%
  filter(!is.na(income_tercile) & !is.na(fine_income_group)) %>%
  group_by(income_tercile, fine_income_group, .drop = FALSE) %>%
  summarise(
    n_unweighted_persons = n(),
    n_weighted_persons = sum(!!sym(var_vosuppwt), na.rm = TRUE),
    avg_hhincome_imp_2023 = weighted.mean(HHINCOME_IMP_2023, w = !!sym(var_vosuppwt), na.rm = TRUE),
    prop_registered_citizen = {
      sum_wt_registered_citizen <- sum(
        ifelse(!is.na(is_citizen) & is_citizen == 1 & !is.na(is_registered) & is_registered == 1, !!sym(var_vosuppwt), 0),
        na.rm = TRUE
      )
      sum_wt_citizen <- sum(
        ifelse(!is.na(is_citizen) & is_citizen == 1, !!sym(var_vosuppwt), 0),
        na.rm = TRUE
      )
      if (sum_wt_citizen > 0) sum_wt_registered_citizen / sum_wt_citizen else NA_real_
    },
    prop_voted_citizen = {
      sum_wt_voted_citizen <- sum(
        ifelse(!is.na(is_citizen) & is_citizen == 1 & !is.na(is_voter) & is_voter == 1, !!sym(var_vosuppwt), 0),
        na.rm = TRUE
      )
      sum_wt_citizen <- sum(
        ifelse(!is.na(is_citizen) & is_citizen == 1, !!sym(var_vosuppwt), 0),
        na.rm = TRUE
      )
      if (sum_wt_citizen > 0) sum_wt_voted_citizen / sum_wt_citizen else NA_real_
    },
    .groups = "drop" 
  )
print("... Weighted summary statistics calculated using dplyr.")
print("First few rows of final summary_stats_cps_voting:")
print(head(summary_stats_cps_voting))
print("Structure of final summary_stats_cps_voting:")
str(summary_stats_cps_voting)
attr(summary_stats_cps_voting, "analysis_note") <- paste(
  "Statistics based on Nov 2022 CPS Voting Supp respondents (>=18) with imputed income.",
  "Income imputed from FAMINC range based on CY2022 ASEC HHINCOME distribution, adjusted to 2023 dollars.",
  "Income groups based on EXTERNAL borders (File I). Analysis uses Nov 2022 VOSUPPWT.",
  "Voting rates calculated among citizens only.",
  "Derived variables based on codes verified from codebook review.",
  "Standard errors NOT adjusted for complex survey design."
)
print(attr(summary_stats_cps_voting, "analysis_note"))

# ==== IIID.9 SAVE SUMMARY RESULTS ====
print("--- Saving CPS Voting Summary Results ---")
if (!exists("summary_output_dir")) stop("FATAL ERROR: summary_output_dir not defined.")
if (!dir.exists(summary_output_dir)) dir.create(summary_output_dir, recursive = TRUE)
if (exists("summary_stats_cps_voting") && !is.null(summary_stats_cps_voting) && nrow(summary_stats_cps_voting) > 0) {
  print(paste("... Saving CPS Voting summary results to:", output_rds_filename))
  tryCatch({ saveRDS(summary_stats_cps_voting, file = output_rds_filename) ; print("... Results saved successfully.") },
           error = function(e) { warning("Error saving summary results to RDS file: ", e$message) })
} else { warning("Summary statistics object 'summary_stats_cps_voting' is empty or does not exist. Skipping save.") }

# ==== IIID.10 CLEAN UP MEMORY ====
print("--- Cleaning up CPS Voting Memory ---")
objects_to_remove_cps_voting <- c(
  "data_final", "summary_stats_cps_voting", "imputation_pools",
  "cps_voting_load_result", "asec_load_result"
)
objects_exist_cps_voting <- objects_to_remove_cps_voting[objects_to_remove_cps_voting %in% ls()]
if(length(objects_exist_cps_voting) > 0) {
  rm(list = objects_exist_cps_voting)
  print(paste("... Removed objects:", paste(objects_exist_cps_voting, collapse=", ")))
} else {
  print("... No large objects specified for removal found.")
}
gc()
print("==== Memory cleanup attempted. Script IIID (Filename Corrected) finished. ====")
# ====== End of Script ======

# ==== 0. ABOUT ====
# Project: 1/3 Country Project Visualizations
# Script:  IIIE_Process_CPS_FSS_Revised_V2_CodebookFix.R
# Version: 2.0.1 # <<< MODIFIED: Output RDS filename changed for IV2 compatibility
# Author:  Maxwell Goshert / Janica Murphy / Gemini
# Date:    2025-06-03 (MST) # <<< Current Timestamp
# Location: Phoenix, Arizona, United States 

# Purpose:
# This R script processes the CPS December 2022 Food Security Supplement (FSS) (cps2022_12s).
# It estimates a continuous income value (HHINCOME_IMP_2023) for Dec 2022 respondents
# based on their reported FAMINC range and the distribution of HHINCOME within corresponding
# ranges from the March 2023 ASEC (cps2023_03s), adjusted to 2023 dollars.
# A random seed is set for reproducibility.
# It then assigns income groups based on this imputed, inflation-adjusted income
# using pre-calculated EXTERNAL borders (from File I).
# It calculates summary statistics (food insecurity rate, avg imputed income)
# for the Dec 2022 respondents using the Dec 2022 Food Security Supplement weight (FSSUPPWT),
# aligned with the Master Viz & Data Plan v1. Code cleaning and derived variable
# logic have been updated based on codebook review.
# USER SHOULD STILL DOUBLE-CHECK FAMINC RANGES IF NEEDED.

# NOTE: This is File IIIE of V+ (Dataset Processing - CPS FSS w/ Imputed Income)

# Workflow Steps Covered (Sections in this script):
# IIIE.0: Setup & Parameters
# IIIE.1: Get Data (Load Dec 2022 FSS & Mar 2023 ASEC, Codebook)
# IIIE.2: Initial Clean & Prep (Separate for each dataset - Updated Codes)
# IIIE.2.5: Adjust ASEC Income & FAMINC Ranges for Inflation (to CY2023 dollars)
# IIIE.3: Prepare Imputation Pools from ASEC Data
# IIIE.4: Perform Probabilistic Imputation for Dec 2022 Data
# IIIE.5: Load Income Borders (External Borders from File I)
# IIIE.6: Assign Income Groups (Using Imputed Income & External Borders)
# IIIE.7: Calculate Derived Variables (Demographics, Food Security - Updated Codes/Logic)
# IIIE.8: Calculate Grouped Summary Statistics (Master Plan Specs - Using FSSUPPWT)
# IIIE.9: Save Summary Table
# IIIE.10: Clean Up Memory

# ==== IIIE.0 SETUP & PARAMETERS ====

# ===== IIIE.0.1 Load Libraries =====

# Ensure packages are installed first if needed:
# install.packages(c("ipumsr", "dplyr", "readr", "stringr", "rlang", "tibble", "purrr", "survey", "here"))
library(ipumsr)
library(dplyr)
library(readr)
library(stringr)
library(rlang)
library(tibble)
library(purrr)
library(survey) # Needed for svydesign used in assign_income_groups helper
library(here)   # For robust file paths

print("... Libraries loaded.")

# ===== IIIE.0.2 Source Shared Functions =====
shared_functions_file <- here::here("II_Shared_Functions.R")
if (file.exists(shared_functions_file)) {
  source(shared_functions_file)
  print("... Shared functions sourced successfully.")
} else {
  stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Shared functions file not found:", shared_functions_file))
}

# ===== IIIE.0.3 Define Parameters =====

# --- Random Seed ---
# Set seed for reproducible imputation using Date + Run Number format.
current_seed_value <- 2025042101 # Use a consistent seed for testing, change for different runs
set.seed(current_seed_value)
print(paste("... Random seed set to", current_seed_value, "for imputation."))

# --- IPUMS Extract Definition ---
ipums_collection <- "cps" # IPUMS Collection for CPS
cps_fss_sample_id <- "cps2022_12s" # December 2022 FSS Supplement
cps_asec_sample_id <- "cps2023_03s"   # March 2023 ASEC Supplement (Donor Pool)

# --- Inflation Adjustment ---
# Using BLS CPI-U Annual Averages (CY2022 -> CY2023) - Used for ASEC income and FAMINC range boundaries
# 2022 CPI-U: 292.655
# 2023 CPI-U: 304.702 # Example - update with final official value if needed
cpi_lookup <- list(
  "2022" = 292.655,
  "2023" = 304.702 
)
ASEC_INCOME_YEAR <- 2022
REFERENCE_YEAR <- 2023

if (!as.character(ASEC_INCOME_YEAR) %in% names(cpi_lookup) || !as.character(REFERENCE_YEAR) %in% names(cpi_lookup)) {
  stop(paste("CPI value for ASEC income year", ASEC_INCOME_YEAR, "or reference year", REFERENCE_YEAR, "not found in cpi_lookup. Please update the list."))
}
cpi_asec_income_year <- cpi_lookup[[as.character(ASEC_INCOME_YEAR)]]
cpi_reference_year <- cpi_lookup[[as.character(REFERENCE_YEAR)]]
inflation_factor_2022_to_2023 <- cpi_reference_year / cpi_asec_income_year
print(paste("... Inflation Factor (CY2022 -> CY2023):", round(inflation_factor_2022_to_2023, 5)))

# ==== IIIE.1 GET DATA (Load Dec 2022 FSS & Mar 2023 ASEC, Codebook) ====
# ... (Download and load function remains the same, calls for FSS and ASEC data) ...
# --- Function to Download and Load (Includes Codebook Generation) ---
download_and_load_ipums <- function(sample_id, variables, collection = "cps", description_suffix, output_parent_dir) {
  extract_description <- paste("1/3 Country Project -", description_suffix, "-", Sys.Date())
  print(paste("--- Processing Sample:", sample_id, "(Collection:", collection, ") ---"))
  if (!is.character(variables) || length(variables) == 0) { stop("FATAL ERROR: 'variables' must be a non-empty character vector.") }
  variables <- unique(variables)
  
  extract_def <- define_extract_micro(
    collection = collection, samples = sample_id, variables = variables, description = extract_description
  )
  print("... IPUMS extract definition created.")
  
  submitted_extract <- tryCatch({ submit_extract(extract_def) },
                                error = function(e) { message("IPUMS API Error during submission for ", sample_id, ":"); message(e$message); stop("Extract submission failed.") })
  extract_number <- submitted_extract$number
  print(paste("... Extract submitted. Number:", extract_number))
  print("... Waiting for extract preparation...")
  Sys.sleep(10) # Give IPUMS servers a moment
  downloadable_extract <- wait_for_extract(submitted_extract)
  print("... Extract is ready for download.")
  
  download_subdir_name <- paste0(toupper(collection), "_Extract_", extract_number, "_", Sys.Date())
  download_dir_specific <- file.path(output_parent_dir, download_subdir_name) 
  if (!dir.exists(download_dir_specific)) {
    dir.create(download_dir_specific, recursive = TRUE)
    print(paste("... Created download subdirectory:", download_dir_specific))
  } else {
    print(paste("... Download subdirectory already exists:", download_dir_specific))
  }
  print(paste("... Downloading extract files to:", download_dir_specific))
  downloaded_files <- tryCatch({ download_extract(downloadable_extract, download_dir = download_dir_specific, overwrite = TRUE) },
                               error = function(e) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR during file download:", e$message)) })
  print("... Files downloaded.")
  
  ddi_file_path <- downloaded_files[grep("\\.xml$", downloaded_files)]
  if (length(ddi_file_path) == 0) {
    ddi_file_path <- list.files(path = download_dir_specific, pattern = paste0(collection, "_", sprintf("%05d", extract_number), "\\.xml$"), full.names = TRUE)
    if (length(ddi_file_path) == 0) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Could not find downloaded DDI (.xml) file in:", download_dir_specific)) }
  }
  ddi_file_path <- ddi_file_path[1]
  print(paste("... DDI file path identified:", ddi_file_path))
  
  codebook_output_filename <- file.path(download_dir_specific, paste0(gsub("\\.xml$", "", basename(ddi_file_path)), "_codebook.txt"))
  if(exists("generate_codebook_from_ddi") && is.function(generate_codebook_from_ddi)){
    print("... Generating text codebook...")
    generate_codebook_from_ddi(ddi_path = ddi_file_path, vars_list = variables, output_txt_path = codebook_output_filename)
  } else { warning("generate_codebook_from_ddi function not found. Skipping codebook generation.") }
  
  raw_data <- read_ipums_micro(ddi = ddi_file_path, verbose = FALSE)
  print(paste("...", sample_id, "Data loaded successfully."))
  print(paste("Dimensions of raw data:", paste(dim(raw_data), collapse = " x ")))
  
  return(list(data = raw_data, ddi = ddi_file_path))
}

# --- Load Dec 2022 FSS Data ---
cps_fss_load_result <- download_and_load_ipums(
  sample_id = cps_fss_sample_id,
  variables = cps_fss_variables_needed,
  description_suffix = paste("CPS", cps_fss_sample_id, "FSS Supp"),
  output_parent_dir = extract_download_parent_dir
)
raw_data_cps_fss <- cps_fss_load_result$data
ddi_cps_fss <- cps_fss_load_result$ddi

# --- Load Mar 2023 ASEC Data ---
asec_load_result <- download_and_load_ipums(
  sample_id = cps_asec_sample_id,
  variables = cps_asec_variables_needed,
  description_suffix = paste("CPS", cps_asec_sample_id, "ASEC Donor"),
  output_parent_dir = extract_download_parent_dir
)
raw_data_cps_asec <- asec_load_result$data
ddi_asec <- asec_load_result$ddi

# ===== IIIE.2.1 Clean Dec 2022 FSS Data =====
print("--- Cleaning Dec 2022 FSS Data (Codes Updated from Codebook Review) ---")
if (!exists("raw_data_cps_fss")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: raw_data_cps_fss object not found.") }
clean_data_fss <- raw_data_cps_fss
rm(raw_data_cps_fss); gc()

# Define variable names
var_fsstatus <- "FSSTATUS"
var_faminc <- "FAMINC"
var_age <- "AGE"
var_sex <- "SEX"
var_hispan <- "HISPAN" 
var_race <- "RACE" # Added for race_ethnicity consistency
var_fssuppwt <- "FSSUPPWT"

# Clean FSSTATUS - Updated NA codes based on codebook
fsstatus_codes_to_na <- c(98, 99) # Updated: No response, NIU
if (var_fsstatus %in% names(clean_data_fss)) {
  print(paste("... Cleaning", var_fsstatus, "- Setting codes", paste(fsstatus_codes_to_na, collapse=", "), "to NA."))
  clean_data_fss <- clean_data_fss %>% mutate(!!sym(var_fsstatus) := if_else(!!sym(var_fsstatus) %in% fsstatus_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(!!sym(var_fsstatus))))
} else { warning(paste(var_fsstatus, "not found.")) }

# Clean FAMINC - Updated NA codes based on codebook
faminc_codes_to_na <- c(995, 996, 997, 999) # Updated: Missing, Refused, DK, Blank
if (var_faminc %in% names(clean_data_fss)) {
  print(paste("... Cleaning", var_faminc, "- Setting codes", paste(faminc_codes_to_na, collapse=", "), "to NA."))
  clean_data_fss <- clean_data_fss %>% mutate(!!sym(var_faminc) := if_else(!!sym(var_faminc) %in% faminc_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(!!sym(var_faminc))))
} else { stop("FATAL ERROR: FAMINC variable not found in Dec 2022 data.")}

# Clean HISPAN - Confirmed NA codes from codebook
hispan_codes_to_na <- c(901, 902) # Confirmed: DK, N/A
if (var_hispan %in% names(clean_data_fss)) {
  print(paste("... Cleaning", var_hispan, "- Setting codes", paste(hispan_codes_to_na, collapse=", "), "to NA."))
  clean_data_fss <- clean_data_fss %>% mutate(!!sym(var_hispan) := if_else(!!sym(var_hispan) %in% hispan_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(!!sym(var_hispan))))
} else { warning(paste("Variable '", var_hispan,"' not found.", sep="")) }

# Clean RACE - Added for consistency
race_codes_to_na <- c(999) # Example, verify with FSS DDI if RACE is different
if (var_race %in% names(clean_data_fss)) {
  print(paste("... Cleaning", var_race, "- Setting code", paste(race_codes_to_na, collapse=", "), "to NA."))
  clean_data_fss <- clean_data_fss %>% mutate(!!sym(var_race) := if_else(!!sym(var_race) %in% race_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(!!sym(var_race))))
} else { warning(paste("Variable '", var_race, "' not found.", sep="")) }


# Clean SEX (Code 9=NIU from codebook)
sex_codes_to_na <- c(9) # NIU
if (var_sex %in% names(clean_data_fss)) {
  print(paste("... Cleaning", var_sex, "- Setting code", paste(sex_codes_to_na, collapse=", "), "to NA."))
  clean_data_fss <- clean_data_fss %>% mutate(!!sym(var_sex) := if_else(!!sym(var_sex) %in% sex_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(!!sym(var_sex))))
} else { warning(paste(var_sex, "not found.")) }

# Ensure weight is numeric and positive
if (var_fssuppwt %in% names(clean_data_fss)) {
  print(paste("... Ensuring", var_fssuppwt, "is numeric."))
  clean_data_fss <- clean_data_fss %>% mutate(!!sym(var_fssuppwt) := as.numeric(!!sym(var_fssuppwt)))
  if (any(clean_data_fss[[var_fssuppwt]] <= 0, na.rm = TRUE)) { warning(paste("Non-positive values found in weight variable:", var_fssuppwt)) }
} else { stop(paste("Weight variable", var_fssuppwt, "not found in Dec 2022 data."))}

# Ensure AGE is numeric
if (var_age %in% names(clean_data_fss)) {
  print(paste("... Ensuring", var_age, "is numeric."))
  clean_data_fss <- clean_data_fss %>% mutate(!!sym(var_age) := as.numeric(!!sym(var_age)))
} else {
  warning(paste("Age variable", var_age, "not found. Age filter might fail."))
}

# Select necessary columns (use updated list)
clean_data_fss <- clean_data_fss %>% select(any_of(cps_fss_variables_needed))
print("... Dec 2022 FSS data cleaned.")

# ===== IIIE.2.2 Clean Mar 2023 ASEC Data =====
print("--- Cleaning Mar 2023 ASEC Data ---")
if (!exists("raw_data_cps_asec")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: raw_data_cps_asec object not found.") }
clean_data_asec <- raw_data_cps_asec
rm(raw_data_cps_asec); gc()

# Define ASEC variable names
var_hhincome_asec <- "HHINCOME"
var_asecwt <- "ASECWT"

# Clean HHINCOME (ASEC version) - Confirmed NA code from codebook review
income_codes_to_na_asec <- c(99999999) # Confirmed: Only NIU code listed
if (var_hhincome_asec %in% names(clean_data_asec)) {
  print(paste("... Cleaning ASEC", var_hhincome_asec, "- Setting code", paste(income_codes_to_na_asec, collapse=", "), "to NA."))
  clean_data_asec <- clean_data_asec %>% mutate(!!sym(var_hhincome_asec) := if_else(!!sym(var_hhincome_asec) %in% income_codes_to_na_asec, NA_real_, as.numeric(!!sym(var_hhincome_asec))))
} else { stop("FATAL ERROR: HHINCOME variable not found in ASEC data.") }

# Ensure ASEC weight is numeric and positive
if (var_asecwt %in% names(clean_data_asec)) {
  print(paste("... Ensuring ASEC", var_asecwt, "is numeric."))
  clean_data_asec <- clean_data_asec %>% mutate(!!sym(var_asecwt) := as.numeric(!!sym(var_asecwt)))
  if (any(clean_data_asec[[var_asecwt]] <= 0, na.rm = TRUE)) { warning(paste("Non-positive values found in weight variable:", var_asecwt)) }
} else { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: ASEC Weight variable", var_asecwt, "not found.")) }

# Filter ASEC data for valid weights and income (for donor pool)
clean_data_asec <- clean_data_asec %>%
  select(all_of(c(var_hhincome_asec, var_asecwt))) %>% # Keep only needed cols
  filter(!is.na(!!sym(var_asecwt)) & !!sym(var_asecwt) > 0 & !is.na(!!sym(var_hhincome_asec)))
print("... Mar 2023 ASEC data cleaned and filtered.")


# ==== IIIE.2.5 Adjust ASEC Income & FAMINC Ranges for Inflation (to CY2023 dollars) ====
print(paste("... Adjusting ASEC HHINCOME (CY2022) and FAMINC ranges for inflation using factor:", round(inflation_factor_2022_to_2023, 5)))

# Adjust ASEC HHINCOME
if ("HHINCOME" %in% names(clean_data_asec)) {
  clean_data_asec <- clean_data_asec %>%
    mutate(HHINCOME_2023 = HHINCOME * inflation_factor_2022_to_2023) %>%
    select(-HHINCOME) # Drop original CY2022 version
  print("... Created inflation-adjusted ASEC variable 'HHINCOME_2023'.")
} else {
  stop("HHINCOME not found in ASEC data, cannot proceed.")
}

# Adjust FAMINC range boundaries (Using ranges assumed same as Nov - USER VERIFY!)
faminc_ranges_adj <- lapply(faminc_ranges, function(range) {
  min_adj <- range[1] * inflation_factor_2022_to_2023
  max_adj <- ifelse(is.infinite(range[2]), Inf, range[2] * inflation_factor_2022_to_2023)
  return(c(min_adj, max_adj))
})
print("... Adjusted FAMINC range boundaries to 2023 dollars (USER MUST VERIFY RANGES FOR DEC 2022!).")


# ==== IIIE.3 Prepare Imputation Pools from ASEC Data ====
print("--- Preparing imputation pools from adjusted ASEC data ---")

# Check required variables exist
if (!"HHINCOME_2023" %in% names(clean_data_asec)) { stop("Adjusted ASEC income HHINCOME_2023 not found.")}
if (!"ASECWT" %in% names(clean_data_asec)) { stop("ASECWT not found in cleaned ASEC data.")}

imputation_pools <- list()
valid_faminc_codes <- names(faminc_ranges_adj)

for (code in valid_faminc_codes) {
  range_adj <- faminc_ranges_adj[[code]]
  min_val <- range_adj[1]
  max_val <- range_adj[2]
  
  if (is.infinite(max_val)) {
    asec_subset <- clean_data_asec %>% filter(HHINCOME_2023 >= min_val)
  } else {
    asec_subset <- clean_data_asec %>% filter(HHINCOME_2023 >= min_val & HHINCOME_2023 < max_val)
  }
  
  if (nrow(asec_subset) > 0 && sum(asec_subset$ASECWT) > 0) {
    imputation_pools[[code]] <- asec_subset %>% select(HHINCOME_2023, ASECWT)
  } else {
    imputation_pools[[code]] <- NULL
    warning("No ASEC records found (or zero total weight) for FAMINC code ", code, " in adjusted range [", round(min_val), ", ", round(max_val), "). Imputation will result in NA for this group.")
  }
}
rm(clean_data_asec); gc() 
print("... Imputation pools created.")


# ==== IIIE.4 Perform Probabilistic Imputation for Dec 2022 Data ====
print("--- Performing probabilistic imputation ---")

if (!var_faminc %in% names(clean_data_fss)) { stop("FAMINC column not found in FSS data for imputation.")}

impute_income <- function(faminc_code) {
  if (is.na(faminc_code)) return(NA_real_)
  pool_data <- imputation_pools[[as.character(faminc_code)]]
  if (is.null(pool_data) || nrow(pool_data) == 0 || sum(pool_data$ASECWT) <= 0) {
    return(NA_real_)
  } else {
    imputed_value <- sample(pool_data$HHINCOME_2023, size = 1, replace = TRUE, prob = pool_data$ASECWT)
    return(imputed_value)
  }
}

print("... Applying imputation function (may take time)...")
imputed_values <- sapply(clean_data_fss[[var_faminc]], impute_income) 
clean_data_fss$HHINCOME_IMP_2023 <- imputed_values

n_imputed <- sum(!is.na(clean_data_fss$HHINCOME_IMP_2023))
n_total <- nrow(clean_data_fss)
print(paste("... Imputation complete.", n_imputed, "out of", n_total, "records received an imputed income value."))
if (n_imputed < n_total) {
  print(paste("...", n_total - n_imputed, "records have NA imputed income (due to NA FAMINC or empty/zero-weight ASEC pool)."))
}

n_before_filter <- nrow(clean_data_fss)
clean_data_fss <- clean_data_fss %>% filter(!is.na(HHINCOME_IMP_2023))
n_after_filter <- nrow(clean_data_fss)
if (n_after_filter < n_before_filter) {
  print(paste("... Removed", n_before_filter - n_after_filter, "records with NA imputed income before grouping."))
}
if (n_after_filter == 0) { stop("FATAL ERROR: Zero rows remaining after filtering out failed imputations.")}


# ==== IIIE.5 LOAD INCOME BORDERS (External Borders from File I) ====
print("--- Loading External Income Borders ---")
if (!file.exists(borders_csv_file)) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: EXTERNAL Within-tercile borders file not found:", borders_csv_file)) }
borders_df <- readr::read_csv(borders_csv_file, show_col_types = FALSE)
print("... EXTERNAL Within-tercile borders loaded successfully.")
print("WARNING: Using income borders calculated from a different dataset/year (File I).")

if (!file.exists(main_cutoffs_rds_file)) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: EXTERNAL Main tercile cutoffs file not found:", main_cutoffs_rds_file)) }
loaded_main_cutoffs <- readRDS(main_cutoffs_rds_file)
if (is.list(loaded_main_cutoffs) && all(c("main_cutoff1", "main_cutoff2") %in% names(loaded_main_cutoffs))) {
  main_cutoff1_val <- loaded_main_cutoffs$main_cutoff1; main_cutoff2_val <- loaded_main_cutoffs$main_cutoff2
  print(paste("... EXTERNAL Main cutoffs loaded successfully: Cutoff1 =", main_cutoff1_val, ", Cutoff2 =", main_cutoff2_val))
} else { stop("FATAL ERROR: Loaded main cutoffs object does not have the expected structure.") }


# ==== IIIE.6 ASSIGN INCOME GROUPS (Using Imputed Income & External Borders) ====
print("--- Assigning Income Groups ---")

if (!exists("clean_data_fss")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: 'clean_data_fss' object not found.") }
if (!exists("borders_df")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: Borders data frame 'borders_df' not found.") }
income_variable_name <- "HHINCOME_IMP_2023" 
if (!income_variable_name %in% names(clean_data_fss)) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Imputed income variable '", income_variable_name, "' not found.")) }
weight_variable_name <- "FSSUPPWT"
if (!weight_variable_name %in% names(clean_data_fss)) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: FSS weight variable", weight_variable_name, "needed for temp design not found.")) }

data_for_design <- clean_data_fss %>% filter(!is.na(!!sym(weight_variable_name)) & !!sym(weight_variable_name) > 0)
if(nrow(data_for_design) < nrow(clean_data_fss)){
  warning("Removed ", nrow(clean_data_fss) - nrow(data_for_design), " rows with missing or non-positive weights before creating temp design.")
}
if(nrow(data_for_design) == 0) { stop("FATAL ERROR: No rows remaining after filtering for positive weights.")}

temp_design_for_groups <- survey::svydesign(ids=~1, data=data_for_design, weights=as.formula(paste0("~", weight_variable_name)))
print(paste("... Calling assign_income_groups function with detail level:", fine_group_detail_level, "and income var:", income_variable_name))
print("... Applying EXTERNAL borders to imputed Dec 2022 FSS income.")

temp_design_with_groups <- assign_income_groups(
  survey_obj = temp_design_for_groups,
  borders_df = borders_df, 
  income_var_name = income_variable_name, 
  detail_level = fine_group_detail_level, 
  main_cutoff1 = main_cutoff1_val, 
  main_cutoff2 = main_cutoff2_val  
)
data_with_groups <- temp_design_with_groups$variables
new_cols <- c("income_tercile", "fine_income_group")
if (all(new_cols %in% names(data_with_groups))) {
  print("... Successfully added 'income_tercile' and 'fine_income_group' columns (based on external borders).")
} else {
  warning("Could not find expected new columns ('income_tercile', 'fine_income_group'). Check assign_income_groups function.")
}
rm(temp_design_for_groups, temp_design_with_groups, borders_df, loaded_main_cutoffs, data_for_design, clean_data_fss); gc()


# ==== IIIE.7 CALCULATE DERIVED VARIABLES (Demographics, Food Security - Updated Codes/Logic) ====
print("--- Calculating Derived Variables (Codes Updated from Codebook Review) ---")
if (!exists("data_with_groups")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: 'data_with_groups' data frame not found.") }
data_final <- data_with_groups 
rm(data_with_groups); gc()

var_age <- "AGE"
var_race <- "RACE"
var_hispan <- "HISPAN"
var_sex <- "SEX"
var_fsstatus <- "FSSTATUS"

required_vars_derived <- c(var_age, var_race, var_hispan, var_sex, var_fsstatus)
missing_vars <- required_vars_derived[!required_vars_derived %in% names(data_final)]
if(length(missing_vars) > 0) {
  stop("FATAL ERROR: The following required variables for derived calculations are missing from data_final: ", paste(missing_vars, collapse=", "), ". Verify names in IIIE.0.3.")
}

data_final <- data_final %>% mutate(
  race_ethnicity = case_when(
    !is.na(!!sym(var_hispan)) & !!sym(var_hispan) != 0 ~ "Hispanic", 
    !is.na(!!sym(var_hispan)) & !!sym(var_hispan) == 0 & !!sym(var_race) == 100 ~ "White, Non-Hispanic", 
    !is.na(!!sym(var_hispan)) & !!sym(var_hispan) == 0 & !!sym(var_race) == 200 ~ "Black, Non-Hispanic", 
    !is.na(!!sym(var_hispan)) & !!sym(var_hispan) == 0 & !!sym(var_race) == 300 ~ "AIAN, Non-Hispanic", 
    !is.na(!!sym(var_hispan)) & !!sym(var_hispan) == 0 & !!sym(var_race) == 651 ~ "Asian, Non-Hispanic", 
    !is.na(!!sym(var_hispan)) & !!sym(var_hispan) == 0 ~ "Other/Multi, Non-Hispanic", 
    TRUE ~ NA_character_
  ),
  is_food_insecure = case_when(
    !!sym(var_fsstatus) %in% c(2, 3) ~ 1, 
    !is.na(!!sym(var_fsstatus)) ~ 0,      
    TRUE ~ NA_real_ 
  )
)
print("... Derived variables calculated using codes updated from codebook review.")


# ==== IIIE.8 CALCULATE GROUPED SUMMARY STATISTICS (Master Plan Specs - Using FSSUPPWT) ====
print("--- Calculating Grouped Summary Statistics (Master Plan Specs) ---")
if (!exists("data_final")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: data_final object not found.") }
grouping_vars_fine <- c("income_tercile", "fine_income_group")
if (!all(grouping_vars_fine %in% names(data_final))) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Fine grouping variables not found")) }

var_fssuppwt <- "FSSUPPWT"
if(!var_fssuppwt %in% names(data_final)) stop(paste("Weight variable", var_fssuppwt, "not found in data_final."))

print("... Calculating weighted means/proportions grouped by External Main Tercile and Fine Group...")

summary_stats_cps_fss <- data_final %>%
  filter(!is.na(income_tercile) & !is.na(fine_income_group)) %>%
  group_by(income_tercile, fine_income_group, .drop = FALSE) %>%
  summarise(
    n_unweighted_persons = n(),
    n_weighted_persons = sum(!!sym(var_fssuppwt), na.rm = TRUE),
    avg_hhincome_imp_2023 = weighted.mean(HHINCOME_IMP_2023, w = !!sym(var_fssuppwt), na.rm = TRUE),
    prop_food_insecure = weighted.mean(is_food_insecure, w = !!sym(var_fssuppwt), na.rm = TRUE),
    .groups = "drop" 
  )

print("... Weighted summary statistics calculated using dplyr.")
print("First few rows of final summary_stats_cps_fss:")
print(head(summary_stats_cps_fss))
print("Structure of final summary_stats_cps_fss:")
str(summary_stats_cps_fss)

attr(summary_stats_cps_fss, "analysis_note") <- paste(
  "Statistics based on Dec 2022 CPS FSS respondents with imputed income.",
  "Income imputed from FAMINC range based on CY2022 ASEC HHINCOME distribution, adjusted to 2023 dollars.",
  "Income groups based on EXTERNAL borders (File I). Analysis uses Dec 2022 FSSUPPWT.",
  "Food insecurity defined as FSSTATUS codes 2 (Low) or 3 (Very Low).",
  "Derived variables based on codes verified from codebook review.",
  "Standard errors NOT adjusted for complex survey design."
)
print(attr(summary_stats_cps_fss, "analysis_note"))


# ==== IIIE.9 SAVE SUMMARY RESULTS ====
print("--- Saving CPS FSS Summary Results ---")
if (!exists("summary_output_dir")) stop("FATAL ERROR: summary_output_dir not defined.")
if (!dir.exists(summary_output_dir)) dir.create(summary_output_dir, recursive = TRUE)

# MODIFIED FILENAME for compatibility with IV2_Combine_Summaries.R (v1.02.00.03)
output_rds_filename <- file.path(summary_output_dir, paste0("cps_fss_", cps_fss_sample_id, "_summary_stats_G_groups.rds"))
print(paste("... Target output RDS filename:", output_rds_filename))


if (exists("summary_stats_cps_fss") && !is.null(summary_stats_cps_fss) && nrow(summary_stats_cps_fss) > 0) {
  print(paste("... Saving CPS FSS summary results to:", output_rds_filename))
  tryCatch({ saveRDS(summary_stats_cps_fss, file = output_rds_filename) ; print("... Results saved successfully.") },
           error = function(e) { warning("Error saving summary results to RDS file: ", e$message) })
} else { warning("Summary statistics object 'summary_stats_cps_fss' is empty or does not exist. Skipping save.") }


# ==== IIIE.10 CLEAN UP MEMORY ====
print("--- Cleaning up CPS FSS Memory ---")
objects_to_remove_cps_fss <- c(
  "data_final", "summary_stats_cps_fss", "imputation_pools",
  "cps_fss_load_result", "asec_load_result"
)
objects_exist_cps_fss <- objects_to_remove_cps_fss[objects_to_remove_cps_fss %in% ls()]

if(length(objects_exist_cps_fss) > 0) {
  rm(list = objects_exist_cps_fss)
  print(paste("... Removed objects:", paste(objects_exist_cps_fss, collapse=", ")))
} else {
  print("... No large objects specified for removal found.")
}
gc()
print("==== Memory cleanup attempted. Script IIIE (Revised V2 - Codebook Fix) finished. ====")
# ====== End of Script ======

# ==== 0. ABOUT ====
# Project: 1/3 Country Project Visualizations
# Script:  IIIF_Process_CPS_EdSupp_Revised_V2_CodebookFix.R # <<< Updated Cleaning/Derived based on Codebook
# Author:  Maxwell Goshert / Janica Murphy / Gemini
# Date:    2025-04-21 20:05:00 (MST) # <<< Current Timestamp
# Location: Phoenix, Arizona, United States # <<< Current Location

# Purpose:
# This R script processes the CPS October Education Supplement (e.g., cps2023_10s).
# It estimates a continuous income value (HHINCOME_IMP_2023) for respondents
# based on their reported FAMINC range and the distribution of HHINCOME within corresponding
# ranges from the March 2023 ASEC (cps2023_03s), adjusted to 2023 dollars.
# A random seed is set for reproducibility.
# It then assigns income groups based on this imputed, inflation-adjusted income
# using pre-calculated EXTERNAL borders (from File I).
# It calculates summary statistics (child and adult enrollment rates)
# for the Education Supplement respondents using the Education Supplement weight (EDSUPPWT),
# aligned with the Master Viz & Data Plan v1. Code cleaning and derived variable
# logic have been updated based on codebook review.
# USER SHOULD STILL DOUBLE-CHECK FAMINC RANGES IF NEEDED.

# NOTE: This is File IIIF of V+ (Dataset Processing - CPS Ed Supp w/ Imputed Income)

# Workflow Steps Covered (Sections in this script):
# IIIF.0: Setup & Parameters
# IIIF.1: Get Data (Load Oct Ed Supp & Mar ASEC, Codebook)
# IIIF.2: Initial Clean & Prep (Separate for each dataset - Updated Codes)
# IIIF.2.5: Adjust ASEC Income & FAMINC Ranges for Inflation (to CY2023 dollars)
# IIIF.3: Prepare Imputation Pools from ASEC Data
# IIIF.4: Perform Probabilistic Imputation for Oct Ed Supp Data
# IIIF.5: Load Income Borders (External Borders from File I)
# IIIF.6: Assign Income Groups (Using Imputed Income & External Borders)
# IIIF.7: Calculate Derived Variables (Demographics, Enrollment - Updated Codes/Logic)
# IIIF.8: Calculate Grouped Summary Statistics (Master Plan Specs - Using EDSUPPWT)
# IIIF.9: Save Summary Table
# IIIF.10: Clean Up Memory

# ==== IIIF.0 SETUP & PARAMETERS ====

# --- Inflation Adjustment ---
# Income reported in Oct 2023 supplement likely refers to previous 12 months or calendar year.
# Assuming income reference period aligns roughly with CY2022 for consistency with ASEC donor.
# Adjusting CY2022 income to CY2023 dollars. USER VERIFY THIS ASSUMPTION.
ASEC_INCOME_YEAR <- 2022
REFERENCE_YEAR <- 2023

# Using BLS CPI-U Annual Averages - USER VERIFY/UPDATE if necessary
cpi_lookup <- list(
  "2022" = 292.655,
  "2023" = 304.702 # Example - update with final official value if needed
)
if (!as.character(ASEC_INCOME_YEAR) %in% names(cpi_lookup) || !as.character(REFERENCE_YEAR) %in% names(cpi_lookup)) {
  stop(paste("CPI value for ASEC income year", ASEC_INCOME_YEAR, "or reference year", REFERENCE_YEAR, "not found in cpi_lookup. Please update the list."))
}
cpi_asec_income_year <- cpi_lookup[[as.character(ASEC_INCOME_YEAR)]]
cpi_reference_year <- cpi_lookup[[as.character(REFERENCE_YEAR)]]
inflation_factor_2022_to_2023 <- cpi_reference_year / cpi_asec_income_year

# --- Function to Download and Load (Includes Codebook Generation) ---
download_and_load_ipums <- function(sample_id, variables, collection = "cps", description_suffix, output_parent_dir) {
  extract_description <- paste("1/3 Country Project -", description_suffix, "-", Sys.Date())
  print(paste("--- Processing Sample:", sample_id, "(Collection:", collection, ") ---"))
  if (!is.character(variables) || length(variables) == 0) { stop("FATAL ERROR: 'variables' must be a non-empty character vector.") }
  variables <- unique(variables)
  
  # Define Extract
  extract_def <- define_extract_micro(
    collection = collection, samples = sample_id, variables = variables, description = extract_description
  )
  print("... IPUMS extract definition created.")
  
  # Submit & Download
  submitted_extract <- tryCatch({ submit_extract(extract_def) },
                                error = function(e) { message("IPUMS API Error during submission for ", sample_id, ":"); message(e$message); stop("Extract submission failed.") })
  extract_number <- submitted_extract$number
  print(paste("... Extract submitted. Number:", extract_number))
  print("... Waiting for extract preparation...")
  Sys.sleep(10)
  downloadable_extract <- wait_for_extract(submitted_extract)
  print("... Extract is ready for download.")
  
  # Download Directory
  download_subdir_name <- paste0(toupper(collection), "_Extract_", extract_number, "_", Sys.Date())
  download_dir_specific <- file.path(output_parent_dir, download_subdir_name) # Use output_parent_dir
  if (!dir.exists(download_dir_specific)) {
    dir.create(download_dir_specific, recursive = TRUE)
    print(paste("... Created download subdirectory:", download_dir_specific))
  } else {
    print(paste("... Download subdirectory already exists:", download_dir_specific))
  }
  print(paste("... Downloading extract files to:", download_dir_specific))
  downloaded_files <- tryCatch({ download_extract(downloadable_extract, download_dir = download_dir_specific, overwrite = TRUE) },
                               error = function(e) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR during file download:", e$message)) })
  print("... Files downloaded.")
  
  # Find DDI
  ddi_file_path <- downloaded_files[grep("\\.xml$", downloaded_files)]
  if (length(ddi_file_path) == 0) {
    ddi_file_path <- list.files(path = download_dir_specific, pattern = paste0(collection, "_", sprintf("%05d", extract_number), "\\.xml$"), full.names = TRUE)
    if (length(ddi_file_path) == 0) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Could not find downloaded DDI (.xml) file in:", download_dir_specific)) }
  }
  ddi_file_path <- ddi_file_path[1]
  print(paste("... DDI file path identified:", ddi_file_path))
  
  # Generate Codebook
  codebook_output_filename <- file.path(download_dir_specific, paste0(gsub("\\.xml$", "", basename(ddi_file_path)), "_codebook.txt"))
  if(exists("generate_codebook_from_ddi") && is.function(generate_codebook_from_ddi)){
    print("... Generating text codebook...")
    generate_codebook_from_ddi(ddi_path = ddi_file_path, vars_list = variables, output_txt_path = codebook_output_filename)
  } else { warning("generate_codebook_from_ddi function not found. Skipping codebook generation.") }
  
  # Load Data
  raw_data <- read_ipums_micro(ddi = ddi_file_path, verbose = FALSE)
  print(paste("...", sample_id, "Data loaded successfully."))
  print(paste("Dimensions of raw data:", paste(dim(raw_data), collapse = " x ")))
  
  # Return list containing data and ddi path
  return(list(data = raw_data, ddi = ddi_file_path))
}
