enroll_codes_to_na <- c(99) # Updated: NIU
if (var_edatt %in% names(clean_data_edsupp)) {
  print(paste("... Cleaning", var_edatt, "- Setting code", paste(enroll_codes_to_na, collapse=", "), "to NA."))
  clean_data_edsupp <- clean_data_edsupp %>% mutate(!!sym(var_edatt) := if_else(!!sym(var_edatt) %in% enroll_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(!!sym(var_edatt))))
} else { warning(paste(var_edatt, "not found.")) }
if (var_edvoca %in% names(clean_data_edsupp)) {
  print(paste("... Cleaning", var_edvoca, "- Setting code", paste(enroll_codes_to_na, collapse=", "), "to NA."))
  clean_data_edsupp <- clean_data_edsupp %>% mutate(!!sym(var_edvoca) := if_else(!!sym(var_edvoca) %in% enroll_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(!!sym(var_edvoca))))
} else { warning(paste(var_edvoca, "not found.")) }

# Clean FAMINC - Updated NA codes based on codebook
faminc_codes_to_na <- c(995, 996, 997, 999) # Updated: Missing, Refused, DK, Blank
if (var_faminc %in% names(clean_data_edsupp)) {
  print(paste("... Cleaning", var_faminc, "- Setting codes", paste(faminc_codes_to_na, collapse=", "), "to NA."))
  clean_data_edsupp <- clean_data_edsupp %>% mutate(!!sym(var_faminc) := if_else(!!sym(var_faminc) %in% faminc_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(!!sym(var_faminc))))
} else { stop("FATAL ERROR: FAMINC variable not found in Ed Supp data.")}

# Clean HISPAN - Confirmed NA codes from codebook
hispan_codes_to_na <- c(901, 902) # Confirmed: DK, N/A
if (var_hispan %in% names(clean_data_edsupp)) {
  print(paste("... Cleaning", var_hispan, "- Setting codes", paste(hispan_codes_to_na, collapse=", "), "to NA."))
  clean_data_edsupp <- clean_data_edsupp %>% mutate(!!sym(var_hispan) := if_else(!!sym(var_hispan) %in% hispan_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(!!sym(var_hispan))))
} else { warning(paste("Variable '", var_hispan,"' not found.", sep="")) }

# Clean RACE - Confirmed NA code from codebook
race_codes_to_na <- c(999) # Confirmed: Blank
if (var_race %in% names(clean_data_edsupp)) {
  print(paste("... Cleaning", var_race, "- Setting code", paste(race_codes_to_na, collapse=", "), "to NA."))
  clean_data_edsupp <- clean_data_edsupp %>% mutate(!!sym(var_race) := if_else(!!sym(var_race) %in% race_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(!!sym(var_race))))
} else { warning(paste("Variable '", var_race, "' not found.", sep="")) }

# Clean SEX - Confirmed NA code from codebook
sex_codes_to_na <- c(9) # Confirmed: NIU
if (var_sex %in% names(clean_data_edsupp)) {
  print(paste("... Cleaning", var_sex, "- Setting code", paste(sex_codes_to_na, collapse=", "), "to NA."))
  clean_data_edsupp <- clean_data_edsupp %>% mutate(!!sym(var_sex) := if_else(!!sym(var_sex) %in% sex_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(!!sym(var_sex))))
} else { warning(paste(var_sex, "not found.")) }

# Ensure weight is numeric and positive
if (var_edsuppwt %in% names(clean_data_edsupp)) {
  print(paste("... Ensuring", var_edsuppwt, "is numeric."))
  clean_data_edsupp <- clean_data_edsupp %>% mutate(!!sym(var_edsuppwt) := as.numeric(!!sym(var_edsuppwt)))
  if (any(clean_data_edsupp[[var_edsuppwt]] <= 0, na.rm = TRUE)) { warning(paste("Non-positive values found in weight variable:", var_edsuppwt)) }
} else { stop(paste("Weight variable", var_edsuppwt, "not found in Ed Supp data."))}

# Ensure AGE is numeric
if (var_age %in% names(clean_data_edsupp)) {
  print(paste("... Ensuring", var_age, "is numeric."))
  clean_data_edsupp <- clean_data_edsupp %>% mutate(!!sym(var_age) := as.numeric(!!sym(var_age)))
} else {
  warning(paste("Age variable", var_age, "not found. Age filters will fail."))
}

# Select necessary columns
clean_data_edsupp <- clean_data_edsupp %>% select(any_of(cps_edsupp_variables_needed))
print("... Oct Ed Supp data cleaned.")

# ===== IIIF.2.2 Clean Mar 2023 ASEC Data =====
print("--- Cleaning Mar 2023 ASEC Data ---")
if (!exists("raw_data_cps_asec")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: raw_data_cps_asec object not found.") }
clean_data_asec <- raw_data_cps_asec
rm(raw_data_cps_asec); gc()

# Define ASEC variable names
var_hhincome_asec <- "HHINCOME"
var_asecwt <- "ASECWT"

# Clean HHINCOME (ASEC version) - Confirmed NA code from codebook review
income_codes_to_na_asec <- c(99999999) # Confirmed: Only NIU code listed
if (var_hhincome_asec %in% names(clean_data_asec)) {
  print(paste("... Cleaning ASEC", var_hhincome_asec, "- Setting code", paste(income_codes_to_na_asec, collapse=", "), "to NA."))
  clean_data_asec <- clean_data_asec %>% mutate(!!sym(var_hhincome_asec) := if_else(!!sym(var_hhincome_asec) %in% income_codes_to_na_asec, NA_real_, as.numeric(!!sym(var_hhincome_asec))))
} else { stop("FATAL ERROR: HHINCOME variable not found in ASEC data.") }

# Ensure ASEC weight is numeric and positive
if (var_asecwt %in% names(clean_data_asec)) {
  print(paste("... Ensuring ASEC", var_asecwt, "is numeric."))
  clean_data_asec <- clean_data_asec %>% mutate(!!sym(var_asecwt) := as.numeric(!!sym(var_asecwt)))
  if (any(clean_data_asec[[var_asecwt]] <= 0, na.rm = TRUE)) { warning(paste("Non-positive values found in weight variable:", var_asecwt)) }
} else { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: ASEC Weight variable", var_asecwt, "not found.")) }

# Filter ASEC data for valid weights and income (for donor pool)
clean_data_asec <- clean_data_asec %>%
  select(all_of(c(var_hhincome_asec, var_asecwt))) %>% # Keep only needed cols
  filter(!is.na(!!sym(var_asecwt)) & !!sym(var_asecwt) > 0 & !is.na(!!sym(var_hhincome_asec)))
print("... Mar 2023 ASEC data cleaned and filtered.")


# ==== IIIF.2.5 Adjust ASEC Income & FAMINC Ranges for Inflation (to CY2023 dollars) ====
print(paste("... Adjusting ASEC HHINCOME (CY2022) and FAMINC ranges for inflation using factor:", round(inflation_factor_2022_to_2023, 5)))

# Adjust ASEC HHINCOME
if ("HHINCOME" %in% names(clean_data_asec)) {
  clean_data_asec <- clean_data_asec %>%
    mutate(HHINCOME_2023 = HHINCOME * inflation_factor_2022_to_2023) %>%
    select(-HHINCOME) # Drop original CY2022 version
  print("... Created inflation-adjusted ASEC variable 'HHINCOME_2023'.")
} else {
  stop("HHINCOME not found in ASEC data, cannot proceed.")
}

# Adjust FAMINC range boundaries (Using ranges assumed same as Nov/Dec - USER VERIFY!)
faminc_ranges_adj <- lapply(faminc_ranges, function(range) {
  min_adj <- range[1] * inflation_factor_2022_to_2023
  max_adj <- ifelse(is.infinite(range[2]), Inf, range[2] * inflation_factor_2022_to_2023)
  return(c(min_adj, max_adj))
})
print("... Adjusted FAMINC range boundaries to 2023 dollars (USER MUST VERIFY RANGES FOR Ed Supp!).")


# ==== IIIF.3 Prepare Imputation Pools from ASEC Data ====
print("--- Preparing imputation pools from adjusted ASEC data ---")

# Check required variables exist
if (!"HHINCOME_2023" %in% names(clean_data_asec)) { stop("Adjusted ASEC income HHINCOME_2023 not found.")}
if (!"ASECWT" %in% names(clean_data_asec)) { stop("ASECWT not found in cleaned ASEC data.")}

imputation_pools <- list()
valid_faminc_codes <- names(faminc_ranges_adj)

for (code in valid_faminc_codes) {
  range_adj <- faminc_ranges_adj[[code]]
  min_val <- range_adj[1]
  max_val <- range_adj[2]
  
  # Subset ASEC data within the adjusted range
  if (is.infinite(max_val)) {
    asec_subset <- clean_data_asec %>% filter(HHINCOME_2023 >= min_val)
  } else {
    asec_subset <- clean_data_asec %>% filter(HHINCOME_2023 >= min_val & HHINCOME_2023 < max_val)
  }
  
  # Store the relevant income values and weights if subset is not empty
  if (nrow(asec_subset) > 0 && sum(asec_subset$ASECWT) > 0) {
    imputation_pools[[code]] <- asec_subset %>% select(HHINCOME_2023, ASECWT)
  } else {
    imputation_pools[[code]] <- NULL
    warning("No ASEC records found (or zero total weight) for FAMINC code ", code, " in adjusted range [", round(min_val), ", ", round(max_val), "). Imputation will result in NA for this group.")
  }
}
rm(clean_data_asec); gc() # Free memory from full ASEC file
print("... Imputation pools created.")


# ==== IIIF.4 Perform Probabilistic Imputation for Oct Ed Supp Data ====
print("--- Performing probabilistic imputation ---")

if (!var_faminc %in% names(clean_data_edsupp)) { stop("FAMINC column not found in Ed Supp data for imputation.")}

# Function to perform weighted random draw from an imputation pool
impute_income <- function(faminc_code) {
  if (is.na(faminc_code)) return(NA_real_)
  pool_data <- imputation_pools[[as.character(faminc_code)]]
  if (is.null(pool_data) || nrow(pool_data) == 0 || sum(pool_data$ASECWT) <= 0) {
    return(NA_real_)
  } else {
    imputed_value <- sample(pool_data$HHINCOME_2023, size = 1, replace = TRUE, prob = pool_data$ASECWT)
    return(imputed_value)
  }
}

# Apply the imputation function row-wise
print("... Applying imputation function (may take time)...")
imputed_values <- sapply(clean_data_edsupp[[var_faminc]], impute_income) # Use [[ ]] for safety
clean_data_edsupp$HHINCOME_IMP_2023 <- imputed_values

n_imputed <- sum(!is.na(clean_data_edsupp$HHINCOME_IMP_2023))
n_total <- nrow(clean_data_edsupp)
print(paste("... Imputation complete.", n_imputed, "out of", n_total, "records received an imputed income value."))
if (n_imputed < n_total) {
  print(paste("...", n_total - n_imputed, "records have NA imputed income (due to NA FAMINC or empty/zero-weight ASEC pool)."))
}

# Remove rows where imputation failed - necessary for grouping
n_before_filter <- nrow(clean_data_edsupp)
clean_data_edsupp <- clean_data_edsupp %>% filter(!is.na(HHINCOME_IMP_2023))
n_after_filter <- nrow(clean_data_edsupp)
if (n_after_filter < n_before_filter) {
  print(paste("... Removed", n_before_filter - n_after_filter, "records with NA imputed income before grouping."))
}
if (n_after_filter == 0) { stop("FATAL ERROR: Zero rows remaining after filtering out failed imputations.")}


# ==== IIIF.5 LOAD INCOME BORDERS (External Borders from File I) ====
print("--- Loading External Income Borders ---")
if (!file.exists(borders_csv_file)) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: EXTERNAL Within-tercile borders file not found:", borders_csv_file)) }
borders_df <- readr::read_csv(borders_csv_file, show_col_types = FALSE)
print("... EXTERNAL Within-tercile borders loaded successfully.")
print("WARNING: Using income borders calculated from a different dataset/year (File I).")

# Load main cutoffs here, just before calling assign_income_groups
if (!file.exists(main_cutoffs_rds_file)) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: EXTERNAL Main tercile cutoffs file not found:", main_cutoffs_rds_file)) }
loaded_main_cutoffs <- readRDS(main_cutoffs_rds_file)
if (is.list(loaded_main_cutoffs) && all(c("main_cutoff1", "main_cutoff2") %in% names(loaded_main_cutoffs))) {
  main_cutoff1_val <- loaded_main_cutoffs$main_cutoff1; main_cutoff2_val <- loaded_main_cutoffs$main_cutoff2
  print(paste("... EXTERNAL Main cutoffs loaded successfully: Cutoff1 =", main_cutoff1_val, ", Cutoff2 =", main_cutoff2_val))
} else { stop("FATAL ERROR: Loaded main cutoffs object does not have the expected structure.") }


# ==== IIIF.6 ASSIGN INCOME GROUPS (Using Imputed Income & External Borders) ====
print("--- Assigning Income Groups ---")

# Operates on the clean_data_edsupp data frame with imputed income
if (!exists("clean_data_edsupp")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: 'clean_data_edsupp' object not found.") }
if (!exists("borders_df")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: Borders data frame 'borders_df' not found.") }
income_variable_name <- "HHINCOME_IMP_2023" # Use imputed income
if (!income_variable_name %in% names(clean_data_edsupp)) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Imputed income variable '", income_variable_name, "' not found.")) }
# Use Oct Ed Supp weight for group assignment consistency
weight_variable_name <- "EDSUPPWT" # VERIFY NAME!
if (!weight_variable_name %in% names(clean_data_edsupp)) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Ed Supp weight variable", weight_variable_name, "needed for temp design not found.")) }

# Create a temporary survey object with Ed Supp weights
# Filter NAs/non-positive weights again just in case
data_for_design <- clean_data_edsupp %>% filter(!is.na(!!sym(weight_variable_name)) & !!sym(weight_variable_name) > 0)
if(nrow(data_for_design) < nrow(clean_data_edsupp)){
  warning("Removed ", nrow(clean_data_edsupp) - nrow(data_for_design), " rows with missing or non-positive weights before creating temp design.")
}
if(nrow(data_for_design) == 0) { stop("FATAL ERROR: No rows remaining after filtering for positive weights.")}

temp_design_for_groups <- survey::svydesign(ids=~1, data=data_for_design, weights=as.formula(paste0("~", weight_variable_name)))
print(paste("... Calling assign_income_groups function with detail level:", fine_group_detail_level, "and income var:", income_variable_name))
print("... Applying EXTERNAL borders to imputed Oct Ed Supp income.")

temp_design_with_groups <- assign_income_groups(
  survey_obj = temp_design_for_groups,
  borders_df = borders_df, # External borders
  income_var_name = income_variable_name, # Imputed income
  detail_level = fine_group_detail_level, # Uses "Groups_20"
  main_cutoff1 = main_cutoff1_val, # External cutoff
  main_cutoff2 = main_cutoff2_val  # External cutoff
)
# Extract the data frame with the new columns
data_with_groups <- temp_design_with_groups$variables
new_cols <- c("income_tercile", "fine_income_group")
if (all(new_cols %in% names(data_with_groups))) {
  print("... Successfully added 'income_tercile' and 'fine_income_group' columns (based on external borders).")
} else {
  warning("Could not find expected new columns ('income_tercile', 'fine_income_group'). Check assign_income_groups function.")
}
# Clean up intermediate objects, keep data_with_groups
rm(temp_design_for_groups, temp_design_with_groups, borders_df, loaded_main_cutoffs, data_for_design, clean_data_edsupp); gc()


# ==== IIIF.7 CALCULATE DERIVED VARIABLES (Demographics, Enrollment - Updated Codes/Logic) ====
print("--- Calculating Derived Variables (Codes Updated from Codebook Review) ---")
if (!exists("data_with_groups")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: 'data_with_groups' data frame not found.") }
data_final <- data_with_groups # Rename for consistency
rm(data_with_groups); gc()

# Define variable names used
var_age <- "AGE"
var_race <- "RACE"
var_hispan <- "HISPAN"
var_sex <- "SEX"
var_edatt <- "EDATT"
var_edvoca <- "EDVOCA"

# Check if variables exist before proceeding
required_vars_derived <- c(var_age, var_race, var_hispan, var_sex, var_edatt, var_edvoca);
missing_vars <- required_vars_derived[!required_vars_derived %in% names(data_final)]
if(length(missing_vars) > 0) {
  stop("FATAL ERROR: The following required variables for derived calculations are missing from data_final: ", paste(missing_vars, collapse=", "), ". Verify names in IIIF.0.3.")
}

# Calculate derived variables
data_final <- data_final %>% mutate(
  # Race/Ethnicity - Logic based on codebook review
  race_ethnicity = case_when(
    !is.na(!!sym(var_hispan)) & !!sym(var_hispan) != 0 ~ "Hispanic", # Confirmed: 0=No Hispanic
    !is.na(!!sym(var_hispan)) & !!sym(var_hispan) == 0 & !!sym(var_race) == 100 ~ "White, Non-Hispanic", # Confirmed: RACE 100=White
    !is.na(!!sym(var_hispan)) & !!sym(var_hispan) == 0 & !!sym(var_race) == 200 ~ "Black, Non-Hispanic", # Confirmed: RACE 200=Black
    !is.na(!!sym(var_hispan)) & !!sym(var_hispan) == 0 & !!sym(var_race) == 300 ~ "AIAN, Non-Hispanic", # RACE 300=American Indian/Aleut/Eskimo 
    !is.na(!!sym(var_hispan)) & !!sym(var_hispan) == 0 & !!sym(var_race) == 651 ~ "Asian, Non-Hispanic", # Confirmed: RACE 651=Asian Only
    !is.na(!!sym(var_hispan)) & !!sym(var_hispan) == 0 ~ "Other/Multi, Non-Hispanic", # Simplify remaining non-Hispanic
    TRUE ~ NA_character_
  ),
  
  # Child Enrollment Indicator (Age < 18) - Updated based on codebook review
  # EDATT: 1=Enrolled, 2=Not enrolled
  is_child_enrolled = case_when(
    !!sym(var_age) < 18 & !!sym(var_edatt) == 1 ~ 1,  # Confirmed: Age < 18 and EDATT=1 (Enrolled)
    !!sym(var_age) < 18 & !!sym(var_edatt) == 2 ~ 0,  # Confirmed: Age < 18 and EDATT=2 (Not enrolled)
    TRUE ~ NA_real_ # Includes adults (>=18) and those with NA EDATT (NIU set earlier)
  ),
  
  # Adult Formal Enrollment Indicator (Age >= 25) - Updated based on codebook review
  # EDATT: 1=Enrolled, 2=Not enrolled
  # EDVOCA: 1=No, 2=Yes
  is_adult_formal_enrolled = case_when(
    !!sym(var_age) >= 25 & (!!sym(var_edatt) == 1 | !!sym(var_edvoca) == 2) ~ 1, # Updated: Age >= 25 and EDATT=1 OR EDVOCA=2 (Yes)
    !!sym(var_age) >= 25 & (!is.na(!!sym(var_edatt)) | !is.na(!!sym(var_edvoca))) ~ 0, # If Age >= 25 and at least one status known and not enrolled above -> Not enrolled
    TRUE ~ NA_real_ # Includes younger adults (<25) and those with NA for both EDATT & EDVOCA (NIU set earlier)
  )
)
print("... Derived variables calculated using codes updated from codebook review.")


# ==== IIIF.8 CALCULATE GROUPED SUMMARY STATISTICS (Master Plan Specs - Using EDSUPPWT) ====
print("--- Calculating Grouped Summary Statistics (Master Plan Specs) ---")
if (!exists("data_final")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: data_final object not found.") }
grouping_vars_fine <- c("income_tercile", "fine_income_group")
if (!all(grouping_vars_fine %in% names(data_final))) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Fine grouping variables not found")) }

# Define weight variable
var_edsuppwt <- "EDSUPPWT" # Confirmed name
if(!var_edsuppwt %in% names(data_final)) stop(paste("Weight variable", var_edsuppwt, "not found in data_final."))

# --- Calculate Summaries using dplyr ---
print("... Calculating weighted means/proportions grouped by External Main Tercile and Fine Group...")

summary_stats_cps_edsupp <- data_final %>%
  filter(!is.na(income_tercile) & !is.na(fine_income_group)) %>%
  group_by(income_tercile, fine_income_group, .drop = FALSE) %>%
  summarise(
    # Counts (Optional)
    n_unweighted_persons = n(),
    n_weighted_persons = sum(!!sym(var_edsuppwt), na.rm = TRUE),
    
    # Income (Calculated from imputed income)
    avg_hhincome_imp_2023 = weighted.mean(HHINCOME_IMP_2023, w = !!sym(var_edsuppwt), na.rm = TRUE),
    
    # Enrollment Rates
    # Proportion of children (<18) enrolled
    prop_child_enrolled = weighted.mean(is_child_enrolled, w = !!sym(var_edsuppwt), na.rm = TRUE),
    
    # Proportion of adults (>=25) enrolled in formal education
    prop_adult_formal_enroll = weighted.mean(is_adult_formal_enrolled, w = !!sym(var_edsuppwt), na.rm = TRUE),
    
    .groups = "drop" # Drop grouping structure after summarise
  )

print("... Weighted summary statistics calculated using dplyr.")
print("First few rows of final summary_stats_cps_edsupp:")
print(head(summary_stats_cps_edsupp))
print("Structure of final summary_stats_cps_edsupp:")
str(summary_stats_cps_edsupp)

attr(summary_stats_cps_edsupp, "analysis_note") <- paste(
  "Statistics based on Oct Ed Supp respondents with imputed income.",
  "Income imputed from FAMINC range based on CY2022 ASEC HHINCOME distribution, adjusted to 2023 dollars.",
  "Income groups based on EXTERNAL borders (File I). Analysis uses Oct Ed Supp EDSUPPWT.",
  "Enrollment indicators based on EDATT/EDVOCA codes verified from codebook review.",
  "Standard errors NOT adjusted for complex survey design."
)
print(attr(summary_stats_cps_edsupp, "analysis_note"))


# ==== IIIF.9 SAVE SUMMARY RESULTS ====
print("--- Saving CPS Ed Supp Summary Results ---")
if (!exists("summary_output_dir")) stop("FATAL ERROR: summary_output_dir not defined.")
if (!dir.exists(summary_output_dir)) dir.create(summary_output_dir, recursive = TRUE)

# MODIFIED FILENAME for compatibility with IV2_Combine_Summaries.R (v1.02.00.03)
output_rds_filename <- file.path(summary_output_dir, paste0("cps_edsupp_", cps_edsupp_sample_id, "_summary_stats_G_groups.rds"))
print(paste("... Target output RDS filename:", output_rds_filename))


if (exists("summary_stats_cps_edsupp") && !is.null(summary_stats_cps_edsupp) && nrow(summary_stats_cps_edsupp) > 0) {
  print(paste("... Saving CPS Ed Supp summary results to:", output_rds_filename))
  tryCatch({ saveRDS(summary_stats_cps_edsupp, file = output_rds_filename) ; print("... Results saved successfully.") },
           error = function(e) { warning("Error saving summary results to RDS file: ", e$message) })
} else { warning("Summary statistics object 'summary_stats_cps_edsupp' is empty or does not exist. Skipping save.") }


# ==== IIIF.10 CLEAN UP MEMORY ====
print("--- Cleaning up CPS Ed Supp Memory ---")
objects_to_remove_cps_edsupp <- c(
  "data_final", "summary_stats_cps_edsupp", "imputation_pools",
  "cps_edsupp_load_result", "asec_load_result"
)
objects_exist_cps_edsupp <- objects_to_remove_cps_edsupp[objects_to_remove_cps_edsupp %in% ls()]

if(length(objects_exist_cps_edsupp) > 0) {
  rm(list = objects_exist_cps_edsupp)
  print(paste("... Removed objects:", paste(objects_exist_cps_edsupp, collapse=", ")))
} else {
  print("... No large objects specified for removal found.")
}
gc()
print("==== Memory cleanup attempted. Script IIIF (Revised V2 - Codebook Fix) finished. ====")
# ====== End of Script ======

# ==== 0. ABOUT ====
# Project: 1/3 Country Project Visualizations
# Script:  IIIG_Process_ATUS_Revised_V4_LoadFix.R # <<< Use read_ipums_micro_list for ATUS
# Author:  Max Goshert / Gemini
# Date:    2025-04-21 21:05:00 (MST) # <<< Current Timestamp
# Location: Phoenix, Arizona, United States # <<< Current Location

# Purpose:
# This R script processes the American Time Use Survey (ATUS) data (at2023).
# It uses read_ipums_micro_list to handle the hierarchical structure.
# It estimates a continuous income value (HHINCOME_IMP_2023) for respondents
# based on their reported FAMINCOME range (proxy, categorical) and the distribution of HHINCOME
# within corresponding ranges from the March 2023 ASEC (cps2023_03s), adjusted to 2023 dollars.
# ATUS FAMINCOME is assumed to represent CY2023 income.
# A random seed is set for reproducibility.
# It calculates the total time spent on educational activities per person using ACTIVITY/DURATION.
# It then assigns income groups based on imputed income using pre-calculated EXTERNAL borders.
# It calculates summary statistics (average hours in educational activities, avg imputed income)
# for ATUS respondents using the ATUS final weight (WT06), aligned with the Master Plan.
# USER MUST VERIFY ALL VARIABLE NAMES, FAMINCOME RANGES, ACTIVITY CODES, AND CLEANING CODES.

# NOTE: This is File IIIG of V+ (Dataset Processing - ATUS w/ Imputed Income & Time Use)

# Workflow Steps Covered (Sections in this script):
# IIIG.0: Setup & Parameters
# IIIG.1: Get Data (Load ATUS & Mar ASEC, Codebook) - Updated Load Function
# IIIG.2: Initial Clean & Prep (Separate for ATUS & ASEC - USER VERIFY CODES/NAMES)
# IIIG.2.5: Adjust ASEC Income & FAMINCOME Ranges for Inflation (to CY2023 dollars)
# IIIG.3: Prepare Imputation Pools from ASEC Data
# IIIG.4: Perform Probabilistic Imputation for ATUS Data
# IIIG.5: Calculate Time Use Summary (Educational Hours)
# IIIG.6: Merge Time Use Summary with Main ATUS Data
# IIIG.7: Load Income Borders (External Borders from File I)
# IIIG.8: Assign Income Groups (Using Imputed Income & External Borders)
# IIIG.9: Calculate Derived Variables (Demographics - USER VERIFY CODES)
# IIIG.10: Calculate Grouped Summary Statistics (Master Plan Specs - Using WT06)
# IIIG.11: Save Summary Table
# IIIG.12: Clean Up Memory

# ==== IIIG.0 SETUP & PARAMETERS ====

# ===== IIIG.0.1 Load Libraries =====

# Ensure packages are installed first if needed:
# install.packages(c("ipumsr", "dplyr", "readr", "stringr", "rlang", "tibble", "purrr", "survey", "here"))
library(ipumsr)
library(dplyr)
library(readr)
library(stringr)
library(rlang)
library(tibble)
library(purrr)
library(survey) # Needed for svydesign used in assign_income_groups helper
library(here)   # For robust file paths

print("... Libraries loaded.")

# ===== IIIG.0.2 Source Shared Functions =====
shared_functions_file <- here::here("II_Shared_Functions.R")
if (file.exists(shared_functions_file)) {
  source(shared_functions_file)
  print("... Shared functions sourced successfully.")
} else {
  stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Shared functions file not found:", shared_functions_file))
}

# ===== IIIG.0.3 Define Parameters =====

# --- Random Seed ---
current_seed_value <- 2025042101 # Use a consistent seed for testing
set.seed(current_seed_value)
print(paste("... Random seed set to", current_seed_value, "for imputation."))

# --- IPUMS Extract Definition ---
ipums_collection_atus <- "atus" # IPUMS Collection for ATUS
ipums_collection_asec <- "cps"  # IPUMS Collection for ASEC donor
atus_sample_id <- "at2023" # Corrected Sample ID per user feedback
cps_asec_sample_id <- "cps2023_03s"   # March 2023 ASEC Supplement (Donor Pool)
print(paste("... Target ATUS sample:", atus_sample_id))
print(paste("... Donor Pool ASEC sample:", cps_asec_sample_id))

# --- Inflation Adjustment ---
# ATUS FAMINCOME refers to CY2023 (User specified) -> No adjustment needed for ATUS FAMINCOME ranges
# ASEC income is CY2022, adjusted to CY2023 dollars.
ATUS_INCOME_YEAR <- 2023 # Corrected based on user feedback
ASEC_INCOME_YEAR <- 2022
REFERENCE_YEAR <- 2023

# Using BLS CPI-U Annual Averages - USER VERIFY/UPDATE if necessary
cpi_lookup <- list(
  "2022" = 292.655,
  "2023" = 304.702 # Example - update with final official value if needed
)
if (!as.character(ASEC_INCOME_YEAR) %in% names(cpi_lookup) || !as.character(REFERENCE_YEAR) %in% names(cpi_lookup)) {
  stop(paste("CPI value for ASEC income year", ASEC_INCOME_YEAR, "or reference year", REFERENCE_YEAR, "not found in cpi_lookup. Please update the list."))
}
cpi_asec_income_year <- cpi_lookup[[as.character(ASEC_INCOME_YEAR)]]
cpi_reference_year <- cpi_lookup[[as.character(REFERENCE_YEAR)]]
inflation_factor_asec <- cpi_reference_year / cpi_asec_income_year
# inflation_factor_atus is 1 since ATUS_INCOME_YEAR == REFERENCE_YEAR
inflation_factor_atus <- 1
print(paste("... Inflation Factor (ATUS", ATUS_INCOME_YEAR, " -> ", REFERENCE_YEAR, "):", round(inflation_factor_atus, 4)))
print(paste("... Inflation Factor (ASEC", ASEC_INCOME_YEAR, " -> ", REFERENCE_YEAR, "):", round(inflation_factor_asec, 4)))

# --- Variables Needed ---
# !!! ACTION NEEDED: Verify ALL variable names below against IPUMS documentation for BOTH samples !!!
# Variables from ATUS (e.g., atus2023) - Based on Master Plan & User Feedback
# In Section IIIG.0.3 Define Parameters, temporarily change:
# atus_variables_needed <- c("CASEID", "CPSIDP", "WT06", "ACTIVITY", "DURATION")
# atus_variables_needed2 <- c("CASEID", "CPSIDP", "WT06","FAMINCOME","ACTIVITY","DURATION")
# atus_variables_needed3 <- c("CASEID", "CPSIDP", "WT06","FAMINCOME","AGE","ACTIVITY","DURATION")
# atus_variables_needed4 <- c("CASEID", "CPSIDP", "WT06","FAMINCOME","AGE","SEX","ACTIVITY","DURATION")
# atus_variables_needed5 <- c("CASEID", "CPSIDP", "WT06","FAMINCOME","AGE","SEX","RACE","ACTIVITY","DURATION")
# atus_variables_needed6 <- c("CASEID", "CPSIDP", "WT06","FAMINCOME","AGE","SEX","RACE","HISPAN","ACTIVITY","DURATION")
atus_variables_needed7 <- c("CASEID", "CPSIDP", "WT06","FAMINCOME","AGE","SEX","RACE","HISPAN","EDUC","ACTIVITY","DURATION")

# atus_variables_needed <- c(

# ==== IIIG.1 GET DATA (Load ATUS & Mar ASEC, Codebook) ====

# --- Function to Download and Load (Includes Codebook Generation) ---
download_and_load_ipums <- function(sample_id, variables, collection, description_suffix, output_parent_dir) {
  extract_description <- paste("1/3 Country Project -", description_suffix, "-", Sys.Date())
  print(paste("--- Processing Sample:", sample_id, "(Collection:", collection, ") ---"))
  if (!is.character(variables) || length(variables) == 0) { stop("FATAL ERROR: 'variables' must be a non-empty character vector.") }
  variables <- unique(variables)
  
  # Define Extract
  # For ATUS, specify data_structure = "hierarchical" to get activity data
  
  if (collection == "atus") {
    message("Defining ATUS extract (hierarchical structure supported automatically by IPUMS API)")
    extract_def <- define_extract_micro(
      collection = collection,
      samples = sample_id,
      variables = variables,
      description = extract_description,
      data_structure = "hierarchical" # This is usually the default for ATUS; you can include if needed
    )
  } else {
    extract_def <- define_extract_micro(
      collection = collection,
      samples = sample_id,
      variables = variables,
      description = extract_description
    )
  }
  print("... IPUMS extract definition created.")

  
  # Submit & Download
  submitted_extract <- tryCatch({ submit_extract(extract_def) },
                                error = function(e) { message("IPUMS API Error during submission for ", sample_id, ":"); message(e$message); stop("Extract submission failed.") })
  extract_number <- submitted_extract$number
  print(paste("... Extract submitted. Number:", extract_number))
  print("... Waiting for extract preparation...")
  Sys.sleep(10)
  downloadable_extract <- wait_for_extract(submitted_extract)
  print("... Extract is ready for download.")
  
  # Download Directory
  download_subdir_name <- paste0(toupper(collection), "_Extract_", extract_number, "_", Sys.Date())
  download_dir_specific <- file.path(output_parent_dir, download_subdir_name) # Use output_parent_dir
  if (!dir.exists(download_dir_specific)) {
    dir.create(download_dir_specific, recursive = TRUE)
    print(paste("... Created download subdirectory:", download_dir_specific))
  } else {
    print(paste("... Download subdirectory already exists:", download_dir_specific))
  }
  print(paste("... Downloading extract files to:", download_dir_specific))
  downloaded_files <- tryCatch({ download_extract(downloadable_extract, download_dir = download_dir_specific, overwrite = TRUE) },
                               error = function(e) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR during file download:", e$message)) })
  print("... Files downloaded.")
  
  # Find DDI
  ddi_file_path <- downloaded_files[grep("\\.xml$", downloaded_files)]
  if (length(ddi_file_path) == 0) {
    ddi_file_path <- list.files(path = download_dir_specific, pattern = paste0(collection, "_", sprintf("%05d", extract_number), "\\.xml$"), full.names = TRUE)
    if (length(ddi_file_path) == 0) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Could not find downloaded DDI (.xml) file in:", download_dir_specific)) }
  }
  ddi_file_path <- ddi_file_path[1]
  print(paste("... DDI file path identified:", ddi_file_path))
  
  # Generate Codebook
  codebook_output_filename <- file.path(download_dir_specific, paste0(gsub("\\.xml$", "", basename(ddi_file_path)), "_codebook.txt"))
  if(exists("generate_codebook_from_ddi") && is.function(generate_codebook_from_ddi)){
    print("... Generating text codebook...")
    # Pass the DDI object directly if read_ipums_ddi is used, or the path
    generate_codebook_from_ddi(ddi_path = ddi_file_path, vars_list = variables, output_txt_path = codebook_output_filename)
  } else { warning("generate_codebook_from_ddi function not found. Skipping codebook generation.") }
  
  # Load Data using DDI path
  # Use read_ipums_micro_list for hierarchical data like ATUS
  if (collection == "atus") {
    print("... Loading ATUS data using read_ipums_micro_list...")
    raw_data <- read_ipums_micro_list(ddi = ddi_file_path, verbose = FALSE)
  } else {
    raw_data <- read_ipums_micro(ddi = ddi_file_path, verbose = FALSE)
  }
  print(paste("...", sample_id, "Data loaded successfully."))
  # Check if data is a list (expected for ATUS)
  if(is.list(raw_data) && !is.data.frame(raw_data)) {
    print(paste("... Loaded data is a list with", length(raw_data), "elements."))
    # Optionally print dimensions of each element
    # for(i in 1:length(raw_data)) { print(paste("Element", i, "dims:", paste(dim(raw_data[[i]]), collapse = " x "))) }
  } else if (is.data.frame(raw_data)) {
    print(paste("Dimensions of loaded data frame:", paste(dim(raw_data), collapse = " x ")))
  }
  
  # Return list containing data and ddi path
  return(list(data = raw_data, ddi = ddi_file_path))
}


# --- Load ATUS Data ---
atus_load_result <- download_and_load_ipums(
  sample_id = atus_sample_id,
  variables = atus_variables_needed,
  collection = ipums_collection_atus,
  description_suffix = paste("ATUS", atus_sample_id),
  output_parent_dir = extract_download_parent_dir
)
raw_data_atus <- atus_load_result$data
ddi_atus <- atus_load_result$ddi
# Note: raw_data_atus should be a list for ATUS data

# --- Load Mar 2023 ASEC Data ---
asec_load_result <- download_and_load_ipums(
  sample_id = cps_asec_sample_id,
  variables = cps_asec_variables_needed,
  collection = ipums_collection_asec,
  description_suffix = paste("CPS", cps_asec_sample_id, "ASEC Donor"),
  output_parent_dir = extract_download_parent_dir
)
raw_data_cps_asec <- asec_load_result$data # This will be a data frame

# ==== IIIG.2 INITIAL CLEAN & PREP (Separate for ATUS & ASEC) ====

# ===== IIIG.2.1 Clean ATUS Data =====
print("--- Cleaning ATUS Data ---")
if (!exists("raw_data_atus")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: raw_data_atus object not found.") }
# Handle the list structure returned by read_ipums_micro_list
# Find the respondent and activity data frames based on expected variable names
respondent_df_name <- NULL
activity_df_name <- NULL
if (is.list(raw_data_atus) && !is.data.frame(raw_data_atus)) {
  print("... Identifying respondent and activity data frames from the loaded list...")
  for (df_name in names(raw_data_atus)) {
    df_cols <- names(raw_data_atus[[df_name]])
    # Respondent file likely contains AGE, SEX, WT06, FAMINCOME etc.
    if (all(c("AGE", "WT06", "FAMINCOME") %in% df_cols)) { # Heuristic
      respondent_df_name <- df_name
      print(paste("... Found potential respondent data frame:", respondent_df_name))
    }
    # Activity file contains ACTIVITY, DURATION
    if (all(c("ACTIVITY", "DURATION") %in% df_cols)) {
      activity_df_name <- df_name
      print(paste("... Found potential activity data frame:", activity_df_name))
    }
  }
  if (is.null(respondent_df_name) || is.null(activity_df_name)) {
    stop("FATAL ERROR: Could not identify respondent and/or activity data frames in the loaded ATUS list.")
  }
  atus_respondent_data <- raw_data_atus[[respondent_df_name]]
  atus_activity_data <- raw_data_atus[[activity_df_name]]
} else if (is.data.frame(raw_data_atus)) {
  # This case should ideally not happen if ACTIVITY/DURATION were requested
  stop("FATAL ERROR: Loaded ATUS data as single data frame, expected list structure when requesting activity variables.")
} else {
  stop("FATAL ERROR: Loaded ATUS data is not in an expected format (list).")
}
rm(raw_data_atus); gc()

# --- Clean Respondent-Level Data ---
print("... Cleaning ATUS Respondent Data...")
# Define variable names
var_faminc_atus <- "FAMINCOME" # User specified
var_age_atus <- "AGE"
var_sex_atus <- "SEX"
var_race_atus <- "RACE"        # VERIFY NAME
var_hispan_atus <- "HISPAN"    # VERIFY NAME
var_educ_atus <- "EDUC"        # VERIFY NAME
var_wt06 <- "WT06"
var_caseid <- "CASEID"         # User specified HH ID
var_cpsidp_atus <- "CPSIDP"    # Corrected Person ID per user feedback

# Check if required identifiers are present
if (!var_caseid %in% names(atus_respondent_data)) warning(paste("Household ID variable", var_caseid, "not found."))
if (!var_cpsidp_atus %in% names(atus_respondent_data)) warning(paste("Person ID variable", var_cpsidp_atus, "not found."))


# Clean FAMINC (Assuming same NA codes as CPS supplements - USER MUST VERIFY!)
faminc_codes_to_na <- c(0, 995, 996, 997, 999) # Assumed: NIU, Missing, Refused, DK, Blank
if (var_faminc_atus %in% names(atus_respondent_data)) {
  print(paste("... Cleaning", var_faminc_atus, "- Setting codes", paste(faminc_codes_to_na, collapse=", "), "to NA. USER VERIFY!"))
  atus_respondent_data <- atus_respondent_data %>% mutate(!!sym(var_faminc_atus) := if_else(!!sym(var_faminc_atus) %in% faminc_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(!!sym(var_faminc_atus))))
} else { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR:", var_faminc_atus, "variable not found in ATUS data."))}

# Clean RACE (Assuming same NA codes as CPS - USER MUST VERIFY!)
race_codes_to_na <- c(999) # Assumed: Blank
if (var_race_atus %in% names(atus_respondent_data)) {
  print(paste("... Cleaning", var_race_atus, "- Setting code", paste(race_codes_to_na, collapse=", "), "to NA. USER VERIFY!"))
  atus_respondent_data <- atus_respondent_data %>% mutate(!!sym(var_race_atus) := if_else(!!sym(var_race_atus) %in% race_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(!!sym(var_race_atus))))
} else { warning(paste("Variable '", var_race_atus, "' not found.", sep="")) }

# Clean HISPAN (Assuming same NA codes as CPS - USER MUST VERIFY!)
hispan_codes_to_na <- c(901, 902) # Assumed: DK, N/A
if (var_hispan_atus %in% names(atus_respondent_data)) {
  print(paste("... Cleaning", var_hispan_atus, "- Setting codes", paste(hispan_codes_to_na, collapse=", "), "to NA. USER VERIFY!"))
  atus_respondent_data <- atus_respondent_data %>% mutate(!!sym(var_hispan_atus) := if_else(!!sym(var_hispan_atus) %in% hispan_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(!!sym(var_hispan_atus))))
} else { warning(paste("Variable '", var_hispan_atus,"' not found.", sep="")) }

# Clean SEX (Assuming same NA codes as CPS - USER MUST VERIFY!)
sex_codes_to_na <- c(9) # Assumed: NIU
if (var_sex_atus %in% names(atus_respondent_data)) {
  print(paste("... Cleaning", var_sex_atus, "- Setting code", paste(sex_codes_to_na, collapse=", "), "to NA. USER VERIFY!"))
  atus_respondent_data <- atus_respondent_data %>% mutate(!!sym(var_sex_atus) := if_else(!!sym(var_sex_atus) %in% sex_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(!!sym(var_sex_atus))))
} else { warning(paste(var_sex_atus, "not found.")) }

# Clean EDUC (Check for NIU/missing codes - e.g., 0, 1?)
educ_codes_to_na <- c(0, 1) # Example - VERIFY!
if (var_educ_atus %in% names(atus_respondent_data)) {
  print(paste("... Cleaning", var_educ_atus, "- Setting codes", paste(educ_codes_to_na, collapse=", "), "to NA. USER VERIFY!"))
  atus_respondent_data <- atus_respondent_data %>% mutate(!!sym(var_educ_atus) := if_else(!!sym(var_educ_atus) %in% educ_codes_to_na, NA_integer_, as.integer(!!sym(var_educ_atus))))
} else { warning(paste(var_educ_atus, "not found.")) }

# Ensure weight is numeric and positive
if (var_wt06 %in% names(atus_respondent_data)) {
  print(paste("... Ensuring", var_wt06, "is numeric."))
  atus_respondent_data <- atus_respondent_data %>% mutate(!!sym(var_wt06) := as.numeric(!!sym(var_wt06)))
  if (any(atus_respondent_data[[var_wt06]] <= 0, na.rm = TRUE)) { warning(paste("Non-positive values found in weight variable:", var_wt06)) }
} else { stop(paste("Weight variable", var_wt06, "not found in ATUS data."))}

# Ensure AGE is numeric
if (var_age_atus %in% names(atus_respondent_data)) {
  print(paste("... Ensuring", var_age_atus, "is numeric."))
  # Check ATUS codebook for specific AGE NA codes
  # age_codes_to_na_atus <- c(...)
  # atus_respondent_data <- atus_respondent_data %>% mutate(!!sym(var_age_atus) := if_else(!!sym(var_age_atus) %in% age_codes_to_na_atus, NA_real_, as.numeric(!!sym(var_age_atus))))
  atus_respondent_data <- atus_respondent_data %>% mutate(!!sym(var_age_atus) := as.numeric(!!sym(var_age_atus)))
} else {
  warning(paste("Age variable", var_age_atus, "not found."))
}

# Select necessary base variables (these should be unique per respondent already)
atus_base_vars_needed_distinct <- setdiff(atus_variables_needed, c("ACTIVITY", "DURATION"))
if (!var_caseid %in% atus_base_vars_needed_distinct) atus_base_vars_needed_distinct <- c(atus_base_vars_needed_distinct, var_caseid)
if (!var_cpsidp_atus %in% atus_base_vars_needed_distinct) atus_base_vars_needed_distinct <- c(atus_base_vars_needed_distinct, var_cpsidp_atus) # Corrected ID

clean_data_atus <- atus_respondent_data %>%
  select(any_of(atus_base_vars_needed_distinct))
# distinct(!!sym(var_caseid), !!sym(var_cpsidp_atus), .keep_all = TRUE) # Should already be distinct if loaded from respondent file

print(paste("... ATUS respondent data cleaned. Contains", nrow(clean_data_atus), "unique respondents."))

# --- Clean Activity Data (Minimal cleaning needed here) ---
print("... Cleaning ATUS Activity Data...")
var_activity <- "ACTIVITY"
var_duration <- "DURATION"
# Use the same IDs as above
if (is.null(atus_activity_data)) stop("ATUS activity data not found or identified.")
if (!var_caseid %in% names(atus_activity_data)) stop(paste("Household ID", var_caseid, "missing from activity data."))
if (!var_cpsidp_atus %in% names(atus_activity_data)) stop(paste("Person ID", var_cpsidp_atus, "missing from activity data.")) # Corrected ID
if (!var_activity %in% names(atus_activity_data)) stop(paste("Activity variable", var_activity, "not found."))
if (!var_duration %in% names(atus_activity_data)) stop(paste("Duration variable", var_duration, "not found."))

# Ensure DURATION is numeric, replace negative with 0? Or NA? Assume NA.
atus_activity_data <- atus_activity_data %>%
  mutate(
    !!sym(var_duration) := as.numeric(!!sym(var_duration)),
    !!sym(var_duration) := ifelse(!!sym(var_duration) < 0, NA_real_, !!sym(var_duration)),
    !!sym(var_activity) := as.character(!!sym(var_activity)) # Ensure character for matching
  ) %>%
  select(all_of(c(var_caseid, var_cpsidp_atus, var_activity, var_duration))) # Keep only needed cols, corrected ID

print("... ATUS activity data cleaned.")


# ===== IIIG.2.2 Clean Mar 2023 ASEC Data =====
print("--- Cleaning Mar 2023 ASEC Data ---")
if (!exists("raw_data_cps_asec")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: raw_data_cps_asec object not found.") }
clean_data_asec <- raw_data_cps_asec
rm(raw_data_cps_asec); gc()

# Define ASEC variable names
var_hhincome_asec <- "HHINCOME"
var_asecwt <- "ASECWT"

# Clean HHINCOME (ASEC version) - Confirmed NA code from codebook review
income_codes_to_na_asec <- c(99999999) # Confirmed: Only NIU code listed
if (var_hhincome_asec %in% names(clean_data_asec)) {
  print(paste("... Cleaning ASEC", var_hhincome_asec, "- Setting code", paste(income_codes_to_na_asec, collapse=", "), "to NA."))
  clean_data_asec <- clean_data_asec %>% mutate(!!sym(var_hhincome_asec) := if_else(!!sym(var_hhincome_asec) %in% income_codes_to_na_asec, NA_real_, as.numeric(!!sym(var_hhincome_asec))))
} else { stop("FATAL ERROR: HHINCOME variable not found in ASEC data.") }

# Ensure ASEC weight is numeric and positive
if (var_asecwt %in% names(clean_data_asec)) {
  print(paste("... Ensuring ASEC", var_asecwt, "is numeric."))
  clean_data_asec <- clean_data_asec %>% mutate(!!sym(var_asecwt) := as.numeric(!!sym(var_asecwt)))
  if (any(clean_data_asec[[var_asecwt]] <= 0, na.rm = TRUE)) { warning(paste("Non-positive values found in weight variable:", var_asecwt)) }
} else { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: ASEC Weight variable", var_asecwt, "not found.")) }

# Filter ASEC data for valid weights and income (for donor pool)
clean_data_asec <- clean_data_asec %>%
  select(all_of(c(var_hhincome_asec, var_asecwt))) %>% # Keep only needed cols
  filter(!is.na(!!sym(var_asecwt)) & !!sym(var_asecwt) > 0 & !is.na(!!sym(var_hhincome_asec)))
print("... Mar 2023 ASEC data cleaned and filtered.")


# ==== IIIG.2.5 Adjust ASEC Income & FAMINCOME Ranges for Inflation (to CY2023 dollars) ====
print(paste("... Adjusting ASEC HHINCOME (CY", ASEC_INCOME_YEAR, ") and ATUS FAMINCOME ranges (CY", ATUS_INCOME_YEAR, ") for inflation to", REFERENCE_YEAR, "dollars."))

# Adjust ASEC HHINCOME
if ("HHINCOME" %in% names(clean_data_asec)) {
  clean_data_asec <- clean_data_asec %>%
    mutate(HHINCOME_2023 = HHINCOME * inflation_factor_asec) %>% # Use ASEC inflation factor
    select(-HHINCOME) # Drop original CY2022 version
  print("... Created inflation-adjusted ASEC variable 'HHINCOME_2023'.")
} else {
  stop("HHINCOME not found in ASEC data, cannot proceed.")
}

# Adjust FAMINCOME range boundaries (Using ranges assumed same as CPS - USER VERIFY!)
# Using inflation_factor_atus (which is 1 since ATUS_INCOME_YEAR == REFERENCE_YEAR)
faminc_ranges_adj <- lapply(faminc_ranges, function(range) {
  min_adj <- range[1] * inflation_factor_atus
  max_adj <- ifelse(is.infinite(range[2]), Inf, range[2] * inflation_factor_atus)
  return(c(min_adj, max_adj))
})
print("... Adjusted FAMINCOME range boundaries to 2023 dollars (USER MUST VERIFY RANGES FOR ATUS!).")


# ==== IIIG.3 Prepare Imputation Pools from ASEC Data ====
print("--- Preparing imputation pools from adjusted ASEC data ---")

# Check required variables exist
if (!"HHINCOME_2023" %in% names(clean_data_asec)) { stop("Adjusted ASEC income HHINCOME_2023 not found.")}
if (!"ASECWT" %in% names(clean_data_asec)) { stop("ASECWT not found in cleaned ASEC data.")}

imputation_pools <- list()
valid_faminc_codes <- names(faminc_ranges_adj)

for (code in valid_faminc_codes) {
  range_adj <- faminc_ranges_adj[[code]]
  min_val <- range_adj[1]
  max_val <- range_adj[2]
  
  # Subset ASEC data within the adjusted range
  if (is.infinite(max_val)) {
    asec_subset <- clean_data_asec %>% filter(HHINCOME_2023 >= min_val)
  } else {
    asec_subset <- clean_data_asec %>% filter(HHINCOME_2023 >= min_val & HHINCOME_2023 < max_val)
  }
  
  # Store the relevant income values and weights if subset is not empty
  if (nrow(asec_subset) > 0 && sum(asec_subset$ASECWT) > 0) {
    imputation_pools[[code]] <- asec_subset %>% select(HHINCOME_2023, ASECWT)
  } else {
    imputation_pools[[code]] <- NULL
    warning("No ASEC records found (or zero total weight) for FAMINC code ", code, " in adjusted range [", round(min_val), ", ", round(max_val), "). Imputation will result in NA for this group.")
  }
}
rm(clean_data_asec); gc() # Free memory from full ASEC file
print("... Imputation pools created.")


# ==== IIIG.4 Perform Probabilistic Imputation for ATUS Data ====
print("--- Performing probabilistic imputation ---")

if (!var_faminc_atus %in% names(clean_data_atus)) { stop("FAMINCOME column not found in ATUS data for imputation.")}

# Function to perform weighted random draw from an imputation pool
impute_income <- function(faminc_code) {
  if (is.na(faminc_code)) return(NA_real_)
  pool_data <- imputation_pools[[as.character(faminc_code)]]
  if (is.null(pool_data) || nrow(pool_data) == 0 || sum(pool_data$ASECWT) <= 0) {
    return(NA_real_)
  } else {
    imputed_value <- sample(pool_data$HHINCOME_2023, size = 1, replace = TRUE, prob = pool_data$ASECWT)
    return(imputed_value)
  }
}

# Apply the imputation function row-wise
print("... Applying imputation function (may take time)...")
imputed_values <- sapply(clean_data_atus[[var_faminc_atus]], impute_income) # Use [[ ]] for safety
clean_data_atus$HHINCOME_IMP_2023 <- imputed_values

n_imputed <- sum(!is.na(clean_data_atus$HHINCOME_IMP_2023))
n_total <- nrow(clean_data_atus)
print(paste("... Imputation complete.", n_imputed, "out of", n_total, "records received an imputed income value."))
if (n_imputed < n_total) {
  print(paste("...", n_total - n_imputed, "records have NA imputed income (due to NA FAMINC or empty/zero-weight ASEC pool)."))
}

# Remove rows where imputation failed - necessary for grouping
n_before_filter <- nrow(clean_data_atus)
clean_data_atus_resp <- clean_data_atus %>% filter(!is.na(HHINCOME_IMP_2023)) # Keep respondent data separate
n_after_filter <- nrow(clean_data_atus_resp)
if (n_after_filter < n_before_filter) {
  print(paste("... Removed", n_before_filter - n_after_filter, "respondents with NA final imputed income before grouping."))
}
if (n_after_filter == 0) { stop("FATAL ERROR: Zero rows remaining after filtering out failed imputations.")}
rm(clean_data_atus); gc() # Remove original cleaned data


# ==== IIIG.5 Calculate Time Use Summary (Educational Hours) ====
print("--- Calculating Time Use Summary (Educational Hours) ---")

# Use the cleaned activity data frame
if (is.null(atus_activity_data)) stop("Cleaned ATUS activity data not found.")

# --- Calculate Total Education Minutes per Person ---
print(paste("... Summing duration for educational activities (prefix:", educ_activity_prefix, ")"))

educ_time_summary <- atus_activity_data %>%
  filter(stringr::str_starts(!!sym(var_activity), educ_activity_prefix) & !is.na(!!sym(var_duration))) %>% # Ensure duration is not NA
  group_by(!!sym(var_caseid), !!sym(var_cpsidp_atus)) %>% # Group by corrected IDs
  summarise(
    total_educ_minutes = sum(!!sym(var_duration), na.rm = TRUE),
    .groups = "drop"
  )

print(paste("... Calculated total education minutes for", nrow(educ_time_summary), "individuals with education activities."))

# Clean up activity data frame
rm(atus_activity_data); gc()

# ==== IIIG.6 Merge Time Use Summary with Main ATUS Data ====
print("--- Merging Education Time Summary with Respondent Data ---")

# Ensure the respondent data has the correct IDs for merging
if (!var_caseid %in% names(clean_data_atus_resp)) stop(paste("Household ID", var_caseid, "not found in respondent data for merging."))
if (!var_cpsidp_atus %in% names(clean_data_atus_resp)) stop(paste("Person ID", var_cpsidp_atus, "not found in respondent data for merging.")) # Corrected ID

# Perform left join to add total education minutes to each respondent
# Respondents with no education activities will get NA from the join
clean_data_atus_resp <- left_join(clean_data_atus_resp, educ_time_summary, by = c(var_caseid, var_cpsidp_atus)) # Corrected ID

# If someone had no education activities, their total minutes should be 0, not NA
clean_data_atus_resp <- clean_data_atus_resp %>%
  mutate(total_educ_minutes = ifelse(is.na(total_educ_minutes), 0, total_educ_minutes))

print("... Merged educational time summary. Respondents with no educational activity have 0 minutes.")
rm(educ_time_summary); gc()

# ==== IIIG.7 LOAD INCOME BORDERS (External Borders from File I) ====
print("--- Loading External Income Borders ---")
if (!file.exists(borders_csv_file)) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: EXTERNAL Within-tercile borders file not found:", borders_csv_file)) }
borders_df <- readr::read_csv(borders_csv_file, show_col_types = FALSE)
print("... EXTERNAL Within-tercile borders loaded successfully.")
print("WARNING: Using income borders calculated from a different dataset/year (File I).")

# Load main cutoffs here, just before calling assign_income_groups
if (!file.exists(main_cutoffs_rds_file)) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: EXTERNAL Main tercile cutoffs file not found:", main_cutoffs_rds_file)) }
loaded_main_cutoffs <- readRDS(main_cutoffs_rds_file)
if (is.list(loaded_main_cutoffs) && all(c("main_cutoff1", "main_cutoff2") %in% names(loaded_main_cutoffs))) {
  main_cutoff1_val <- loaded_main_cutoffs$main_cutoff1; main_cutoff2_val <- loaded_main_cutoffs$main_cutoff2
  print(paste("... EXTERNAL Main cutoffs loaded successfully: Cutoff1 =", main_cutoff1_val, ", Cutoff2 =", main_cutoff2_val))
} else { stop("FATAL ERROR: Loaded main cutoffs object does not have the expected structure.") }


# ==== IIIG.8 ASSIGN INCOME GROUPS (Using Imputed Income & External Borders) ====
print("--- Assigning Income Groups ---")

# Operates on the clean_data_atus_resp data frame with imputed income
if (!exists("clean_data_atus_resp")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: 'clean_data_atus_resp' object not found.") }
if (!exists("borders_df")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: Borders data frame 'borders_df' not found.") }
income_variable_name <- "HHINCOME_IMP_2023" # Use imputed income
if (!income_variable_name %in% names(clean_data_atus_resp)) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Imputed income variable '", income_variable_name, "' not found.")) }
# Use ATUS weight for group assignment consistency
weight_variable_name <- "WT06" # VERIFY NAME!
if (!weight_variable_name %in% names(clean_data_atus_resp)) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: ATUS weight variable", weight_variable_name, "needed for temp design not found.")) }

# Create a temporary survey object with ATUS weights
# Filter NAs/non-positive weights again just in case
data_for_design <- clean_data_atus_resp %>% filter(!is.na(!!sym(weight_variable_name)) & !!sym(weight_variable_name) > 0)
if(nrow(data_for_design) < nrow(clean_data_atus_resp)){
  warning("Removed ", nrow(clean_data_atus_resp) - nrow(data_for_design), " rows with missing or non-positive weights before creating temp design.")
}
if(nrow(data_for_design) == 0) { stop("FATAL ERROR: No rows remaining after filtering for positive weights.")}

temp_design_for_groups <- survey::svydesign(ids=~1, data=data_for_design, weights=as.formula(paste0("~", weight_variable_name)))
print(paste("... Calling assign_income_groups function with detail level:", fine_group_detail_level, "and income var:", income_variable_name))
print("... Applying EXTERNAL borders to imputed ATUS income.")

temp_design_with_groups <- assign_income_groups(
  survey_obj = temp_design_for_groups,
  borders_df = borders_df, # External borders
  income_var_name = income_variable_name, # Imputed income
  detail_level = fine_group_detail_level, # Uses "Groups_20"
  main_cutoff1 = main_cutoff1_val, # External cutoff
  main_cutoff2 = main_cutoff2_val  # External cutoff
)
# Extract the data frame with the new columns
data_with_groups <- temp_design_with_groups$variables
new_cols <- c("income_tercile", "fine_income_group")
if (all(new_cols %in% names(data_with_groups))) {
  print("... Successfully added 'income_tercile' and 'fine_income_group' columns (based on external borders).")
} else {
  warning("Could not find expected new columns ('income_tercile', 'fine_income_group'). Check assign_income_groups function.")
}
# Clean up intermediate objects, keep data_with_groups
rm(temp_design_for_groups, temp_design_with_groups, borders_df, loaded_main_cutoffs, data_for_design, clean_data_atus_resp); gc()


# ==== IIIG.9 CALCULATE DERIVED VARIABLES (Demographics, Education Hours - USER VERIFY CODES) ====
print("--- Calculating Derived Variables ---")
if (!exists("data_with_groups")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: 'data_with_groups' data frame not found.") }
data_final <- data_with_groups # Rename for consistency
rm(data_with_groups); gc()

# Define variable names used - USER VERIFY THESE FOR ATUS sample
var_age_atus <- "AGE"
var_race_atus <- "RACE" # VERIFY NAME!
var_hispan_atus <- "HISPAN" # VERIFY NAME!
var_sex_atus <- "SEX"

# Check if variables exist before proceeding
required_vars_derived <- c(var_age_atus, var_race_atus, var_hispan_atus, var_sex_atus, "total_educ_minutes") # Check for merged variable
missing_vars <- required_vars_derived[!required_vars_derived %in% names(data_final)]
if(length(missing_vars) > 0) {
  stop("FATAL ERROR: The following required variables for derived calculations are missing from data_final: ", paste(missing_vars, collapse=", "), ". Verify names and earlier steps.")
}

# Calculate derived variables
data_final <- data_final %>% mutate(
  # Race/Ethnicity
  # !!! ACTION NEEDED: Verify ATUS codes for RACE & HISPAN used here !!!
  race_ethnicity = case_when(
    !is.na(!!sym(var_hispan_atus)) & !!sym(var_hispan_atus) != 0 ~ "Hispanic", # Example: Assuming 0=No Hispanic
    !is.na(!!sym(var_hispan_atus)) & !!sym(var_hispan_atus) == 0 & !!sym(var_race_atus) == 100 ~ "White, Non-Hispanic", # Example: RACE 100=White
    !is.na(!!sym(var_hispan_atus)) & !!sym(var_hispan_atus) == 0 & !!sym(var_race_atus) == 200 ~ "Black, Non-Hispanic", # Example: RACE 200=Black
    !is.na(!!sym(var_hispan_atus)) & !!sym(var_hispan_atus) == 0 & !!sym(var_race_atus) == 651 ~ "Asian, Non-Hispanic", # Example: RACE 651=Asian Only
    !is.na(!!sym(var_hispan_atus)) & !!sym(var_hispan_atus) == 0 ~ "Other/Multi, Non-Hispanic", # Simplify remaining
    TRUE ~ NA_character_
  ),
  
  # Education Hours (Convert total minutes to hours)
  hours_educ = total_educ_minutes / 60
  
)
print("... Derived variables calculated (USER MUST VERIFY ALL CODES & LOGIC).")


# ==== IIIG.10 CALCULATE GROUPED SUMMARY STATISTICS (Master Plan Specs - Using WT06) ====
print("--- Calculating Grouped Summary Statistics (Master Plan Specs) ---")
if (!exists("data_final")) { stop("FATAL ERROR: data_final object not found.") }
grouping_vars_fine <- c("income_tercile", "fine_income_group")
if (!all(grouping_vars_fine %in% names(data_final))) { stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Fine grouping variables not found")) }

# Define weight variable
var_wt06 <- "WT06" # ATUS Final Weight
if(!var_wt06 %in% names(data_final)) stop(paste("Weight variable", var_wt06, "not found in data_final."))

# --- Calculate Summaries using dplyr ---
print("... Calculating weighted means/proportions grouped by External Main Tercile and Fine Group...")

summary_stats_atus <- data_final %>%
  filter(!is.na(income_tercile) & !is.na(fine_income_group)) %>%
  group_by(income_tercile, fine_income_group, .drop = FALSE) %>%
  summarise(
    # Counts (Optional)
    n_unweighted_persons = n(),
    n_weighted_persons = sum(!!sym(var_wt06), na.rm = TRUE),
    
    # Income (Calculated from imputed income)
    avg_hhincome_imp_2023 = weighted.mean(HHINCOME_IMP_2023, w = !!sym(var_wt06), na.rm = TRUE),
    
    # Average Hours in Educational Activities
    # Use the derived 'hours_educ' variable
    avg_hrs_educ_activity = weighted.mean(hours_educ, w = !!sym(var_wt06), na.rm = TRUE),
    
    .groups = "drop" # Drop grouping structure after summarise
  )

print("... Weighted summary statistics calculated using dplyr.")
print("First few rows of final summary_stats_atus:")
print(head(summary_stats_atus))
print("Structure of final summary_stats_atus:")
str(summary_stats_atus)

attr(summary_stats_atus, "analysis_note") <- paste(
  "Statistics based on ATUS respondents with imputed income.",
  "Income imputed from FAMINCOME range based on ASEC HHINCOME distribution, adjusted to 2023 dollars (ATUS FAMINCOME reference year assumed 2023).",
  "Income groups based on EXTERNAL borders (File I). Analysis uses ATUS WT06 weight.",
  "Educational hours calculated by summing DURATION for ACTIVITY codes starting with '06'.",
  "Derived variables based on placeholder codes - USER VERIFICATION REQUIRED.",
  "Standard errors NOT adjusted for complex survey design."
)
print(attr(summary_stats_atus, "analysis_note"))


# ==== IIIG.11 SAVE SUMMARY RESULTS ====
print("--- Saving ATUS Summary Results ---")
if (!exists("summary_output_dir")) stop("FATAL ERROR: summary_output_dir not defined.")
if (!dir.exists(summary_output_dir)) dir.create(summary_output_dir, recursive = TRUE)

# Use the defined output filename
if (exists("summary_stats_atus") && !is.null(summary_stats_atus) && nrow(summary_stats_atus) > 0) {
  print(paste("... Saving ATUS summary results to:", output_rds_filename))
  tryCatch({ saveRDS(summary_stats_atus, file = output_rds_filename) ; print("... Results saved successfully.") },
           error = function(e) { warning("Error saving summary results to RDS file: ", e$message) })
} else { warning("Summary statistics object 'summary_stats_atus' is empty or does not exist. Skipping save.") }


# ==== IIIG.12 CLEAN UP MEMORY ====
print("--- Cleaning up ATUS Memory ---")
objects_to_remove_atus <- c(
  "data_final", "summary_stats_atus", "imputation_pools",
  "atus_load_result", "asec_load_result", "atus_respondent_data" # Added respondent data
  # Keep extract definitions? Might be useful if debugging needed later
  # "atus_extract_def", "cps_asec_extract_def"
)
objects_exist_atus <- objects_to_remove_atus[objects_to_remove_atus %in% ls()]

if(length(objects_exist_atus) > 0) {
  rm(list = objects_exist_atus)
  print(paste("... Removed objects:", paste(objects_exist_atus, collapse=", ")))
} else {
  print("... No large objects specified for removal found.")
}
gc()
print("==== Memory cleanup attempted. Script IIIG (Revised V3 - User Fix) finished. ====")
# ====== End of Script ======

# ==== 0. ABOUT ====
# Project: 1/3 Country Project Visualizations
# Script:  IV_Combine_Summaries_Revised.R
# Version: 1.02.00.03 # <<< MODIFIED: Retains G-group structure (e.g., T1-G01), no aggregation/mapping to SG-groups.
# Author:  Maxwell Goshert / Janica Murphy / Gemini
# Date:    2025-06-03 (MST) # <<< Updated Date
# Location: Phoenix, Arizona, United States

# Purpose:
# This R script performs Stage 4 of the project workflow: Combining Summary Statistics.
# It reads individual summary statistics .rds files (assumed to be summarized at a
# G-group per tercile level, e.g., T1-G01 to T1-G04), adds a 'source_dataset' column,
# validates the G-group format, and then binds all valid data into a single master summary table.
# The 'fine_income_group' column in the output will directly reflect the G-group identifiers
# from the input files.
# Finally, it saves the combined data frame to a new .rds file.

# NOTE: This is File IV of V+ (Combine Summaries)

# Workflow Steps Covered (Sections in this script):
# IV.0: Setup & Parameters
# IV.1: Find Summary Files
# IV.2: Load and Combine Files
# IV.2.1: Validate and Prepare G-group Data (No SG-group aggregation/mapping)
# IV.3: Save Combined Data
# IV.4: Clean Up Memory

# ==== IV.0 SETUP & PARAMETERS ====

# ===== IV.0.1 Load Libraries =====
library(dplyr)
library(readr)
library(purrr)
library(stringr)
library(here)
library(tibble)

print("... Libraries loaded.")

# ===== IV.0.2 Define Parameters =====
summary_output_dir <- here::here("data", "summary_outputs")
print(paste("... Input/Output directory for summary statistics:", summary_output_dir))
# This pattern assumes input files are named with _G_groups.rds and contain G-groups per tercile.
rds_file_pattern_find <- "^(acs|cps_asec|nhis|cps_vote|cps_fss|cps_edsupp)_.*_G_groups\\.rds$"
print(paste("... Pattern to find summary RDS files:", rds_file_pattern_find))
combined_output_filename <- file.path(summary_output_dir, "combined_summary_stats_final_G_groups.rds") # Output reflects G-group structure
print(paste("... Output filename for combined data:", combined_output_filename))
print("... Parameters defined.")


# ==== IV.1 FIND SUMMARY FILES ====
print("--- Finding summary RDS files ---")
if (!dir.exists(summary_output_dir)) {
  stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: Summary output directory does not exist:", summary_output_dir))
}
summary_files <- list.files(
  path = summary_output_dir,
  pattern = rds_file_pattern_find,
  full.names = TRUE
)
if (length(summary_files) == 0) {
  stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: No summary RDS files found in", summary_output_dir, "matching the pattern:", rds_file_pattern_find,
             "\nEnsure your input files (from Scripts I-III) are summarized at the G-group per tercile level (e.g., T1-G01) and named with '_G_groups.rds'."))
}
print(paste("... Found", length(summary_files), "summary files to combine:"))
print(basename(summary_files))


# ==== IV.2 LOAD AND COMBINE FILES ====
print("--- Loading and combining summary files ---")
summary_files_named <- purrr::set_names(summary_files, basename(summary_files))
combined_summary_stats_raw <- purrr::map_dfr(summary_files_named, readRDS, .id = "source_filename")

if (nrow(combined_summary_stats_raw) > 0) {
  print("... Files loaded and initially combined.")
  source_prefixes <- c("acs", "cps_asec", "nhis", "cps_vote", "cps_fss", "cps_edsupp")
  prefix_pattern_extract <- paste0("^(", paste(source_prefixes, collapse = "|"), ")")
  
  combined_summary_stats_raw <- combined_summary_stats_raw %>%
    mutate(
      source_dataset = stringr::str_extract(source_filename, prefix_pattern_extract),
      .before = 1
    ) %>%
    select(-source_filename)
  print("... Added 'source_dataset' column.")
} else {
  warning("Combined data frame is empty after loading files. Check individual RDS files.")
  combined_summary_stats <- tibble() 
}

# ===== IV.2.1 VALIDATE AND PREPARE G-GROUP DATA (No SG-group aggregation/mapping) =====
print("--- Validating G-group data format and retaining G-groups ---")

if (exists("combined_summary_stats_raw") && nrow(combined_summary_stats_raw) > 0 &&
    "fine_income_group" %in% names(combined_summary_stats_raw) &&
    "income_tercile" %in% names(combined_summary_stats_raw)) {
  
  # Define the expected G-group pattern.
  # This assumes input G-groups are T[1-3]-G0[1-4] as per the context of previous script versions.
  # If your G-groups are numbered differently (e.g., up to G20), adjust this pattern.
  expected_g_group_pattern <- "^T([1-3])-G(0[1-4])$" 
  # For 20 G-groups per tercile, it would be: "^T([1-3])-G(\\d{2})$" and ensure G-numbers are 01-20.
  
  combined_summary_stats <- combined_summary_stats_raw %>%
    mutate(
      is_valid_g_group_format = stringr::str_detect(fine_income_group, expected_g_group_pattern)
    )
  
  n_invalid_format <- sum(!combined_summary_stats$is_valid_g_group_format, na.rm = TRUE)
  if (n_invalid_format > 0) {
    warning(paste(n_invalid_format, "rows found with 'fine_income_group' not matching the expected pattern ('", 
                  expected_g_group_pattern, "'). These rows will be removed.", sep=""))
    print("Examples of non-matching 'fine_income_group' values:")
    print(head(combined_summary_stats %>% filter(!is_valid_g_group_format) %>% distinct(fine_income_group)))
  }
  
  combined_summary_stats <- combined_summary_stats %>%
    filter(is_valid_g_group_format) %>% # Keep only rows with the expected G-group format
    select(-is_valid_g_group_format)   # Remove the intermediate validation column
  
  if (nrow(combined_summary_stats) == 0) {
    warning(paste("No data remained after filtering for the G-group format:", expected_g_group_pattern,
                  ". Check the 'fine_income_group' column in your input RDS files and the 'expected_g_group_pattern' in this script."))
    combined_summary_stats <- tibble() # Ensure it's an empty tibble if no data passes
  } else {
    print("... Valid G-group formatted data retained. No aggregation to SG-groups performed.")
    print(paste("Dimensions of final G-group table:", paste(dim(combined_summary_stats), collapse = " x ")))
  }
  
} else {
  if(exists("combined_summary_stats_raw") && nrow(combined_summary_stats_raw) == 0) {
    warning("Raw combined data frame was empty. No processing performed.")
    combined_summary_stats <- combined_summary_stats_raw 
  } else {
    warning("Prerequisite columns ('fine_income_group', 'income_tercile') not found in raw combined data, or raw data empty. Skipping processing.")
    if(exists("combined_summary_stats_raw")) combined_summary_stats <- combined_summary_stats_raw 
    else combined_summary_stats <- tibble()
  }
}

# Final checks
if (nrow(combined_summary_stats) > 0) {
  grouping_vars_check <- c("source_dataset", "income_tercile", "fine_income_group")
  if (!all(grouping_vars_check %in% names(combined_summary_stats))) {
    missing_group_vars <- grouping_vars_check[!grouping_vars_check %in% names(combined_summary_stats)]
    warning(paste("Combined data is missing one or more standard grouping columns after processing:", paste(missing_group_vars, collapse=", ")))
  }
  print("First few rows of final combined G-group table:")
  print(head(combined_summary_stats))
  print("Structure of final combined G-group table:")
  str(combined_summary_stats)
  print("Unique fine_income_group values in final table (should be T[1-3]-G[XX]):")
  print(sort(unique(combined_summary_stats$fine_income_group)))
  print("Unique income_tercile values in final table:")
  print(unique(combined_summary_stats$income_tercile))
} else {
  print("Final combined_summary_stats is empty.")
}


# ==== IV.3 SAVE COMBINED TABLE ====
print(paste("--- Saving combined G-group summary table to:", combined_output_filename, "---"))
if (exists("combined_summary_stats") && nrow(combined_summary_stats) > 0) {
  tryCatch({
    saveRDS(combined_summary_stats, file = combined_output_filename)
    print("... Combined G-group summary table saved successfully.")
  },
  error = function(e) {
    warning("Error saving combined summary results to RDS file: ", e$message)
  })
} else {
  warning("Combined summary table is empty or does not exist. Skipping save.")
}


# ==== IV.4 CLEAN UP MEMORY ====
print("--- Cleaning up memory ---")
# Only combined_summary_stats_raw was intermediate if it existed and was processed.
objects_to_remove <- c("summary_files", "summary_files_named", "combined_summary_stats_raw") 
objects_exist <- objects_to_remove[objects_to_remove %in% ls()]
if(length(objects_exist) > 0) {
  rm(list = objects_exist)
  print(paste("... Removed objects:", paste(objects_exist, collapse=", ")))
} else {
  print("... No intermediate objects specified for removal found.")
}
gc()
print("==== Memory cleanup attempted. Script IV finished. ====")
# ====== End of Script ======

# ==== 0. ABOUT ====
# Project: 1/3 Country Project Visualizations (Tercile-Based with Intra-Tercile Groups)
# Script:  V2_Visualization_Functions_Quartiles.R
# Version: 1.07.00.00 # <<< MODIFIED: Corrected internal variable name errors and finalized dual-axis plotting.
# Author:  Maxwell Goshert / Gemini
# Date:    2025-06-07 (MST) # Current date

# Purpose: Defines reusable functions for creating standard visualizations
#          based on 3 main terciles, with 4 fine-grained income groups within each.
#          These G-groups (e.g., T1-G01) are treated as the sub-groups for plotting.

# ==== 1. LOAD REQUIRED LIBRARIES ====
# Ensure these libraries are loaded in the main script calling these functions
# library(tidyverse);
library(readr); library(ggplot2); library(scales);
# library(stringr); library(here); library(ggtext); library(glue);
# library(grid); library(gridExtra); library(ggnewscale);

# ==== 1.5 HELPER FUNCTION TO EXTRACT LEGEND ====
get_legend <- function(plot) {
  plot_gtable <- tryCatch(ggplot2::ggplot_gtable(ggplot2::ggplot_build(plot)), error = function(e) NULL)
  if (is.null(plot_gtable)) return(grid::nullGrob())
  legend_index <- which(sapply(plot_gtable$grobs, function(x) x$name) == "guide-box")
  if (length(legend_index) > 0) return(plot_gtable$grobs[[legend_index]])
  else return(grid::nullGrob())
}

if (!exists("%||%", mode = "function")) {
  `%||%` <- function(a, b) { if (is.null(a) || length(a) == 0 || all(is.na(a))) b else a }
}

# ==== 2. SINGLE-LINE PLOT (3 TERCILES, 4 G-GROUPS EACH) ====
create_quartile_plot <- function( 
    combined_data,
    borders_data_path, 
    y_var_col,
    y_axis_label,
    plot_title,
    
    x_axis_title_text = "Population Distribution by Household Income",
    output_filename,
    output_dir,
    y_axis_format = "number",
    source_filter = NULL,
    border_t1_t2 = 64400, 
    border_t2_t3 = 130000, 
    t1_color = "#C0392B",  
    t2_color = "#F5B041",  
    t3_color = "#27AE60",  
    background_alpha = 0.2,
    vline_t1_t2_color = t2_color, 
    vline_t2_t3_color = t3_color, 
    vline_alpha = 0.25,
    base_font = "sans",
    
    line_color = "black",
    line_width = 0.7,
    loess_span = 0.75, 
    tercile_label_fontsize = 10, 
    xaxis_title_fontsize = 11,
    middle_row_height_npc = 0.06,
    bottom_row_height_npc = 0.04,
    intra_tercile_quantile_group = "Groups_4"
) {
  
  if (!file.exists(borders_data_path)) { stop("FATAL ERROR: Cannot find borders file:", borders_data_path) }
  required_cols <- c("source_dataset", "income_tercile", "fine_income_group", y_var_col)
  if (!all(required_cols %in% names(combined_data))) {
    missing_cols <- required_cols[!required_cols %in% names(combined_data)]
    stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: combined_data is missing required columns:", paste(missing_cols, collapse=", "), "for plot:", output_filename))
    
  }
  
  borders_data <- readr::read_csv(borders_data_path, show_col_types = FALSE)
  tercile_labels_in_borders_df <- borders_data %>%
    dplyr::filter(QuantileGroup == intra_tercile_quantile_group) %>%
    dplyr::mutate(tercile_group_label = stringr::str_trim(MainTercile)) %>%
    dplyr::distinct(tercile_group_label)
  t1_label <- tercile_labels_in_borders_df$tercile_group_label[stringr::str_detect(tercile_labels_in_borders_df$tercile_group_label, regex("Tercile 1|Bottom", ignore_case = TRUE))]
  t2_label <- tercile_labels_in_borders_df$tercile_group_label[stringr::str_detect(tercile_labels_in_borders_df$tercile_group_label, regex("Tercile 2|Middle", ignore_case = TRUE))]
  t3_label <- tercile_labels_in_borders_df$tercile_group_label[stringr::str_detect(tercile_labels_in_borders_df$tercile_group_label, regex("Tercile 3|Top", ignore_case = TRUE))]
  if(length(t1_label)!=1 ||
     length(t2_label)!=1 || length(t3_label)!=1) { stop("FATAL ERROR: Could not uniquely identify all three main tercile labels in borders file.") }
  
  max_income_t3_sg4_row <- borders_data %>%
    dplyr::filter(QuantileGroup == intra_tercile_quantile_group, MainTercile == t3_label) %>%
    dplyr::arrange(dplyr::desc(QuantileProbability)) %>%
    dplyr::slice(1)
  approx_max_income_overall <- if(nrow(max_income_t3_sg4_row) == 1 && is.finite(max_income_t3_sg4_row$CutoffValue)) {
    max_income_t3_sg4_row$CutoffValue * 1.2
  } else {
    border_t2_t3 * 2 
  }
  sub_group_borders_prepared <- borders_data %>%
    dplyr::filter(QuantileGroup == intra_tercile_quantile_group) %>%
    dplyr::mutate(tercile_group = stringr::str_trim(MainTercile)) %>%
    dplyr::arrange(tercile_group, QuantileProbability) %>%
    
    dplyr::group_by(tercile_group) %>%
    dplyr::mutate(within_tercile_sub_group_num = dplyr::row_number()) %>%
    dplyr::ungroup() %>%
    dplyr::mutate(
      quantile_position = dplyr::case_when(
        tercile_group == t1_label ~ within_tercile_sub_group_num,
        tercile_group == t2_label ~ 4 + within_tercile_sub_group_num,
        tercile_group == t3_label ~ 8 + within_tercile_sub_group_num,
        TRUE ~ NA_real_
      )
    ) %>%
    dplyr::select(quantile_position, upper_income_bound = CutoffValue) %>%
    dplyr::filter(!is.na(quantile_position)) %>%
    dplyr::bind_rows(
      tibble::tibble(quantile_position = 0, upper_income_bound = 0),
      tibble::tibble(quantile_position = 12, upper_income_bound = approx_max_income_overall)
    ) %>%
    dplyr::distinct(quantile_position, .keep_all = TRUE) %>%
    dplyr::arrange(quantile_position)
  
  plot_data <- combined_data %>%
    dplyr::filter(stringr::str_detect(fine_income_group, "^T[1-3]-G0[1-4]$")) %>%
    { if (!is.null(source_filter)) dplyr::filter(., source_dataset == !!source_filter) else .
    } %>%
    dplyr::select( tercile_group_raw = income_tercile, fine_group_raw = fine_income_group, y_value = !!rlang::sym(y_var_col) ) %>%
    dplyr::mutate(
      tercile_main_group = stringr::str_trim(tercile_group_raw),
      tercile_main_group = dplyr::case_when(
        stringr::str_detect(tercile_main_group, regex("1|Bottom", ignore_case = TRUE)) ~ t1_label,
        stringr::str_detect(tercile_main_group, regex("2|Middle", ignore_case = TRUE)) ~ t2_label,
        stringr::str_detect(tercile_main_group, regex("3|Top", ignore_case = TRUE)) ~ t3_label,
        TRUE ~ NA_character_
      ),
      
      intra_tercile_sub_group_num = as.numeric(stringr::str_extract(fine_group_raw, "(?<=G0)[1-4]$")),
      quantile_position = dplyr::case_when(
        tercile_main_group == t1_label ~ intra_tercile_sub_group_num,
        tercile_main_group == t2_label ~ 4 + intra_tercile_sub_group_num,
        tercile_main_group == t3_label ~ 8 + intra_tercile_sub_group_num,
        TRUE ~ NA_real_
      )
    ) %>%
    dplyr::select(tercile_main_group, quantile_position, y_value, fine_income_group = fine_group_raw) %>%
    dplyr::filter(!is.na(quantile_position) & !is.na(y_value)) %>%
    dplyr::arrange(quantile_position)
  
  if(nrow(plot_data) == 0) {
    warning(paste("No valid data rows remaining for G-groups. Plot:", output_filename))
    return(invisible(NULL))
  }
  
  critical_income_borders <- sort(unique(c(border_t1_t2, border_t2_t3)))
  other_desired_ticks <- c(10000, 25000, 75000, 200000, 500000)
  target_incomes_list <- as.list(critical_income_borders)
  min_income_separation <- 15000 
  
  for (tick in other_desired_ticks) {
    is_too_close <- any(abs(tick - unlist(target_incomes_list)) < min_income_separation)
    if (!is_too_close) {
      target_incomes_list <- c(target_incomes_list, tick)
    }
  }
  target_incomes <- sort(unique(unlist(target_incomes_list)))
  target_labels_raw <- scales::dollar(target_incomes, accuracy=1) 
  
  get_quantile_pos_12_groups <- function(inc_target, border_df_12_groups) {
    lower_bound_row <- border_df_12_groups %>% dplyr::filter(upper_income_bound < inc_target) %>% dplyr::slice_max(upper_income_bound, n = 1, with_ties = FALSE)
    upper_bound_row <- border_df_12_groups %>% dplyr::filter(upper_income_bound >= inc_target) %>% dplyr::slice_min(upper_income_bound, n = 1, with_ties = FALSE)
    if (nrow(lower_bound_row) == 0) return(min(upper_bound_row$quantile_position, 0.5, na.rm=TRUE))
    if (nrow(upper_bound_row) == 0) return(max(lower_bound_row$quantile_position, 12.5, na.rm=TRUE)) 
    pos_lower <- lower_bound_row$quantile_position;
    inc_lower <- lower_bound_row$upper_income_bound
    pos_upper <- upper_bound_row$quantile_position; inc_upper <- upper_bound_row$upper_income_bound
    if (inc_upper == inc_lower) return(pos_lower)
    frac <- (inc_target - inc_lower) / (inc_upper - inc_lower)
    return(max(0.5, min(12.5, pos_lower + frac * (pos_upper - pos_lower))))
  }
  income_label_positions <- purrr::map_dbl(target_incomes, ~ get_quantile_pos_12_groups(., sub_group_borders_prepared))
  x_axis_info <- tibble::tibble( breaks = income_label_positions, labels_raw = target_labels_raw, income = target_incomes ) %>%
    dplyr::arrange(breaks) %>%
    dplyr::mutate(
      is_border = (income %in% c(border_t1_t2, border_t2_t3)),
      break_diff = breaks - dplyr::lag(breaks, default = 
                                         -Inf)
    ) %>%
    dplyr::filter(break_diff > 0.5 | is_border) %>%
    dplyr::mutate(
      breaks = dplyr::case_when(
        income == border_t1_t2 ~ 4.5,
        income == border_t2_t3 ~ 8.5,
        TRUE ~ breaks
      ),
      labels = dplyr::case_when(
        is_border & income == border_t1_t2 ~ glue::glue("<span style='color:{vline_t1_t2_color};'>**{labels_raw}**</span>"),
        is_border & income == border_t2_t3 ~ glue::glue("<span style='color:{vline_t2_t3_color};'>**{labels_raw}**</span>"),
        TRUE ~ glue::glue("<span style='color:black;'>{labels_raw}</span>")
      )
    ) %>%
    dplyr::select(breaks, labels, income, is_border) %>%
    dplyr::distinct(breaks, .keep_all = TRUE)
  x_axis_info_no_borders <- x_axis_info %>% dplyr::filter(!is_border)
  x_axis_info_final <- x_axis_info %>% dplyr::select(breaks, labels)
  
  g <- ggplot2::ggplot(plot_data, ggplot2::aes(x = quantile_position, y = y_value)) +
    ggplot2::annotate("rect", xmin = 0.5, xmax = 4.5, ymin = -Inf, ymax = Inf, fill = t1_color, alpha = background_alpha) +
