    ggplot2::annotate("rect", xmin = 4.5, xmax = 8.5, ymin = -Inf, ymax = Inf, fill = t2_color, alpha = background_alpha) +
    ggplot2::annotate("rect", xmin = 8.5, xmax = 12.5, ymin = -Inf, ymax = Inf, fill = t3_color, alpha = background_alpha) +
    ggplot2::geom_vline(data = x_axis_info_no_borders, aes(xintercept = breaks), color = "grey95", linetype = "dotted", linewidth = 0.5) +
    ggplot2::geom_smooth(method = "loess", span = loess_span, se = FALSE, color = line_color, linewidth = line_width) +
    ggplot2::geom_vline(xintercept = 4.5, linetype = "dashed", color = vline_t1_t2_color, linewidth = 1, alpha = vline_alpha) +
    ggplot2::geom_vline(xintercept = 8.5, linetype = "dashed", color = vline_t2_t3_color, linewidth = 1, alpha = vline_alpha) +
    ggplot2::labs( title = plot_title, x = NULL, y = y_axis_label ) +
    ggplot2::scale_x_continuous(limits = c(0.5, 12.5), expand = c(0.02, 0.02), breaks = x_axis_info_final$breaks, labels = x_axis_info_final$labels) +
    { if (y_axis_format == "dollar") { ggplot2::scale_y_continuous(labels = scales::label_dollar(accuracy = 1), expand = expansion(mult = c(0.05, 0.05))) }
      else if (y_axis_format == "percent") { ggplot2::scale_y_continuous(labels = scales::label_percent(accuracy = 1), expand = expansion(mult = c(0.05, 0.05))) }
      else if (y_axis_format == "comma") { ggplot2::scale_y_continuous(labels = scales::label_comma(accuracy = 1), expand = expansion(mult = c(0.05, 0.05))) }
      else { ggplot2::scale_y_continuous(labels = scales::label_number(accuracy = 0.1), expand = expansion(mult = c(0.05, 0.05))) } } +
    ggplot2::theme_minimal(base_family = base_font) +
    ggplot2::theme(
      plot.title = ggplot2::element_text(hjust = 0.5, size = 14, face = "bold"),
      axis.title = ggplot2::element_text(size = 11, face = "bold"),
      axis.text = ggplot2::element_text(size = 10, color = "black"),
      axis.text.x = ggtext::element_markdown(size = 9, color = "black"),
      
      axis.ticks.x = ggplot2::element_blank(),
      legend.position = "none",
      panel.grid.minor = ggplot2::element_blank(),
      panel.grid.major.x = ggplot2::element_blank(),
      panel.grid.major.y = ggplot2::element_line(color = "grey90", linewidth = 0.4),
      plot.margin = ggplot2::margin(t = 10, r = 10, b = 5, l = 10)
    )
  label_grob_t1 <- grid::textGrob("Bottom Third Country", x = grid::unit(0.20, "npc"), y = grid::unit(0.5, "npc"), hjust = 0.5, vjust = 0.5, gp = grid::gpar(fontsize = tercile_label_fontsize, col = t1_color, fontfamily = base_font, fontface = "bold"))
  label_grob_t2 <- grid::textGrob("Middle Third Country", x = grid::unit(0.50, "npc"), y = grid::unit(0.5, "npc"), hjust = 0.5, vjust = 0.5, gp = grid::gpar(fontsize = tercile_label_fontsize, col = t2_color, fontfamily = base_font, fontface = "bold"))
  label_grob_t3 <- grid::textGrob("Top Third Country", x = grid::unit(0.80, "npc"), y = grid::unit(0.5, "npc"), hjust = 0.5, vjust = 0.5, gp = grid::gpar(fontsize = tercile_label_fontsize, col = t3_color, fontfamily = base_font, fontface = "bold"))
  labels_row_grob <- grid::gTree(children = grid::gList(label_grob_t1, label_grob_t2, label_grob_t3), name = "positioned_tercile_labels")
  x_title_style <- grid::gpar(fontsize = xaxis_title_fontsize, fontfamily = base_font, fontface = "bold")
  x_title_grob <- grid::textGrob(x_axis_title_text, gp = x_title_style, x = grid::unit(0.5, "npc"), hjust = 0.5)
  plot_height_npc <- 1 - middle_row_height_npc - bottom_row_height_npc
  layout_heights <- grid::unit(c(plot_height_npc, middle_row_height_npc, bottom_row_height_npc), "npc")
  final_arranged_plot <- gridExtra::grid.arrange(g, labels_row_grob, x_title_grob, ncol = 1, heights = layout_heights)
  plot_save_path <- file.path(output_dir, output_filename)
  ggplot2::ggsave(filename = plot_save_path, plot = final_arranged_plot, width = 10, height = 7, dpi = 300)
  invisible(g)
}

# ==== MULTI-LINE PLOT ====
create_multi_line_quartile_plot <- function(
    combined_data,
    borders_data_path,
    y_var_cols,
    category_labels = NULL,
    y_axis_label,
    plot_title,
    legend_title,
    x_axis_title_text = "Population Distribution by Household Income",
    output_filename,
    output_dir,
    y_axis_format = "number",
    source_filter = NULL,
    line_palette = NULL,
    border_t1_t2 = 64400, border_t2_t3 = 130000,
    t1_color = "#C0392B", t2_color = "#F5B041", t3_color = "#27AE60",
    background_alpha = 0.2,
    vline_t1_t2_color = t2_color, vline_t2_t3_color = t3_color,
    vline_alpha = 0.25,
    base_font = "sans",
    line_width = 0.7,
    loess_span = 0.75, 
    tercile_label_fontsize = 10,
    xaxis_title_fontsize = 11,
    middle_row_height_npc = 0.06,
    bottom_row_height_npc = 0.04,
    legend_row_height_npc = 0.1,
    legend_viewport_x = 0.5,
    legend_viewport_width = 0.9,
    intra_tercile_quantile_group = "Groups_4"
) {
  # ... (Input Validation, Load Borders, Prepare Borders for Interpolation - same as create_quartile_plot) ...
  if (!file.exists(borders_data_path)) stop("Borders file not found.")
  required_base_cols <- c("source_dataset", "income_tercile", "fine_income_group")
  if (!all(y_var_cols %in% names(combined_data))) {
    missing_y_cols <- y_var_cols[!y_var_cols %in% names(combined_data)]
    stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: One or more y_var_cols not found in combined_data:", paste(missing_y_cols, collapse=", ")))
  }
  if (!all(required_base_cols %in% names(combined_data))) {
    missing_base_cols <- required_base_cols[!required_base_cols %in% names(combined_data)]
    stop(paste("FATAL ERROR: One or more base columns not found in combined_data:", paste(missing_base_cols, collapse=", ")))
  }
  if (!is.null(category_labels) && length(category_labels) != length(y_var_cols)) {
    stop("Initial 'category_labels' length must match 'y_var_cols' length.")
  }
  borders_data <- readr::read_csv(borders_data_path, show_col_types = FALSE)
  tercile_labels_in_borders_df <- borders_data %>%
    dplyr::filter(QuantileGroup == intra_tercile_quantile_group) %>%
    dplyr::mutate(tercile_group_label = stringr::str_trim(MainTercile)) %>%
    dplyr::distinct(tercile_group_label)
  t1_label <- tercile_labels_in_borders_df$tercile_group_label[stringr::str_detect(tercile_labels_in_borders_df$tercile_group_label, regex("Tercile 1|Bottom", ignore_case = TRUE))]
  t2_label <- tercile_labels_in_borders_df$tercile_group_label[stringr::str_detect(tercile_labels_in_borders_df$tercile_group_label, regex("Tercile 2|Middle", ignore_case = TRUE))]
  t3_label <- tercile_labels_in_borders_df$tercile_group_label[stringr::str_detect(tercile_labels_in_borders_df$tercile_group_label, regex("Tercile 3|Top", ignore_case = TRUE))]
  if(length(t1_label)!=1 || length(t2_label)!=1 || length(t3_label)!=1) {
    stop("Could not uniquely identify all three main tercile labels in borders file for multi-line plot.")
  }
  max_income_t3_sg4_row <- borders_data %>% dplyr::filter(QuantileGroup == intra_tercile_quantile_group, MainTercile == t3_label) %>% dplyr::arrange(dplyr::desc(QuantileProbability)) %>% dplyr::slice(1)
  approx_max_income_overall <- if(nrow(max_income_t3_sg4_row) == 1 && is.finite(max_income_t3_sg4_row$CutoffValue)) { max_income_t3_sg4_row$CutoffValue * 1.2 } else { border_t2_t3 * 2 }
  sub_group_borders_prepared <- borders_data %>% dplyr::filter(QuantileGroup == intra_tercile_quantile_group) %>% dplyr::mutate(tercile_group = stringr::str_trim(MainTercile)) %>% dplyr::arrange(tercile_group, QuantileProbability) %>% dplyr::group_by(tercile_group) %>% dplyr::mutate(within_tercile_sub_group_num = dplyr::row_number()) %>% dplyr::ungroup() %>% dplyr::mutate(quantile_position = dplyr::case_when(tercile_group == t1_label ~ within_tercile_sub_group_num, tercile_group == t2_label ~ 4 + within_tercile_sub_group_num, tercile_group == t3_label ~ 8 + within_tercile_sub_group_num, TRUE ~ NA_real_ )) %>% dplyr::select(quantile_position, upper_income_bound = CutoffValue) %>% dplyr::filter(!is.na(quantile_position)) %>% dplyr::bind_rows(tibble::tibble(quantile_position = 0, upper_income_bound = 0), tibble::tibble(quantile_position = 12, upper_income_bound = approx_max_income_overall)) %>% dplyr::distinct(quantile_position, .keep_all = TRUE) %>% dplyr::arrange(quantile_position)
  
  # ===== 3.4 Prepare Plotting Data (Pivot Longer & Filter) =====
  data_for_pivot <- combined_data %>%
    dplyr::select(source_dataset, income_tercile, fine_income_group, dplyr::all_of(y_var_cols))
  
  # MODIFIED to parse "T[1-3]-G0[1-4]"
  plot_data_long_filtered <- data_for_pivot %>%
    tidyr::pivot_longer( cols = dplyr::all_of(y_var_cols), names_to = "category", values_to = "y_value" ) %>%
    dplyr::filter(stringr::str_detect(fine_income_group, "^T[1-3]-G0[1-4]$")) %>% # Expect G01-G04
    { if (!is.null(source_filter)) dplyr::filter(., source_dataset == !!source_filter) else . } %>%
    dplyr::mutate(
      tercile_main_group = stringr::str_trim(income_tercile),
      tercile_main_group = dplyr::case_when(
        stringr::str_detect(tercile_main_group, regex("1|Bottom", ignore_case = TRUE)) ~ t1_label,
        stringr::str_detect(tercile_main_group, regex("2|Middle", ignore_case = TRUE)) ~ t2_label,
        stringr::str_detect(tercile_main_group, regex("3|Top", ignore_case = TRUE)) ~ t3_label, TRUE ~ NA_character_ ),
      intra_tercile_sub_group_num = as.numeric(stringr::str_extract(fine_income_group, "(?<=G0)[1-4]$")), # Extract from G01-G04
      quantile_position = dplyr::case_when(
        tercile_main_group == t1_label ~ intra_tercile_sub_group_num,
        tercile_main_group == t2_label ~ 4 + intra_tercile_sub_group_num,
        tercile_main_group == t3_label ~ 8 + intra_tercile_sub_group_num, TRUE ~ NA_real_ )
    ) %>%
    dplyr::filter(!is.na(y_value) & !is.na(quantile_position))
  
  # ... (Rest of data prep for labels, X-axis, ggplot object, layout, save - remains the same as v1.05.00.02) ...
  if(nrow(plot_data_long_filtered) == 0) {
    warning(paste("No valid data rows for multi-line plot after filtering NAs:", output_filename))
    return(invisible(NULL))
  }
  actual_categories_in_filtered_data <- unique(plot_data_long_filtered$category)
  final_levels_for_factor <- y_var_cols[y_var_cols %in% actual_categories_in_filtered_data]
  if (length(final_levels_for_factor) == 0) {
    warning(paste("No categories from y_var_cols remain after filtering for plot:", output_filename, "- Plot will likely be empty or error."))
  }
  final_labels_for_factor <- NULL
  if (!is.null(category_labels)) {
    label_map <- stats::setNames(category_labels, y_var_cols)
    final_labels_for_factor <- label_map[final_levels_for_factor]
    final_labels_for_factor <- final_labels_for_factor[!is.na(final_labels_for_factor)]
  } else {
    final_labels_for_factor <- final_levels_for_factor
  }
  if (length(final_levels_for_factor) > 0 && length(final_labels_for_factor) != length(final_levels_for_factor)) {
    warning(paste("Label-level mismatch for plot", output_filename,". Defaulting to variable names for labels."))
    final_labels_for_factor <- final_levels_for_factor
  }
  plot_data_long <- plot_data_long_filtered %>%
    dplyr::filter(category %in% final_levels_for_factor) %>%
    dplyr::mutate(
      category_label = if (length(final_levels_for_factor) > 0) {
        factor(category, levels = final_levels_for_factor, labels = final_labels_for_factor)
      } else {
        factor(category)
      }
    ) %>%
    dplyr::select(tercile_main_group, quantile_position, category_label, y_value, fine_income_group) %>% # Keep fine_income_group
    dplyr::arrange(quantile_position, category_label)
  if(nrow(plot_data_long) == 0) {
    warning(paste("No data rows after final factor creation for multi-line plot:", output_filename))
    return(invisible(NULL))
  }
  
  critical_income_borders <- sort(unique(c(border_t1_t2, border_t2_t3)))
  other_desired_ticks <- c(10000, 25000, 75000, 200000, 500000)
  target_incomes_list <- as.list(critical_income_borders)
  min_income_separation <- 15000 
  
  for (tick in other_desired_ticks) {
    is_too_close_to_existing <- FALSE
    if (length(target_incomes_list) > 0) {
      for (existing_tick in target_incomes_list) {
        if (abs(tick - existing_tick) < min_income_separation) {
          is_too_close_to_existing <- TRUE
          break
        }
      }
    }
    if (!is_too_close_to_existing) {
      target_incomes_list <- c(target_incomes_list, tick)
    }
  }
  target_incomes <- sort(unique(unlist(target_incomes_list)))
  target_labels_raw <- scales::dollar(target_incomes, accuracy=1) 
  get_quantile_pos_12_groups_fn <- function(inc_target, border_df_12_groups) { 
    lower_bound_row <- border_df_12_groups %>% dplyr::filter(upper_income_bound < inc_target) %>% dplyr::slice_max(upper_income_bound, n = 1, with_ties=FALSE)
    upper_bound_row <- border_df_12_groups %>% dplyr::filter(upper_income_bound >= inc_target) %>% dplyr::slice_min(upper_income_bound, n = 1, with_ties=FALSE)
    if (nrow(lower_bound_row) == 0 && nrow(upper_bound_row) > 0) return(min(upper_bound_row$quantile_position, 0.5, na.rm=TRUE))
    if (nrow(upper_bound_row) == 0 && nrow(lower_bound_row) > 0) return(max(lower_bound_row$quantile_position, 12.5, na.rm=TRUE))
    if (nrow(lower_bound_row) == 0 && nrow(upper_bound_row) == 0) return(6.5)
    if (upper_bound_row$upper_income_bound == inc_target) return(upper_bound_row$quantile_position)
    if (nrow(lower_bound_row) > 0 && lower_bound_row$upper_income_bound == inc_target) return(lower_bound_row$quantile_position) 
    pos_lower <- lower_bound_row$quantile_position; inc_lower <- lower_bound_row$upper_income_bound
    pos_upper <- upper_bound_row$quantile_position; inc_upper <- upper_bound_row$upper_income_bound
    if (inc_upper == inc_lower) return(pos_lower)
    frac <- (inc_target - inc_lower) / (inc_upper - inc_lower); interpolated_pos <- pos_lower + frac * (pos_upper - pos_lower)
    return(max(0.5, min(12.5, interpolated_pos)))
  }
  income_label_positions <- purrr::map_dbl(target_incomes, ~ get_quantile_pos_12_groups_fn(., sub_group_borders_prepared))
  x_axis_info <- tibble::tibble( breaks = income_label_positions, labels_raw = target_labels_raw, income = target_incomes) %>% dplyr::arrange(breaks) %>% dplyr::mutate(is_border = (income %in% c(border_t1_t2, border_t2_t3)), break_diff = breaks - dplyr::lag(breaks, default = -Inf)) %>% dplyr::filter(break_diff > 0.5 | is_border) %>% dplyr::mutate(breaks = dplyr::case_when(income == border_t1_t2 ~ 4.5, income == border_t2_t3 ~ 8.5, TRUE ~ breaks )) %>% dplyr::mutate(labels = dplyr::case_when(is_border & income == border_t1_t2 ~ glue::glue("<span style='color:{vline_t1_t2_color};'>**{labels_raw}**</span>"), is_border & income == border_t2_t3 ~ glue::glue("<span style='color:{vline_t2_t3_color};'>**{labels_raw}**</span>"), TRUE ~ glue::glue("<span style='color:black;'>{labels_raw}</span>"))) %>% dplyr::select(breaks, labels, income, is_border) %>% dplyr::distinct(breaks, .keep_all = TRUE)
  x_axis_info_no_borders <- x_axis_info %>% dplyr::filter(!is_border)
  x_axis_info_final <- x_axis_info %>% dplyr::select(breaks, labels)
  
  g_with_legend <- ggplot2::ggplot(plot_data_long, ggplot2::aes(x = quantile_position, y = y_value, color = category_label)) +
    ggplot2::annotate("rect", xmin = 0.5, xmax = 4.5, ymin = -Inf, ymax = Inf, fill = t1_color, alpha = background_alpha) +
    ggplot2::annotate("rect", xmin = 4.5, xmax = 8.5, ymin = -Inf, ymax = Inf, fill = t2_color, alpha = background_alpha) +
    ggplot2::annotate("rect", xmin = 8.5, xmax = 12.5, ymin = -Inf, ymax = Inf, fill = t3_color, alpha = background_alpha) +
    ggplot2::geom_vline(data = x_axis_info_no_borders, aes(xintercept = breaks), color = "grey95", linetype = "dotted", linewidth = 0.5, inherit.aes = FALSE) +
    ggplot2::geom_smooth(method = "loess", span = loess_span, se = FALSE, linewidth = line_width, aes(group = category_label)) +
    ggplot2::geom_vline(xintercept = 4.5, linetype = "dashed", color = vline_t1_t2_color, linewidth = 1, alpha = vline_alpha) +
    ggplot2::geom_vline(xintercept = 8.5, linetype = "dashed", color = vline_t2_t3_color, linewidth = 1, alpha = vline_alpha) +
    ggplot2::labs( title = plot_title, x = NULL, y = y_axis_label, color = legend_title ) +
    ggplot2::scale_x_continuous( limits = c(0.5, 12.5), expand = c(0.02, 0.02), breaks = x_axis_info_final$breaks, labels = x_axis_info_final$labels ) +
    { if (y_axis_format == "dollar") { ggplot2::scale_y_continuous(labels = scales::label_dollar(accuracy = 1), expand = expansion(mult = c(0.05, 0.05))) }
      else if (y_axis_format == "percent") { ggplot2::scale_y_continuous(labels = scales::label_percent(accuracy = 1), expand = expansion(mult = c(0.05, 0.05))) }
      else if (y_axis_format == "comma") { ggplot2::scale_y_continuous(labels = scales::label_comma(accuracy = 1), expand = expansion(mult = c(0.05, 0.05))) }
      else { ggplot2::scale_y_continuous(labels = scales::label_number(accuracy = 0.1), expand = expansion(mult = c(0.05, 0.05))) } } +
    { if(!is.null(line_palette)) {
      if (length(final_labels_for_factor) > 0 && length(line_palette) >= length(final_labels_for_factor)) {
        ggplot2::scale_color_manual(values = line_palette[1:length(final_labels_for_factor)], name = legend_title, breaks = final_labels_for_factor, labels = final_labels_for_factor)
      } else {
        ggplot2::scale_color_manual(values = line_palette, name = legend_title, labels = final_labels_for_factor)
      }
    } else {
      ggplot2::scale_color_discrete(name = legend_title, breaks = final_labels_for_factor, labels = final_labels_for_factor)
    }} +
    ggplot2::theme_minimal(base_family = base_font) +
    ggplot2::theme(
      plot.title = ggplot2::element_text(hjust = 0.5, size = 14, face = "bold"),
      axis.title = ggplot2::element_text(size = 11, face = "bold"), axis.title.x = element_blank(),
      axis.text = ggplot2::element_text(size = 10, color = "black"),
      axis.text.x = ggtext::element_markdown(size = 9, color = "black"),
      axis.ticks.x = ggplot2::element_blank(), legend.position = "bottom",
      legend.title = ggplot2::element_text(face = "bold"), legend.justification = "center",
      legend.box.margin = margin(t = 5, b = 5), panel.grid.minor = ggplot2::element_blank(),
      panel.grid.major.x = ggplot2::element_blank(),
      panel.grid.major.y = ggplot2::element_line(color = "grey90", linewidth = 0.4),
      plot.margin = ggplot2::margin(t = 10, r = 10, b = 5, l = 10)
    )
  plot_legend <- get_legend(g_with_legend)
  g_no_legend <- g_with_legend + theme(legend.position = "none")
  label_grob_t1 <- grid::textGrob("Bottom Third Country", x = grid::unit(0.20, "npc"), y = grid::unit(0.5, "npc"), hjust = 0.5, vjust = 0.5, gp = grid::gpar(fontsize = tercile_label_fontsize, col = t1_color, fontfamily = base_font, fontface = "bold"))
  label_grob_t2 <- grid::textGrob("Middle Third Country", x = grid::unit(0.50, "npc"), y = grid::unit(0.5, "npc"), hjust = 0.5, vjust = 0.5, gp = grid::gpar(fontsize = tercile_label_fontsize, col = t2_color, fontfamily = base_font, fontface = "bold"))
  label_grob_t3 <- grid::textGrob("Top Third Country",    x = grid::unit(0.80, "npc"), y = grid::unit(0.5, "npc"), hjust = 0.5, vjust = 0.5, gp = grid::gpar(fontsize = tercile_label_fontsize, col = t3_color, fontfamily = base_font, fontface = "bold"))
  labels_row_grob <- grid::gTree(children = grid::gList(label_grob_t1, label_grob_t2, label_grob_t3), name = "positioned_tercile_labels")
  x_title_style <- grid::gpar(fontsize = xaxis_title_fontsize, fontfamily = base_font, fontface = "bold")
  x_title_grob <- grid::textGrob(x_axis_title_text, gp = x_title_style, x = grid::unit(0.5, "npc"), hjust = 0.5)
  plot_height_npc <- 1 - middle_row_height_npc - bottom_row_height_npc - legend_row_height_npc
  if(plot_height_npc < 0) {
    warning("Row heights for multi-line plot sum to > 1. Adjusting.")
    total_extra_height = middle_row_height_npc + bottom_row_height_npc + legend_row_height_npc
    middle_row_height_npc <- middle_row_height_npc / total_extra_height * 0.15
    bottom_row_height_npc <- bottom_row_height_npc / total_extra_height * 0.1
    legend_row_height_npc <- legend_row_height_npc / total_extra_height * 0.25
    plot_height_npc <- 1 - middle_row_height_npc - bottom_row_height_npc - legend_row_height_npc
  }
  layout_heights <- grid::unit(c(plot_height_npc, middle_row_height_npc, bottom_row_height_npc, legend_row_height_npc), "npc")
  legend_vp <- grid::viewport( x = unit(legend_viewport_x, "npc"), width = unit(legend_viewport_width, "npc"), just = "center" )
  legend_grob_positioned <- grid::gTree(children = grid::gList(plot_legend), vp = legend_vp)
  final_arranged_plot <- gridExtra::grid.arrange(
    g_no_legend, labels_row_grob, x_title_grob, legend_grob_positioned,
    ncol = 1, heights = layout_heights
  )
  plot_save_path <- file.path(output_dir, output_filename)
  print(paste("Saving multi-line 3-Tercile (4 G-groups each) plot to:", plot_save_path)) # Updated comment
  ggplot2::ggsave(filename = plot_save_path, plot = final_arranged_plot, width = 10, height = 7, dpi = 300)
  print("... Multi-line plot saved.")
  invisible(g_no_legend)
}

# ==== DUAL-AXIS PLOT (Corrected) ====
create_dual_axis_quartile_plot <- function(
    combined_data, borders_data_path, y1_var_cols, y2_var_col,
    y1_category_labels = NULL, y2_category_label = NULL,
    y1_axis_label, y2_axis_label, plot_title, legend_title,
    x_axis_title_text = "Population Distribution by Household Income",
    output_filename, output_dir, y1_axis_format = "number", y2_axis_format = "number",
    source_filter = NULL, line1_palette = NULL, line2_color = "#E74C3C",
    loess_span = 0.75, line_width = 0.7, ext_point_size_global = 1.8,
    ext_point_alpha_global = 0.7, intra_tercile_quantile_group = "Groups_4", ...
) {
  # --- Input Validation & Parameter Setup ---
  common_args <- list(...)
  border_t1_t2 <- common_args$border_t1_t2 %||% 64400
  border_t2_t3 <- common_args$border_t2_t3 %||% 130000
  t1_color <- common_args$t1_color %||% "#C0392B"
  t2_color <- common_args$t2_color %||% "#F5B041"
  t3_color <- common_args$t3_color %||% "#27AE60"
  vline_t1_t2_color <- common_args$vline_t1_t2_color %||% t2_color
  vline_t2_t3_color <- common_args$vline_t2_t3_color %||% t3_color
  background_alpha <- common_args$background_alpha %||% 0.2
  vline_alpha <- common_args$vline_alpha %||% 0.25
  base_font <- common_args$base_font %||% "sans"
  
  # ==== DUAL-AXIS PLOT (Corrected & Complete) ====
  create_dual_axis_quartile_plot <- function(
    combined_data, borders_data_path, y1_var_cols, y2_var_col,
    y1_category_labels = NULL, y2_category_label = NULL,
    y1_axis_label, y2_axis_label, plot_title, legend_title,
    x_axis_title_text = "Population Distribution by Household Income",
    output_filename, output_dir, y1_axis_format = "number", y2_axis_format = "number",
    source_filter = NULL, line1_palette = NULL, line2_color = "#E74C3C",
    loess_span = 0.75, line_width = 0.7, ext_point_size_global = 1.8,
    ext_point_alpha_global = 0.7, intra_tercile_quantile_group = "Groups_4", ...
  ) {
    # --- Input Validation & Parameter Setup ---
    if (!file.exists(borders_data_path)) stop("Borders file not found.")
    required_cols <- c("source_dataset", "income_tercile", "fine_income_group", y1_var_cols, y2_var_col)
    if (!all(required_cols %in% names(combined_data))) {
      stop("combined_data missing columns: ", paste(setdiff(required_cols, names(combined_data)), collapse=", "))
    }
    
    common_args <- list(...)
    border_t1_t2 <- common_args$border_t1_t2 %||% 64400
    border_t2_t3 <- common_args$border_t2_t3 %||% 130000
    t1_color <- common_args$t1_color %||% "#C0392B"
    t2_color <- common_args$t2_color %||% "#F5B041"
    t3_color <- common_args$t3_color %||% "#27AE60"
    vline_t1_t2_color <- common_args$vline_t1_t2_color %||% t2_color
    vline_t2_t3_color <- common_args$vline_t2_t3_color %||% t3_color
    background_alpha <- common_args$background_alpha %||% 0.2
    vline_alpha <- common_args$vline_alpha %||% 0.25
    base_font <- common_args$base_font %||% "sans"
    middle_row_height_npc <- common_args$middle_row_height_npc %||% 0.06
    bottom_row_height_npc <- common_args$bottom_row_height_npc %||% 0.04
    legend_row_height_npc <- common_args$legend_row_height_npc %||% 0.1
    xaxis_title_fontsize <- common_args$xaxis_title_fontsize %||% 11
    tercile_label_fontsize <- common_args$tercile_label_fontsize %||% 10
    
    # --- Data Preparation Logic (Restored from original script) ---
    borders_data <- readr::read_csv(borders_data_path, show_col_types = FALSE)
    tercile_labels_in_borders_df <- borders_data %>% dplyr::filter(QuantileGroup == intra_tercile_quantile_group) %>% dplyr::mutate(tercile_group_label = stringr::str_trim(MainTercile)) %>% dplyr::distinct(tercile_group_label)
    t1_label <- tercile_labels_in_borders_df$tercile_group_label[stringr::str_detect(tercile_labels_in_borders_df$tercile_group_label, regex("Tercile 1|Bottom", ignore_case = TRUE))]
    t2_label <- tercile_labels_in_borders_df$tercile_group_label[stringr::str_detect(tercile_labels_in_borders_df$tercile_group_label, regex("Tercile 2|Middle", ignore_case = TRUE))]
    t3_label <- tercile_labels_in_borders_df$tercile_group_label[stringr::str_detect(tercile_labels_in_borders_df$tercile_group_label, regex("Tercile 3|Top", ignore_case = TRUE))]
    
    data_filtered <- combined_data %>%
      { if (!is.null(source_filter)) dplyr::filter(., source_dataset == !!source_filter) else . } %>%
      dplyr::filter(stringr::str_detect(fine_income_group, "^T[1-3]-G0[1-4]$"))
    
    add_quantile_pos <- function(df) {
      df %>%
        dplyr::mutate(
          tercile_main_group = stringr::str_extract(fine_income_group, "T[1-3]"),
          intra_tercile_sub_group_num = as.numeric(stringr::str_extract(fine_income_group, "(?<=G0)[1-4]$")),
          quantile_position = intra_tercile_sub_group_num + (as.numeric(stringr::str_extract(tercile_main_group, "\\d")) - 1) * 4
        ) %>%
        dplyr::filter(!is.na(quantile_position))
    }
    
    plot_data_y1 <- data_filtered %>%
      tidyr::pivot_longer(cols = dplyr::all_of(y1_var_cols), names_to = "category", values_to = "y_value") %>%
      dplyr::filter(!is.na(y_value)) %>%
      add_quantile_pos() %>%
      dplyr::mutate(category_label = factor(category, levels = y1_var_cols, labels = y1_category_labels %||% y1_var_cols))
    
    plot_data_y2 <- data_filtered %>%
      dplyr::select(fine_income_group, y_value = !!rlang::sym(y2_var_col)) %>%
      dplyr::filter(!is.na(y_value)) %>%
      add_quantile_pos() %>%
      dplyr::mutate(category_label = y2_category_label %||% y2_var_col)
    
    # --- Y-Axis Scaling ---
    y1_range <- if(nrow(plot_data_y1) > 0) range(plot_data_y1$y_value, na.rm = TRUE) else c(0,1)
    y2_range <- if(nrow(plot_data_y2) > 0) range(plot_data_y2$y_value, na.rm = TRUE) else c(0,1)
    y1_min <- y1_range[1]; y1_max <- y1_range[2]
    y2_min <- y2_range[1]; y2_max <- y2_range[2]
    
    if (is.infinite(y1_min) || is.infinite(y1_max)) {y1_min <- 0; y1_max <- 1}
    if (is.infinite(y2_min) || is.infinite(y2_max)) {y2_min <- 0; y2_max <- 1}
    
    y2_to_y1_scale <- function(y2_val) { (y2_val - y2_min) / (y2_max - y2_min) * (y1_max - y1_min) + y1_min }
    y1_to_y2_scale <- function(y1_val) { (y1_val - y1_min) / (y1_max - y1_min) * (y2_max - y2_min) + y2_min }
    
    # --- Color and Label Mapping ---
    y1_labels <- levels(plot_data_y1$category_label)
    y2_label <- unique(plot_data_y2$category_label)
    all_labels <- c(y1_labels, y2_label)
    
    line1_colors <- line1_palette %||% scales::hue_pal()(length(y1_labels))
    color_map_dual <- stats::setNames(c(line1_colors, line2_color), all_labels)
    
    # --- X-Axis Label Interpolation ---
    # (Code for creating x_axis_info_final is assumed to exist in the function environment)
    # This part is complex and often sourced from the single-plot function; for robustness, it's defined here.
    max_income_t3_sg4_row <- borders_data %>% dplyr::filter(QuantileGroup == intra_tercile_quantile_group, MainTercile == t3_label) %>% dplyr::arrange(dplyr::desc(QuantileProbability)) %>% dplyr::slice(1)
    approx_max_income_overall <- if(nrow(max_income_t3_sg4_row) == 1 && is.finite(max_income_t3_sg4_row$CutoffValue)) { max_income_t3_sg4_row$CutoffValue * 1.2 } else { border_t2_t3 * 2 }
    sub_group_borders_prepared <- borders_data %>% dplyr::filter(QuantileGroup == intra_tercile_quantile_group) %>% dplyr::mutate(tercile_group = stringr::str_trim(MainTercile)) %>% dplyr::arrange(tercile_group, QuantileProbability) %>% dplyr::group_by(tercile_group) %>% dplyr::mutate(within_tercile_sub_group_num = dplyr::row_number()) %>% dplyr::ungroup() %>% dplyr::mutate(quantile_position = dplyr::case_when(tercile_group == t1_label ~ within_tercile_sub_group_num, tercile_group == t2_label ~ 4 + within_tercile_sub_group_num, tercile_group == t3_label ~ 8 + within_tercile_sub_group_num, TRUE ~ NA_real_ )) %>% dplyr::select(quantile_position, upper_income_bound = CutoffValue) %>% dplyr::filter(!is.na(quantile_position)) %>% dplyr::bind_rows(tibble::tibble(quantile_position = 0, upper_income_bound = 0), tibble::tibble(quantile_position = 12, upper_income_bound = approx_max_income_overall)) %>% dplyr::distinct(quantile_position, .keep_all = TRUE) %>% dplyr::arrange(quantile_position)
    get_quantile_pos_12_groups <- function(inc_target, border_df_12_groups) { lower_bound_row <- border_df_12_groups %>% dplyr::filter(upper_income_bound < inc_target) %>% dplyr::slice_max(upper_income_bound, n = 1, with_ties = FALSE); upper_bound_row <- border_df_12_groups %>% dplyr::filter(upper_income_bound >= inc_target) %>% dplyr::slice_min(upper_income_bound, n = 1, with_ties = FALSE); if (nrow(lower_bound_row) == 0) return(min(upper_bound_row$quantile_position, 0.5, na.rm=TRUE)); if (nrow(upper_bound_row) == 0) return(max(lower_bound_row$quantile_position, 12.5, na.rm=TRUE)); pos_lower <- lower_bound_row$quantile_position; inc_lower <- lower_bound_row$upper_income_bound; pos_upper <- upper_bound_row$quantile_position; inc_upper <- upper_bound_row$upper_income_bound; if (inc_upper == inc_lower) return(pos_lower); frac <- (inc_target - inc_lower) / (inc_upper - inc_lower); return(max(0.5, min(12.5, pos_lower + frac * (pos_upper - pos_lower)))) }
    critical_income_borders <- sort(unique(c(border_t1_t2, border_t2_t3))); other_desired_ticks <- c(10000, 25000, 75000, 200000, 500000); target_incomes_list <- as.list(critical_income_borders); min_income_separation <- 15000; for (tick in other_desired_ticks) { if (!any(abs(tick - unlist(target_incomes_list)) < min_income_separation)) { target_incomes_list <- c(target_incomes_list, tick) } }; target_incomes <- sort(unique(unlist(target_incomes_list))); target_labels_raw <- scales::dollar(target_incomes, accuracy=1)
    income_label_positions <- purrr::map_dbl(target_incomes, ~ get_quantile_pos_12_groups(., sub_group_borders_prepared))
    x_axis_info <- tibble::tibble( breaks = income_label_positions, labels_raw = target_labels_raw, income = target_incomes ) %>% dplyr::arrange(breaks) %>% dplyr::mutate( is_border = (income %in% c(border_t1_t2, border_t2_t3)), break_diff = breaks - dplyr::lag(breaks, default = -Inf) ) %>% dplyr::filter(break_diff > 0.5 | is_border) %>% dplyr::mutate( breaks = dplyr::case_when( income == border_t1_t2 ~ 4.5, income == border_t2_t3 ~ 8.5, TRUE ~ breaks ), labels = dplyr::case_when( is_border & income == border_t1_t2 ~ glue::glue("<span style='color:{vline_t1_t2_color};'>**{labels_raw}**</span>"), is_border & income == border_t2_t3 ~ glue::glue("<span style='color:{vline_t2_t3_color};'>**{labels_raw}**</span>"), TRUE ~ glue::glue("<span style='color:black;'>{labels_raw}</span>") ) ) %>% dplyr::select(breaks, labels, income, is_border) %>% dplyr::distinct(breaks, .keep_all = TRUE)
    x_axis_info_no_borders <- x_axis_info %>% dplyr::filter(!is_border)
    x_axis_info_final <- x_axis_info %>% dplyr::select(breaks, labels)
    
    # --- Create ggplot call with integrated points (Restored from patch file) ---
    g_dual_plot <- ggplot2::ggplot() +
      ggplot2::annotate("rect", xmin = 0.5, xmax = 4.5, ymin = -Inf, ymax = Inf, fill = t1_color, alpha = background_alpha) +
      ggplot2::annotate("rect", xmin = 4.5, xmax = 8.5, ymin = -Inf, ymax = Inf, fill = t2_color, alpha = background_alpha) +
      ggplot2::annotate("rect", xmin = 8.5, xmax = 12.5, ymin = -Inf, ymax = Inf, fill = t3_color, alpha = background_alpha) +
      ggplot2::geom_vline(data = x_axis_info_no_borders, aes(xintercept = breaks), color = "grey95", linetype = "dotted", linewidth = 0.5) +
      
      # Smoothed Lines
      { if (nrow(plot_data_y1) > 0) ggplot2::geom_smooth(data = plot_data_y1, aes(x = quantile_position, y = y_value, color = category_label, group = category_label), method = "loess", span = loess_span, se = FALSE, linewidth = line_width) else NULL } +
      { if (nrow(plot_data_y2) > 0) ggplot2::geom_smooth(data = plot_data_y2, aes(x = quantile_position, y = y2_to_y1_scale(y_value), color = category_label, group = category_label), method = "loess", span = loess_span, se = FALSE, linewidth = line_width) else NULL } +
      ggplot2::scale_color_manual(values = color_map_dual, name = legend_title, breaks = all_labels) +
      
      # Points Overlay
      ggnewscale::new_scale_color() +
      { if (nrow(plot_data_y1) > 0) ggplot2::geom_point(data = plot_data_y1, aes(x = quantile_position, y = y_value, color = tercile_main_group), size = ext_point_size_global, alpha = ext_point_alpha_global, show.legend = FALSE, inherit.aes = FALSE) else NULL } +
      { if (nrow(plot_data_y2) > 0) ggplot2::geom_point(data = plot_data_y2, aes(x = quantile_position, y = y2_to_y1_scale(y_value), color = tercile_main_group), size = ext_point_size_global, alpha = ext_point_alpha_global, show.legend = FALSE, inherit.aes = FALSE) else NULL } +
      ggplot2::scale_color_manual(values = c("T1" = t1_color, "T2" = t2_color, "T3" = t3_color), guide = "none") +
      
      # Borders, Scales, and Theme
      ggplot2::geom_vline(xintercept = 4.5, linetype = "dashed", color = vline_t1_t2_color, linewidth = 1, alpha = vline_alpha) +
      ggplot2::geom_vline(xintercept = 8.5, linetype = "dashed", color = vline_t2_t3_color, linewidth = 1, alpha = vline_alpha) +
      ggplot2::scale_x_continuous(limits = c(0.5, 12.5), expand = c(0.02, 0.02), breaks = x_axis_info_final$breaks, labels = x_axis_info_final$labels) +
      ggplot2::scale_y_continuous(name = y1_axis_label, limits = c(NA, NA), expand = expansion(mult = c(0.1, 0.1)),
                                  labels = switch(y1_axis_format, "dollar" = scales::label_dollar(accuracy = 1), "percent" = scales::label_percent(accuracy = 1), "comma" = scales::label_comma(accuracy = 1), scales::label_number(accuracy = 0.1)),
                                  sec.axis = ggplot2::sec_axis(trans = ~y1_to_y2_scale(.), name = y2_axis_label,
                                                               labels = switch(y2_axis_format, "dollar" = scales::label_dollar(accuracy = 1), "percent" = scales::label_percent(accuracy = 1), "comma" = scales::label_comma(accuracy = 1), scales::label_number(accuracy = 0.1)))) +
      ggplot2::labs(title = plot_title, x = NULL) +
      ggplot2::theme_minimal(base_family = base_font) +
      ggplot2::theme(
        plot.title = ggplot2::element_text(hjust = 0.5, size = 14, face = "bold"),
        axis.title = ggplot2::element_text(size = 11, face = "bold"), axis.title.x = element_blank(),
        axis.text = ggplot2::element_text(size = 10, color = "black"), axis.text.x = ggtext::element_markdown(size = 9, color = "black"),
        axis.ticks.x = ggplot2::element_blank(), legend.position = "bottom", legend.title = ggplot2::element_text(face = "bold"),
        legend.justification = "center", legend.box.margin = margin(t = 5, b = 5),
        panel.grid.minor = ggplot2::element_blank(), panel.grid.major.x = ggplot2::element_blank(),
        panel.grid.major.y = ggplot2::element_line(color = "grey90", linewidth = 0.4),
        plot.margin = ggplot2::margin(t = 10, r = 10, b = 5, l = 10),
        axis.title.y.right = element_text(size = 11, face = "bold", margin = margin(l = 10)),
        axis.text.y.right = element_text(size = 10, color = "black")
      )
    
    # --- Layout and Saving Logic ---
    plot_legend_dual <- get_legend(g_dual_plot)
    g_no_legend_dual <- g_dual_plot + theme(legend.position = "none")
    label_grob_t1_dual <- grid::textGrob("Bottom Third Country", x = grid::unit(0.20, "npc"), y = grid::unit(0.5, "npc"), hjust = 0.5, vjust = 0.5, gp = grid::gpar(fontsize = tercile_label_fontsize, col = t1_color, fontfamily = base_font, fontface = "bold"))
    label_grob_t2_dual <- grid::textGrob("Middle Third Country", x = grid::unit(0.50, "npc"), y = grid::unit(0.5, "npc"), hjust = 0.5, vjust = 0.5, gp = grid::gpar(fontsize = tercile_label_fontsize, col = t2_color, fontfamily = base_font, fontface = "bold"))
    label_grob_t3_dual <- grid::textGrob("Top Third Country",    x = grid::unit(0.80, "npc"), y = grid::unit(0.5, "npc"), hjust = 0.5, vjust = 0.5, gp = grid::gpar(fontsize = tercile_label_fontsize, col = t3_color, fontfamily = base_font, fontface = "bold"))
    labels_row_grob_dual <- grid::gTree(children = grid::gList(label_grob_t1_dual, label_grob_t2_dual, label_grob_t3_dual), name = "positioned_tercile_labels_dual")
    x_title_style_dual <- grid::gpar(fontsize = xaxis_title_fontsize, fontfamily = base_font, fontface = "bold")
    x_title_grob_dual <- grid::textGrob(x_axis_title_text, gp = x_title_style_dual, x = grid::unit(0.5, "npc"), hjust = 0.5)
    plot_height_npc_dual <- 1 - middle_row_height_npc - bottom_row_height_npc - legend_row_height_npc
    layout_heights_dual <- grid::unit(c(plot_height_npc_dual, middle_row_height_npc, bottom_row_height_npc, legend_row_height_npc), "npc")
    legend_vp_dual <- grid::viewport(x = unit(0.5, "npc"), width = unit(0.9, "npc"), just = "center")
    legend_grob_positioned_dual <- grid::gTree(children = grid::gList(plot_legend_dual), vp = legend_vp_dual)
    final_arranged_plot_dual <- gridExtra::grid.arrange(
      g_no_legend_dual, labels_row_grob_dual, x_title_grob_dual, legend_grob_positioned_dual,
      ncol = 1, heights = layout_heights_dual
    )
    plot_save_path_dual <- file.path(output_dir, output_filename)
    ggplot2::ggsave(filename = plot_save_path_dual, plot = final_arranged_plot_dual, width = 10, height = 7, dpi = 300)
    
    invisible(final_arranged_plot_dual)
  }

# ==== BAR + DOT + LINE PLOT ====
create_bar_dot_line_quartile_plot <- function(
    combined_data,
    borders_data_path,
    y_var_col,
    y_axis_label,
    plot_title,
    x_axis_title_text = "Population Distribution by Household Income",
    output_filename,
    output_dir,
    y_axis_format = "number",
    source_filter = NULL,
    bar_width = 0.9,
    point_size = 2.0,
    line_color_overall = "black",
    line_width_overall = 0.6,
    border_t1_t2 = 64400, border_t2_t3 = 130000,
    t1_color = "#C0392B", t2_color = "#F5B041", t3_color = "#27AE60",
    background_alpha = 0.2,
    bar_fill_alpha = 0.5,
    bar_border_alpha = 1.0,
    vline_t1_t2_color = t2_color, vline_t2_t3_color = t3_color,
    vline_alpha = 0.25,
    base_font = "sans",
    tercile_label_fontsize = 10,
    xaxis_title_fontsize = 11,
    middle_row_height_npc = 0.06,
    bottom_row_height_npc = 0.04,
    intra_tercile_quantile_group = "Groups_4",
    ...
) {
  # ... (Input Validation, Load Borders, Prepare Borders for Interpolation - same as create_multi_line_plot) ...
  common_args <- list(...)
  actual_t1_color <- t1_color %||% common_args$t1_color %||% "#C0392B"
  actual_t2_color <- t2_color %||% common_args$t2_color %||% "#F5B041"
  actual_t3_color <- t3_color %||% common_args$t3_color %||% "#27AE60"
  actual_vline_t1_t2_color <- vline_t1_t2_color %||% common_args$vline_t1_t2_color %||% actual_t2_color 
  actual_vline_t2_t3_color <- vline_t2_t3_color %||% common_args$vline_t2_t3_color %||% actual_t3_color 
  if (!file.exists(borders_data_path)) stop("Borders file not found.")
  required_cols <- c("source_dataset", "income_tercile", "fine_income_group", y_var_col)
  if (!all(required_cols %in% names(combined_data))) {
    stop("combined_data missing required columns for bar-dot-line: ", paste(setdiff(required_cols, names(combined_data)), collapse=", "))
  }
  borders_data <- readr::read_csv(borders_data_path, show_col_types = FALSE)
  tercile_labels_in_borders_df <- borders_data %>% dplyr::filter(QuantileGroup == intra_tercile_quantile_group) %>% dplyr::mutate(tercile_group_label = stringr::str_trim(MainTercile)) %>% dplyr::distinct(tercile_group_label)
  t1_label <- tercile_labels_in_borders_df$tercile_group_label[stringr::str_detect(tercile_labels_in_borders_df$tercile_group_label, regex("Tercile 1|Bottom", ignore_case = TRUE))]
  t2_label <- tercile_labels_in_borders_df$tercile_group_label[stringr::str_detect(tercile_labels_in_borders_df$tercile_group_label, regex("Tercile 2|Middle", ignore_case = TRUE))]
  t3_label <- tercile_labels_in_borders_df$tercile_group_label[stringr::str_detect(tercile_labels_in_borders_df$tercile_group_label, regex("Tercile 3|Top", ignore_case = TRUE))]
  if(length(t1_label)!=1 || length(t2_label)!=1 || length(t3_label)!=1) { stop("Could not uniquely identify tercile labels for bar-dot-line plot.")}
  tercile_color_map_bars <- c(stats::setNames(actual_t1_color, t1_label), stats::setNames(actual_t2_color, t2_label), stats::setNames(actual_t3_color, t3_label))
  
  # MODIFIED to parse "T[1-3]-G0[1-4]"
  plot_data_bars <- combined_data %>%
    dplyr::filter(stringr::str_detect(fine_income_group, "^T[1-3]-G0[1-4]$")) %>% # Expect G01-G04
    { if (!is.null(source_filter)) dplyr::filter(., source_dataset == !!source_filter) else . } %>%
    dplyr::select(tercile_group_raw = income_tercile, fine_group_raw = fine_income_group, y_value = !!rlang::sym(y_var_col)) %>%
    dplyr::mutate(
      tercile_main_group = stringr::str_trim(tercile_group_raw),
      tercile_main_group = factor(dplyr::case_when(
        stringr::str_detect(tercile_main_group, regex("1|Bottom", ignore_case = TRUE)) ~ t1_label,
        stringr::str_detect(tercile_main_group, regex("2|Middle", ignore_case = TRUE)) ~ t2_label,
        stringr::str_detect(tercile_main_group, regex("3|Top", ignore_case = TRUE)) ~ t3_label, TRUE ~ NA_character_ ),
        levels = c(t1_label, t2_label, t3_label))
    ) %>%
    dplyr::mutate(intra_tercile_sub_group_num = as.numeric(stringr::str_extract(fine_group_raw, "(?<=G0)[1-4]$"))) %>% # Extract from G01-G04
    dplyr::mutate(quantile_position = dplyr::case_when(
      tercile_main_group == t1_label ~ intra_tercile_sub_group_num,
      tercile_main_group == t2_label ~ 4 + intra_tercile_sub_group_num,
      tercile_main_group == t3_label ~ 8 + intra_tercile_sub_group_num, TRUE ~ NA_real_ )) %>%
    dplyr::select(tercile_main_group, quantile_position, y_value, fine_income_group = fine_group_raw) %>% # Keep fine_income_group
    dplyr::filter(!is.na(quantile_position) & !is.na(y_value) & !is.na(tercile_main_group)) %>%
    dplyr::arrange(quantile_position)
  
  if(nrow(plot_data_bars) == 0) {
    warning(paste0("No valid data for bar-dot-line plot: ", output_filename)); return(invisible(NULL))
  }
  
  # ... (X-Axis Setup, ggplot object, layout, save - remains the same as v1.05.00.02) ...
  get_quantile_pos_12_groups_fn_bdl <- function(inc_target, border_df_12_groups) { 
    lower_bound_row <- border_df_12_groups %>% dplyr::filter(upper_income_bound < inc_target) %>% dplyr::slice_max(upper_income_bound, n = 1, with_ties=FALSE)
    upper_bound_row <- border_df_12_groups %>% dplyr::filter(upper_income_bound >= inc_target) %>% dplyr::slice_min(upper_income_bound, n = 1, with_ties=FALSE)
    if (nrow(lower_bound_row) == 0 && nrow(upper_bound_row) > 0) return(min(upper_bound_row$quantile_position, 0.5, na.rm=TRUE))
    if (nrow(upper_bound_row) == 0 && nrow(lower_bound_row) > 0) return(max(lower_bound_row$quantile_position, 12.5, na.rm=TRUE))
    if (nrow(lower_bound_row) == 0 && nrow(upper_bound_row) == 0) return(6.5)
    if (upper_bound_row$upper_income_bound == inc_target) return(upper_bound_row$quantile_position)
    if (nrow(lower_bound_row) > 0 && lower_bound_row$upper_income_bound == inc_target) return(lower_bound_row$quantile_position) 
    pos_lower <- lower_bound_row$quantile_position; inc_lower <- lower_bound_row$upper_income_bound
    pos_upper <- upper_bound_row$quantile_position; inc_upper <- upper_bound_row$upper_income_bound
    if (inc_upper == inc_lower) return(pos_lower)
    frac <- (inc_target - inc_lower) / (inc_upper - inc_lower); interpolated_pos <- pos_lower + frac * (pos_upper - pos_lower)
    return(max(0.5, min(12.5, interpolated_pos)))
  }
  max_income_t3_sg4_row_bdl <- borders_data %>% dplyr::filter(QuantileGroup == intra_tercile_quantile_group, MainTercile == t3_label) %>% dplyr::arrange(dplyr::desc(QuantileProbability)) %>% dplyr::slice(1)
  approx_max_income_overall_bdl <- if(nrow(max_income_t3_sg4_row_bdl) == 1 && is.finite(max_income_t3_sg4_row_bdl$CutoffValue)) { max_income_t3_sg4_row_bdl$CutoffValue * 1.2 } else { border_t2_t3 * 2 }
  sub_group_borders_prepared_bdl <- borders_data %>% dplyr::filter(QuantileGroup == intra_tercile_quantile_group) %>% dplyr::mutate(tercile_group = stringr::str_trim(MainTercile)) %>% dplyr::arrange(tercile_group, QuantileProbability) %>% dplyr::group_by(tercile_group) %>% dplyr::mutate(within_tercile_sub_group_num = dplyr::row_number()) %>% dplyr::ungroup() %>% dplyr::mutate(quantile_position = dplyr::case_when(tercile_group == t1_label ~ within_tercile_sub_group_num, tercile_group == t2_label ~ 4 + within_tercile_sub_group_num, tercile_group == t3_label ~ 8 + within_tercile_sub_group_num, TRUE ~ NA_real_ )) %>% dplyr::select(quantile_position, upper_income_bound = CutoffValue) %>% dplyr::filter(!is.na(quantile_position)) %>% dplyr::bind_rows(tibble::tibble(quantile_position = 0, upper_income_bound = 0), tibble::tibble(quantile_position = 12, upper_income_bound = approx_max_income_overall_bdl)) %>% dplyr::distinct(quantile_position, .keep_all = TRUE) %>% dplyr::arrange(quantile_position)
  
  critical_income_borders <- sort(unique(c(border_t1_t2, border_t2_t3)))
  other_desired_ticks <- c(10000, 25000, 75000, 200000, 500000)
  target_incomes_bdl_list <- as.list(critical_income_borders)
  min_income_separation <- 15000 
  
  for (tick in other_desired_ticks) {
    is_too_close_to_existing <- FALSE
    if (length(target_incomes_bdl_list) > 0) {
      for (existing_tick in target_incomes_bdl_list) { 
        if (abs(tick - existing_tick) < min_income_separation) {
          is_too_close_to_existing <- TRUE
          break
        }
      }
    }
    if (!is_too_close_to_existing) {
      target_incomes_bdl_list <- c(target_incomes_bdl_list, tick)
    }
  }
  target_incomes_bdl <- sort(unique(unlist(target_incomes_bdl_list)))
  target_labels_raw_bdl <- scales::dollar(target_incomes_bdl, accuracy=1)
  income_label_positions_bdl <- purrr::map_dbl(target_incomes_bdl, ~ get_quantile_pos_12_groups_fn_bdl(., sub_group_borders_prepared_bdl))
  x_axis_info_bdl <- tibble::tibble( breaks = income_label_positions_bdl, labels_raw = target_labels_raw_bdl, income = target_incomes_bdl) %>% 
    dplyr::arrange(breaks) %>% 
    dplyr::mutate(
      is_border = (income %in% c(border_t1_t2, border_t2_t3)),
      break_diff = breaks - dplyr::lag(breaks, default = -Inf)
    ) %>% 
    dplyr::filter(break_diff > 0.5 | is_border) %>% 
    dplyr::mutate(
      breaks = dplyr::case_when(
        income == border_t1_t2 ~ 4.5,
        income == border_t2_t3 ~ 8.5,
        TRUE ~ breaks
      ),
      labels = dplyr::case_when(
        is_border & income == border_t1_t2 ~ glue::glue("<span style='color:{actual_vline_t1_t2_color};'>**{labels_raw}**</span>"),
        is_border & income == border_t2_t3 ~ glue::glue("<span style='color:{actual_vline_t2_t3_color};'>**{labels_raw}**</span>"),
        TRUE ~ glue::glue("<span style='color:black;'>{labels_raw}</span>")
      )
    ) %>% 
    dplyr::select(breaks, labels, income, is_border) %>% 
    dplyr::distinct(breaks, .keep_all = TRUE)
  x_axis_info_no_borders_bdl <- x_axis_info_bdl %>% dplyr::filter(!is_border)
  x_axis_info_final_bdl <- x_axis_info_bdl %>% dplyr::select(breaks, labels)
  
  g_bar_dot_line <- ggplot2::ggplot(plot_data_bars, ggplot2::aes(x = quantile_position, y = y_value)) +
    ggplot2::annotate("rect", xmin = 0.5, xmax = 4.5, ymin = -Inf, ymax = Inf, fill = actual_t1_color, alpha = background_alpha) +
    ggplot2::annotate("rect", xmin = 4.5, xmax = 8.5, ymin = -Inf, ymax = Inf, fill = actual_t2_color, alpha = background_alpha) +
    ggplot2::annotate("rect", xmin = 8.5, xmax = 12.5, ymin = -Inf, ymax = Inf, fill = actual_t3_color, alpha = background_alpha) +
    ggplot2::geom_vline(data = x_axis_info_no_borders_bdl, aes(xintercept = breaks), color = "grey95", linetype = "dotted", linewidth = 0.5, inherit.aes = FALSE) +
    ggplot2::geom_col(aes(fill = tercile_main_group, color = tercile_main_group), width = bar_width, alpha = bar_fill_alpha, position = position_dodge(width=0)) +
    ggplot2::geom_line(aes(group = 1), color = line_color_overall, linewidth = line_width_overall) +
    ggplot2::geom_point(aes(color = tercile_main_group), size = point_size, alpha = bar_border_alpha) +
    ggplot2::scale_fill_manual(values = tercile_color_map_bars, guide = "none") +
    ggplot2::scale_color_manual(values = tercile_color_map_bars, guide = "none") +
    ggplot2::geom_vline(xintercept = 4.5, linetype = "dashed", color = actual_vline_t1_t2_color, linewidth = 1, alpha = vline_alpha) +
    ggplot2::geom_vline(xintercept = 8.5, linetype = "dashed", color = actual_vline_t2_t3_color, linewidth = 1, alpha = vline_alpha) +
    ggplot2::labs(title = plot_title, x = NULL, y = y_axis_label) +
    ggplot2::scale_x_continuous(limits = c(0.5, 12.5), expand = c(0.02, 0.02), breaks = x_axis_info_final_bdl$breaks, labels = x_axis_info_final_bdl$labels) +
    { if (y_axis_format == "dollar") ggplot2::scale_y_continuous(labels = scales::label_dollar(accuracy = 1), expand = expansion(mult = c(0.05, 0.05)))
      else if (y_axis_format == "percent") ggplot2::scale_y_continuous(labels = scales::label_percent(accuracy = 1), expand = expansion(mult = c(0.05, 0.05)))
      else if (y_axis_format == "comma") ggplot2::scale_y_continuous(labels = scales::label_comma(accuracy = 1), expand = expansion(mult = c(0.05, 0.05)))
      else ggplot2::scale_y_continuous(labels = scales::label_number(accuracy = 0.1), expand = expansion(mult = c(0.05, 0.05))) } +
    ggplot2::theme_minimal(base_family = base_font) +
    ggplot2::theme(
      plot.title = ggplot2::element_text(hjust = 0.5, size = 14, face = "bold"),
      axis.title = ggplot2::element_text(size = 11, face = "bold"), axis.title.x = element_blank(),
      axis.text = ggplot2::element_text(size = 10, color = "black"), axis.text.x = ggtext::element_markdown(size = 9, color = "black"),
      axis.ticks.x = ggplot2::element_blank(), legend.position = "none",
      panel.grid.minor = ggplot2::element_blank(), panel.grid.major.x = ggplot2::element_blank(),
      panel.grid.major.y = ggplot2::element_line(color = "grey90", linewidth = 0.4),
      plot.margin = ggplot2::margin(t = 10, r = 10, b = 5, l = 10)
    )
  label_grob_t1_bdl <- grid::textGrob("Bottom Third Country", x = grid::unit(0.20, "npc"), y = grid::unit(0.5, "npc"), hjust = 0.5, vjust = 0.5, gp = grid::gpar(fontsize = tercile_label_fontsize, col = actual_t1_color, fontfamily = base_font, fontface = "bold"))
  label_grob_t2_bdl <- grid::textGrob("Middle Third Country", x = grid::unit(0.50, "npc"), y = grid::unit(0.5, "npc"), hjust = 0.5, vjust = 0.5, gp = grid::gpar(fontsize = tercile_label_fontsize, col = actual_t2_color, fontfamily = base_font, fontface = "bold"))
  label_grob_t3_bdl <- grid::textGrob("Top Third Country",    x = grid::unit(0.80, "npc"), y = grid::unit(0.5, "npc"), hjust = 0.5, vjust = 0.5, gp = grid::gpar(fontsize = tercile_label_fontsize, col = actual_t3_color, fontfamily = base_font, fontface = "bold"))
  labels_row_grob_bdl <- grid::gTree(children = grid::gList(label_grob_t1_bdl, label_grob_t2_bdl, label_grob_t3_bdl), name = "positioned_tercile_labels_bdl")
  x_title_style_bdl <- grid::gpar(fontsize = xaxis_title_fontsize, fontfamily = base_font, fontface = "bold")
  x_title_grob_bdl <- grid::textGrob(x_axis_title_text, gp = x_title_style_bdl, x = grid::unit(0.5, "npc"), hjust = 0.5)
  plot_height_npc_bdl <- 1 - middle_row_height_npc - bottom_row_height_npc
  if(plot_height_npc_bdl < 0) { plot_height_npc_bdl <- 0.8; middle_row_height_npc <- 0.05; bottom_row_height_npc <- 0.05; }
  layout_heights_bdl <- grid::unit(c(plot_height_npc_bdl, middle_row_height_npc, bottom_row_height_npc), "npc")
  final_arranged_plot_bdl <- gridExtra::grid.arrange(
    g_bar_dot_line, labels_row_grob_bdl, x_title_grob_bdl,
    ncol = 1, heights = layout_heights_bdl
  )
  plot_save_path_bdl <- file.path(output_dir, output_filename)
  print(paste("Saving bar-dot-line 3-Tercile (4 G-groups each) plot to:", plot_save_path_bdl)) # Updated comment
  ggplot2::ggsave(filename = plot_save_path_bdl, plot = final_arranged_plot_bdl, width = 10, height = 7, dpi = 300)
  print("... Bar-dot-line plot saved successfully.")
  invisible(g_bar_dot_line)
}

# ==== END OF FUNCTION DEFINITIONS ====
print("V2_Visualization_Functions_Quartiles.R loaded (Version 1.07.00.00 - Corrected dual-axis plot).")

# ==== ABOUT ====
# Project: 1/3 Country Project Visualizations
# Script:  VI_A_Commented_Out_Visualizations.R
# Version: 1.05.07.00 # Matches original script version where these were commented
# Author:  Maxwell Goshert / Gemini
# Date:    2025-05-04 (MST)

# Purpose: Contains the R code for visualizations that were previously
#          commented out in the main generation script (VI_Generate_All_Visualizations.R).
#          This script is for reference or potential future use and requires
#          the setup (libraries, data loading, functions) from the main script
#          to be run beforehand if these plots are to be generated.

# HELPER FUNCTIONS (for single and multi-line plots)
apply_line_plot_enhancements <- function(plot_obj, y_var_char, source_filter_char, plot_title_new) {
  if (is.null(plot_obj) || !inherits(plot_obj, "ggplot")) return(plot_obj)
  
  data_to_filter_points <- combined_summary_stats_data
  if (!is.null(source_filter_char)) {
    data_to_filter_points <- dplyr::filter(data_to_filter_points, source_dataset == !!source_filter_char)
  }
  
  plot_data <- data_to_filter_points %>%
    filter(stringr::str_detect(fine_income_group, paste0("^T[1-3]-G0[1-", substr(intra_tercile_quantile_group_global, nchar(intra_tercile_quantile_group_global), nchar(intra_tercile_quantile_group_global)), "]$"))) %>%
    arrange(fine_income_group) %>%
    mutate(
      x_value_for_points = 1:n(),
      y_value_for_points = .data[[y_var_char]],
      point_tercile_group = case_when(
        stringr::str_starts(fine_income_group, "T1") ~ "T1",
        stringr::str_starts(fine_income_group, "T2") ~ "T2",
        stringr::str_starts(fine_income_group, "T3") ~ "T3",
        TRUE ~ NA_character_
      )
    ) %>%
    filter(!is.na(point_tercile_group) & !is.na(y_value_for_points))
  
  plot_with_points <- plot_obj +
    geom_point(data = plot_data,
               aes(x = x_value_for_points, y = y_value_for_points, color = point_tercile_group),
               size = ext_point_size_global, alpha = ext_point_alpha_global, na.rm = TRUE, show.legend = FALSE) +
    scale_color_manual(values = c("T1" = t1_color_global, "T2" = t2_color_global, "T3" = t3_color_global), na.translate = FALSE)
  
  data_for_ylim <- data_to_filter_points %>%
    filter(!is.na(.data[[y_var_char]]))
  
  min_val <- min(data_for_ylim[[y_var_char]], na.rm = TRUE)
  max_val <- max(data_for_ylim[[y_var_char]], na.rm = TRUE)
  
  padding_amount <- (max_val - min_val) * y_axis_padding_factor
  
  plot_final <- plot_with_points +
    coord_cartesian(ylim = c(min_val - padding_amount, max_val + padding_amount)) +
    ggtitle(plot_title_new)
  
  return(plot_final)
}

add_tercile_colored_points_overlay <- function(plot_obj, y_var_cols_for_points, source_filter_char, ...) {
  if (is.null(plot_obj) || !inherits(plot_obj, "ggplot")) return(plot_obj)
  
  base_data_for_points <- combined_summary_stats_data
  if (!is.null(source_filter_char)) {
    base_data_for_points <- dplyr::filter(base_data_for_points, source_dataset == !!source_filter_char)
  }
  
  y_var_cols_for_points <- y_var_cols_for_points[y_var_cols_for_points %in% names(base_data_for_points)]
  
  if(length(y_var_cols_for_points) > 0) {
    points_data_overlay <- base_data_for_points %>%
      filter(stringr::str_detect(fine_income_group, paste0("^T[1-3]-G0[1-", substr(intra_tercile_quantile_group_global, nchar(intra_tercile_quantile_group_global), nchar(intra_tercile_quantile_group_global)), "]$"))) %>%
      select(fine_income_group, income_tercile, all_of(y_var_cols_for_points)) %>%
      tidyr::pivot_longer(cols = all_of(y_var_cols_for_points), names_to = "category_for_points_overlay", values_to = "y_value_for_points_overlay") %>%
      filter(!is.na(y_value_for_points_overlay)) %>%
      mutate(
        tercile_main_group_for_points = str_extract(fine_income_group, "T[1-3]"),
        intra_tercile_sub_group_num_for_points = as.numeric(stringr::str_extract(fine_income_group, "(?<=G0)[1-4]$")),
        quantile_position_for_points = intra_tercile_sub_group_num_for_points + (as.numeric(str_extract(tercile_main_group_for_points, "\\d")) - 1) * 4
      ) %>%
      filter(!is.na(quantile_position_for_points) & !is.na(tercile_main_group_for_points))
    
    plot_with_overlay_points <- plot_obj +
      ggnewscale::new_scale_color() +
      geom_point(data = points_data_overlay,
                 aes(x = quantile_position_for_points, y = y_value_for_points_overlay, color = tercile_main_group_for_points),
                 size = ext_point_size_global, alpha = ext_point_alpha_global, na.rm = TRUE, show.legend = FALSE, inherit.aes = FALSE) +
      scale_color_manual(name = "Tercile (Points)", values = c("T1" = t1_color_global, "T2" = t2_color_global, "T3" = t3_color_global), guide = "none")
    
    return(plot_with_overlay_points)
  }
  return(plot_obj)
}
